	public class AdbInstanceDataReader : AdbDataReader
	{
		private Dictionary<Guid, List<AdbBaseRecord>>.Enumerator classEnumerator;

		private List<AdbBaseRecord> currentRecordList;

		private AdbBaseRecord currentRecord;

		private int currentRecordIndex;

		private Guid currentClassGuid;

		public AdbInstanceDataReader(Dictionary<Guid, List<AdbBaseRecord>> adbRecordsForTypeMap, AdbDataSet.SimCaseInfo caseInfo, Dictionary<Guid, int> recordGuidToId, int numExistingRecords)
			: base(caseInfo)
		{
			base.caseInfo = caseInfo;
			base.recordGuidToId = recordGuidToId;
			base.numExistingRecords = numExistingRecords;
			count = CountAdbRecords(adbRecordsForTypeMap);
			fieldNames = new string[4];
			numFields = 4;
			fieldNames[0] = "Id";
			fieldNameIndex["Id"] = 0;
			fieldNames[1] = "Guid";
			fieldNameIndex["Guid"] = 1;
			fieldNames[2] = "CaseId";
			fieldNameIndex["CaseId"] = 2;
			fieldNames[3] = "LastChangeNumber";
			fieldNameIndex["LastChangeNumber"] = 3;
			classEnumerator = adbRecordsForTypeMap.GetEnumerator();
		}

		protected int CountAdbRecords(Dictionary<Guid, List<AdbBaseRecord>> adbRecordsForTypeMap)
		{
			int num = 0;
			int num2 = numExistingRecords;
			foreach (List<AdbBaseRecord> value in adbRecordsForTypeMap.Values)
			{
				foreach (AdbBaseRecord item in value)
				{
					if (!recordGuidToId.ContainsKey(item.MyGuid))
					{
						recordGuidToId[item.MyGuid] = num2++;
					}
					num++;
				}
			}
			return num;
		}

		public override bool Read()
		{
			if (currentRecordList == null || currentRecordIndex == currentRecordList.Count)
			{
				if (classEnumerator.MoveNext())
				{
					currentClassGuid = classEnumerator.Current.Key;
					currentRecordList = classEnumerator.Current.Value;
					currentRecord = currentRecordList[0];
					currentRecordIndex = 1;
					return true;
				}
				return false;
			}
			currentRecord = currentRecordList[currentRecordIndex++];
			return true;
		}

		public override object GetValue(int i)
		{
			object result = null;
			switch (i)
			{
			case 0:
				result = recordGuidToId[currentRecord.MyGuid];
				break;
			case 1:
				result = currentRecord.MyGuid.ToString();
				break;
			case 2:
				result = currentClassGuid.ToString();
				break;
			case 3:
				result = caseInfo.caseId;
				break;
			case 4:
				result = currentRecord.LastChangeNumber;
				break;
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
	public class AdbFieldValueReader : AdbDataReader
	{
		private Dictionary<Guid, List<AdbBaseRecord>>.Enumerator classEnumerator;

		private List<AdbBaseRecord> currentRecordList;

		private AdbBaseRecord currentRecord;

		private int currentRecordIndex;

		private Guid currentClassGuid;

		private Dictionary<Guid, AdbFieldAttribute>.Enumerator fieldEnumerator;

		private KeyValuePair<Guid, AdbFieldAttribute> guidAndFieldAttr;

		private FieldInfo currentFieldInfo;

		private object currentValue;

		public AdbFieldValueReader(AdbTop adbTop, Dictionary<Guid, List<AdbBaseRecord>> adbRecordsForTypeMap, Dictionary<Guid, AdbTop.typeTable> classTypes, AdbDataSet.SimCaseInfo caseInfo, Dictionary<Guid, int> recordGuidToId)
			: base(caseInfo)
		{
			base.caseInfo = caseInfo;
			base.recordGuidToId = recordGuidToId;
			count = CountScalarFields(adbRecordsForTypeMap, classTypes);
			fieldNames = new string[6];
			numFields = 6;
			fieldNames[0] = "RecordId";
			fieldNameIndex["RecordId"] = 0;
			fieldNames[1] = "CaseId";
			fieldNameIndex["CaseId"] = 1;
			fieldNames[2] = "FieldGuid";
			fieldNameIndex["FieldGuid"] = 2;
			fieldNames[3] = "IntegerValue";
			fieldNameIndex["IntegerValue"] = 3;
			fieldNames[4] = "DoubleValue";
			fieldNameIndex["DoubleValue"] = 4;
			fieldNames[5] = "StringValue";
			fieldNameIndex["StringValue"] = 5;
			classEnumerator = adbRecordsForTypeMap.GetEnumerator();
		}

		protected int CountScalarFields(Dictionary<Guid, List<AdbBaseRecord>> adbRecordsForTypeMap, Dictionary<Guid, AdbTop.typeTable> classTypes)
		{
			int num = 0;
			foreach (List<AdbBaseRecord> value2 in adbRecordsForTypeMap.Values)
			{
				foreach (AdbBaseRecord item in value2)
				{
					foreach (AdbFieldAttribute field in classTypes[item.ClassGuid].classInfo.fieldList)
					{
						FieldInfo f = field.F;
						object value = f.GetValue(item);
						if ((!f.FieldType.IsGenericType || !(f.FieldType.GetGenericTypeDefinition() != typeof(Nullable<>))) && value != null && (!value.GetType().Name.Equals("Int32") || (int)value != 0) && (!value.GetType().Name.Equals("Double") || 0.0 != (double)value))
						{
							num++;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			return num;
		}

		public override bool Read()
		{
			bool result = false;
			if (currentRecordList == null && classEnumerator.MoveNext())
			{
				currentClassGuid = classEnumerator.Current.Key;
				currentRecordList = classEnumerator.Current.Value;
				currentRecord = currentRecordList[0];
				currentRecordIndex = 1;
				fieldEnumerator = currentRecord.ClassInfo.fields.GetEnumerator();
			}
			bool flag = false;
			while (!flag)
			{
				if (fieldEnumerator.MoveNext())
				{
					guidAndFieldAttr = fieldEnumerator.Current;
					currentFieldInfo = guidAndFieldAttr.Value.F;
					if (!currentFieldInfo.FieldType.IsGenericType || !(currentFieldInfo.FieldType.GetGenericTypeDefinition() != typeof(Nullable<>)))
					{
						currentValue = currentFieldInfo.GetValue(currentRecord);
						if (currentValue != null && (!currentValue.GetType().Name.Equals("Int32") || (int)currentValue != 0) && (!currentValue.GetType().Name.Equals("Double") || 0.0 != (double)currentValue))
						{
							flag = true;
							result = true;
						}
					}
				}
				else if (currentRecordIndex != currentRecordList.Count)
				{
					currentRecord = currentRecordList[currentRecordIndex++];
					fieldEnumerator = currentRecord.ClassInfo.fields.GetEnumerator();
				}
				else
				{
					if (!classEnumerator.MoveNext())
					{
						break;
					}
					currentClassGuid = classEnumerator.Current.Key;
					currentRecordList = classEnumerator.Current.Value;
					currentRecord = currentRecordList[0];
					currentRecordIndex = 1;
					fieldEnumerator = currentRecord.ClassInfo.fields.GetEnumerator();
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public override object GetValue(int i)
		{
			object result = null;
			switch (i)
			{
			case 0:
				result = recordGuidToId[currentRecord.MyGuid];
				break;
			case 1:
				result = caseInfo.caseId;
				break;
			case 2:
				result = guidAndFieldAttr.Key.ToString();
				break;
			default:
				if (currentValue != null)
				{
					if (i == 3 && currentValue.GetType().Name.Equals("Boolean"))
					{
						result = ((currentValue != null && (bool)currentValue) ? 1 : 0);
					}
					else if (i == 3 && currentValue.GetType().Name.Equals("Int32"))
					{
						result = currentValue;
					}
					else if (i == 3 && currentFieldInfo.FieldType.IsSubclassOf(typeof(AdbBaseRecord)))
					{
						AdbBaseRecord adbBaseRecord = (AdbBaseRecord)currentValue;
						result = recordGuidToId[adbBaseRecord.MyGuid];
					}
					else if (i == 4 && currentValue.GetType().Name.Equals("Double"))
					{
						result = currentValue;
					}
					else if (i == 5 && currentValue.GetType().Name.Equals("String"))
					{
						result = currentValue;
					}
				}
				break;
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
	public class AdbArrayValueReader : AdbDataReader
	{
		private Dictionary<Guid, List<AdbBaseRecord>>.Enumerator classEnumerator;

		private List<AdbBaseRecord> currentRecordList;

		private AdbBaseRecord currentRecord;

		private int currentRecordIndex;

		private Guid currentClassGuid;

		private Dictionary<Guid, AdbFieldAttribute>.Enumerator fieldEnumerator;

		private KeyValuePair<Guid, AdbFieldAttribute> guidAndFieldAttr;

		private FieldInfo currentFieldInfo;

		private object currentArray;

		private int currentArrayLength;

		private int currentArrayIndex = -1;

		private Type[] genericArgs;

		private bool hasIdColumn;

		private MethodInfo idAtLocationMethod;

		private MethodInfo valueAtLocationMethod;

		public AdbArrayValueReader(AdbTop adbTop, Dictionary<Guid, List<AdbBaseRecord>> adbRecordsForTypeMap, Dictionary<Guid, AdbTop.typeTable> classTypes, AdbDataSet.SimCaseInfo caseInfo, Dictionary<Guid, int> recordGuidToId)
			: base(caseInfo)
		{
			base.caseInfo = caseInfo;
			base.recordGuidToId = recordGuidToId;
			count = CountArrayValues(adbRecordsForTypeMap, classTypes);
			fieldNames = new string[9];
			numFields = 9;
			fieldNames[0] = "RecordId";
			fieldNameIndex["RecordId"] = 0;
			fieldNames[1] = "CaseId";
			fieldNameIndex["CaseId"] = 1;
			fieldNames[2] = "FieldGuid";
			fieldNameIndex["FieldGuid"] = 2;
			fieldNames[3] = "Idx";
			fieldNameIndex["Idx"] = 3;
			fieldNames[4] = "IntegerId";
			fieldNameIndex["IntegerId"] = 4;
			fieldNames[5] = "StringId";
			fieldNameIndex["StringId"] = 5;
			fieldNames[6] = "IntegerValue";
			fieldNameIndex["IntegerValue"] = 6;
			fieldNames[7] = "DoubleValue";
			fieldNameIndex["DoubleValue"] = 7;
			fieldNames[8] = "StringValue";
			fieldNameIndex["StringValue"] = 8;
			classEnumerator = adbRecordsForTypeMap.GetEnumerator();
		}

		protected int CountArrayValues(Dictionary<Guid, List<AdbBaseRecord>> adbRecordsForTypeMap, Dictionary<Guid, AdbTop.typeTable> classTypes)
		{
			int num = 0;
			foreach (List<AdbBaseRecord> value2 in adbRecordsForTypeMap.Values)
			{
				foreach (AdbBaseRecord item in value2)
				{
					foreach (AdbFieldAttribute field in classTypes[item.ClassGuid].classInfo.fieldList)
					{
						FieldInfo f = field.F;
						if (f.FieldType.IsGenericType && f.FieldType.GetGenericTypeDefinition() != typeof(Nullable<>))
						{
							object value = f.GetValue(item);
							if (value != null)
							{
								int num2 = (int)value.GetType().GetProperty("Length").GetValue(value);
								num += num2;
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			return num;
		}

		public override bool Read()
		{
			bool result = false;
			if (currentRecordList == null && classEnumerator.MoveNext())
			{
				currentClassGuid = classEnumerator.Current.Key;
				currentRecordList = classEnumerator.Current.Value;
				currentRecord = currentRecordList[0];
				currentRecordIndex = 1;
				fieldEnumerator = currentRecord.ClassInfo.fields.GetEnumerator();
			}
			if (currentArray != null)
			{
				currentArrayIndex++;
				if (currentArrayIndex == currentArrayLength)
				{
					currentArray = null;
				}
			}
			bool flag = currentArray != null;
			if (flag)
			{
				result = true;
			}
			while (!flag)
			{
				if (fieldEnumerator.MoveNext())
				{
					guidAndFieldAttr = fieldEnumerator.Current;
					currentFieldInfo = guidAndFieldAttr.Value.F;
					if (currentClassGuid.ToString().ToUpper().Equals("6DA8E8B3-14D5-4832-8C8E-ABA7FF7228D2") && currentFieldInfo.Name.Equals("Inlets"))
					{
						currentClassGuid.ToString();
						if (currentFieldInfo.FieldType.IsGenericType && currentFieldInfo.FieldType.GetGenericTypeDefinition() != typeof(Nullable<>))
						{
							currentArray = currentFieldInfo.GetValue(currentRecord);
							if (currentArray == null)
							{
								continue;
							}
							PropertyInfo property = currentArray.GetType().GetProperty("Length");
							currentArrayLength = (int)property.GetValue(currentArray);
							_ = currentRecord;
						}
					}
					if (!currentFieldInfo.FieldType.IsGenericType || !(currentFieldInfo.FieldType.GetGenericTypeDefinition() != typeof(Nullable<>)))
					{
						continue;
					}
					currentArray = currentFieldInfo.GetValue(currentRecord);
					if (currentArray != null)
					{
						Type type = currentArray.GetType();
						PropertyInfo property2 = type.GetProperty("Length");
						currentArrayLength = (int)property2.GetValue(currentArray);
						if (currentArrayLength != 0)
						{
							genericArgs = guidAndFieldAttr.Value.F.FieldType.GetGenericArguments();
							hasIdColumn = !genericArgs[0].Name.Equals("AdbNoId");
							valueAtLocationMethod = type.GetMethod("ValueAtLocation");
							idAtLocationMethod = type.GetMethod("IDAtLocation");
							currentArrayIndex = 0;
							flag = true;
							result = true;
						}
					}
				}
				else if (currentRecordIndex != currentRecordList.Count)
				{
					currentRecord = currentRecordList[currentRecordIndex++];
					fieldEnumerator = currentRecord.ClassInfo.fields.GetEnumerator();
				}
				else
				{
					if (!classEnumerator.MoveNext())
					{
						break;
					}
					currentClassGuid = classEnumerator.Current.Key;
					currentRecordList = classEnumerator.Current.Value;
					currentRecord = currentRecordList[0];
					currentRecordIndex = 1;
					fieldEnumerator = currentRecord.ClassInfo.fields.GetEnumerator();
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public override object GetValue(int i)
		{
			object result = null;
			switch (i)
			{
			case 0:
				result = recordGuidToId[currentRecord.MyGuid];
				break;
			case 1:
				result = caseInfo.caseId;
				break;
			case 2:
				result = guidAndFieldAttr.Key.ToString();
				break;
			default:
				if (currentArray == null)
				{
					break;
				}
				switch (i)
				{
				case 3:
					result = currentArrayIndex;
					break;
				case 4:
				case 5:
				{
					if (!hasIdColumn)
					{
						break;
					}
					object obj2 = idAtLocationMethod.Invoke(currentArray, new object[1] { currentArrayIndex });
					if (obj2 != null)
					{
						if (i == 4 && genericArgs[0].Name.Equals("Int32"))
						{
							result = obj2;
						}
						else if (i == 4 && genericArgs[0].IsSubclassOf(typeof(AdbBaseRecord)))
						{
							AdbBaseRecord adbBaseRecord2 = (AdbBaseRecord)obj2;
							result = recordGuidToId[adbBaseRecord2.MyGuid];
						}
						else if (i == 5 && genericArgs[0].Name.Equals("String"))
						{
							result = obj2;
						}
					}
					break;
				}
				default:
				{
					object obj = valueAtLocationMethod.Invoke(currentArray, new object[1] { currentArrayIndex });
					if (obj != null)
					{
						if (i == 6 && genericArgs[1].Name.Equals("Boolean"))
						{
							result = ((obj != null && (bool)obj) ? 1 : 0);
						}
						else if (i == 6 && genericArgs[1].Name.Equals("Int32"))
						{
							result = obj;
						}
						else if (i == 6 && genericArgs[1].IsSubclassOf(typeof(AdbBaseRecord)))
						{
							AdbBaseRecord adbBaseRecord = (AdbBaseRecord)obj;
							result = recordGuidToId[adbBaseRecord.MyGuid];
						}
						else if (i == 7 && genericArgs[1].Name.Equals("Double"))
						{
							result = obj;
						}
						else if (i == 8 && genericArgs[1].Name.Equals("String"))
						{
							result = obj;
						}
					}
					break;
				}
				}
				break;
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
	public class DeletedRecordDataReader : AdbDataReader
	{
		private Guid currentGuid;

		private HashSet<Guid>.Enumerator deletedRecordGuidEnumerator;

		public DeletedRecordDataReader(AdbDataSet.SimCaseInfo caseInfo, Dictionary<Guid, int> recordGuidToId, HashSet<Guid> deletedRecordGuids)
			: base(caseInfo)
		{
			base.caseInfo = caseInfo;
			base.recordGuidToId = recordGuidToId;
			deletedRecordGuidEnumerator = deletedRecordGuids.GetEnumerator();
			count = deletedRecordGuids.Count;
			fieldNames = new string[1];
			numFields = 1;
			fieldNames[0] = "Id";
			fieldNameIndex["Id"] = 0;
		}

		public override bool Read()
		{
			if (deletedRecordGuidEnumerator.MoveNext())
			{
				currentGuid = deletedRecordGuidEnumerator.Current;
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public override object GetValue(int i)
		{
			object result = null;
			if (i == 0)
			{
				result = recordGuidToId[currentGuid];
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
	[ComVisible(true)]
	[Guid("F46A5C18-C272-4101-BCF1-BAB8213197C8")]
	public class AdbDataMover : AdbBase
	{
		public ConvertersBetweenObjects ConverterSet { get; set; }

		public AdbBaseRecord TargetRecord { get; set; }

		public override bool ShouldBeInRecordMap => false;

		public AdbDataMover(AdbTop top, Guid? existingGuid)
			: base(top, existingGuid)
		{
		}

		public AdbDataMover(AdbTop top)
			: base(top)
		{
		}

		public AdbDataMover()
		{
		}

		[AdbMethodIdent("5DEC71D4-3292-4BE7-8220-35B69BC5648F")]
		public string GetVariables()
		{
			return ConverterSet.GetVariableDescriptionNonAds();
		}

		[AdbMethodIdent("A7CA5EFA-3E62-4281-85EA-81908AC4FB51")]
		public string GetData()
		{
			XDocument adsXmlVariableDesc = ConverterSet.GetAdsXmlVariableDesc();
			adsXmlVariableDesc.ToString();
			IArgumentData args = TargetRecord.GetArgs(adsXmlVariableDesc.Element("Args"));
			return ConverterSet.ConvertFromAds(args).ToString();
		}

		[AdbMethodIdent("CA7BA191-C651-4678-A592-F5BF486064FE")]
		public string GetDataWithNulls()
		{
			XDocument adsXmlVariableDesc = ConverterSet.GetAdsXmlVariableDesc();
			adsXmlVariableDesc.ToString();
			IArgumentData args = TargetRecord.GetArgs(adsXmlVariableDesc.Element("Args"), includeNullData: true);
			return ConverterSet.ConvertFromAds(args).ToString();
		}

		[AdbMethodIdent("FB996002-9852-45E8-BBC1-BCDA7D8D77A6")]
		public string PutData(object dataTree)

			ConverterSet.ConvertToAdsRaw(dataTree, (IAdsDataStorer)(object)TargetRecord);
			return null;
		}

		[AdbMethodIdent("26449202-2903-47DF-A840-BD69611A1B74")]

	public class AdbIOReal : IAdbBaseObject, IAdsDataStorer
	{
		private static string[] _quals = new string[2] { "Calculated", "Input" };

		private double?[] _vals = new double?[_quals.Length];

		public object Value(object[] id, int currentIndex)
		{
			object result = null;
			if (currentIndex == id.Length)
			{
				double?[] vals = _vals;
				for (int i = 0; i < vals.Length; i++)
				{
					double? num = vals[i];
					if (num.HasValue)
					{
						result = num;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			else if (id[currentIndex] is string value)
			{
				for (int j = 0; j < _quals.Length; j++)
				{
					if (_quals[j].Equals(value, StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase))
					{
						result = _vals[j];
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public void RestoreValue(object[] id, int currentIndex, object newValue)
		{
			SetValue(id, currentIndex, newValue);
		}

		public void SetValue(object[] id, int currentIndex, object newValue)
		{
			if (currentIndex == id.Length)
			{
				if (newValue == null)
				{
					Array.Clear(_vals, 0, _vals.Length);
				}
				else
				{
					_vals[0] = (double)newValue;
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (!(id[currentIndex] is string value))
				{
					return;
				}
				for (int i = 0; i < _quals.Length; i++)
				{
					if (_quals[i].Equals(value, StringComparison.CurrentCultureIgnoreCase))
					{
						_vals[i] = (double)newValue;
						break;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public object ExecuteMethod(object[] id, int currentIndex, object[] arguments)
		{
			object result = null;
			if (currentIndex == id.Length - 1)
			{
				string text = ((string)id[currentIndex]).ToUpper();
				if (text.Equals(AdbArrayMethodGuids.SetValueAt))
				{
					string value = arguments[0] as string;
					int num = Array.IndexOf(_quals, value);
					if (num >= 0)
					{
						if (arguments[1] != null)
						{
							_vals[num] = (double)arguments[1];
						}
						else
						{
							_vals[num] = null;
						}
						result = num;
					}
				}
				else if (text.Equals(AdbArrayMethodGuids.SetValueAtLocation))
				{
					int num2 = (int)arguments[0];
					if (num2 >= 0 && num2 < _quals.Length)
					{
						if (arguments[1] != null)
						{
							_vals[num2] = (double)arguments[1];
						}
						else
						{
							_vals[num2] = null;
						}
						result = num2;
					}
				}
				else if (!text.Equals(AdbArrayMethodGuids.Append))
				{
					if (text.Equals(AdbArrayMethodGuids.ValueAt))
					{
						string value2 = arguments[0] as string;
						int num3 = Array.IndexOf(_quals, value2);
						if (num3 >= 0)
						{
							result = _vals[num3];
						}
					}
					else if (text.Equals(AdbArrayMethodGuids.ValueAtLocation))
					{
						int num4 = (int)arguments[0];
						if (num4 >= 0 && num4 < _quals.Length)
						{
							result = _vals[num4];
						}
						if (num4 == -1)
						{
							double?[] vals = _vals;
							for (int i = 0; i < vals.Length; i++)
							{
								double? num5 = vals[i];
								if (num5.HasValue)
								{
									result = num5;
									break;
								}
							}
						}
					}
					else if (text.Equals(AdbArrayMethodGuids.Length))
					{
						result = _quals.Length;
					}
					else if (!text.Equals(AdbArrayMethodGuids.InsertBeforeLocation) && !text.Equals(AdbArrayMethodGuids.RemoveAtLocation) && !text.Equals(AdbArrayMethodGuids.RemoveAt))
					{
						if (text.Equals(AdbArrayMethodGuids.IdAtLocation))
						{
							int num6 = (int)arguments[0];
							if (num6 >= 0 && num6 < _quals.Length)
							{
								result = _quals[num6];
							}
						}
						else if (!text.Equals(AdbArrayMethodGuids.SetId))
						{
							if (text.Equals(AdbArrayMethodGuids.FindId))
							{
								string value3 = arguments[0] as string;
								result = Array.IndexOf(_quals, value3);
							}
							else if (text.Equals(AdbArrayMethodGuids.FindValue))
							{
								double value4 = (double)arguments[0];
								result = Array.IndexOf(_vals, value4);
							}
							else if (text.Equals(AdbArrayMethodGuids.Clear))
							{
								Array.Clear(_vals, 0, _vals.Length);
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public void SetValue(XmlReader reader, AdbGuidCache guidCache)
		{
			Array.Clear(_vals, 0, _vals.Length);
			while (reader.Read() && (reader.NodeType != XmlNodeType.EndElement || !reader.Name.Equals("Value")))
			{
				if (reader.NodeType == XmlNodeType.Element)
				{
					for (int i = 0; i < _quals.Length; i++)
					{
						if (_quals[i].Equals(reader.Name))
						{
							reader.Read();
							if (double.TryParse(reader.Value, out var result))
							{
								_vals[i] = result;
							}
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					if (reader.NodeType != XmlNodeType.Text)
					{
						continue;
					}
					for (int j = 0; j < _quals.Length; j++)
					{
						if (_quals[j].Equals("Calculated") && double.TryParse(reader.Value, out var result2))
						{
							_vals[j] = result2;
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public string OutputString(AdbGuidCache guidCache)
		{
			string text = "";
			for (int i = 0; i < _quals.Length; i++)
			{
				if (_vals[i].HasValue)
				{
					XElement xElement = new XElement(_quals[i], _vals[i].ToString());
					text += xElement.ToString();
				}
			}
			return text;
		}

		public bool DeleteAllOutput(int mask = 3)
		{
			bool result = false;
			_vals[0] = null;
			if (!_vals[1].HasValue)
			{
				result = true;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public void Traverse()
		{
		}

		public static implicit operator AdbIOReal(double newval)
		{
			AdbIOReal adbIOReal = new AdbIOReal();
			adbIOReal._vals[0] = newval;
			return adbIOReal;
		}

		public bool StoreData(StorePathNode path, IArgumentData pathData, StringBuilder formattedData)
		{
			if (false)
			{
				ConverterWithArgs.DumpArg(pathData);
			}
			if (pathData.IsScalar)
			{
				formattedData?.Append(" = " + pathData.sVal + "\n");
				_vals[0] = pathData.rVal;
				return true;
			}
			formattedData?.Append(" = null\n");
			_vals[0] = null;
			return true;
		}
	}
	public class ExpandableMemoryMappedFile
	{
		protected MemoryMappedFile _file;

		protected long _size;

		protected string _name;

		protected long _numExpansions;

		public long ExpandCount => _numExpansions;

		public MemoryMappedFile File => _file;

		public ExpandableMemoryMappedFile(string shareName, long initialSize)
		{
			_size = initialSize;
			_name = shareName;
			_file = MemoryMappedFile.CreateOrOpen(_name, _size);
		}

		public MemoryMappedViewStream CreateViewStream()
		{
			return _file.CreateViewStream();
		}

		public void Expand()
		{
			_size *= 2L;
			_file.Dispose();
			_numExpansions++;
			string text = _name + _numExpansions;
			if (AdbSynchronizer.ShouldWriteSyncStuff)
			{
				DebugLog.WriteDebug("    Expanding file: " + text + " to " + _size + " bytes");
			}
			_file = MemoryMappedFile.CreateOrOpen(text, _size);
		}

		public void WasExpanded(long expandCount)
		{
			if (_numExpansions < expandCount)
			{
				while (_numExpansions < expandCount)
				{
					_size *= 2L;
					_numExpansions++;
				}
				_file.Dispose();
				string mapName = _name + _numExpansions;
				_file = MemoryMappedFile.OpenExisting(mapName);
			}
		}
	}
	public class StatusSection
	{
		public long FirstChangeNumber { get; set; }

		public long LastChangeNumber { get; set; }

		public long GuidFileOffset { get; set; }

		public long GuidFileLength { get; set; }

		public long DataFileOffset { get; set; }

		public long DataFileLength { get; set; }

		public long NewGuidFileExpandCount { get; set; }

		public long NewDataFileExpandCount { get; set; }

		public StatusSection()
		{
			NewDataFileExpandCount = -1L;
			NewGuidFileExpandCount = -1L;
		}
	}
	public class SyncStatus
	{
		public long NextChangeNumber { get; set; }

		public long NextOpenGuidLocation { get; set; }

		public long NextOpenDataLocation { get; set; }

		public int NumberOfSections { get; set; }

		public List<StatusSection> Sections { get; set; }

		public SyncStatus()
		{
			Sections = new List<StatusSection>();
		}
	}
	[ComVisible(true)]
	[Guid("BBBB9796-3B88-465E-B815-33371D7E9320")]
	public class AdbSynchronizer : AdbBase
	{
		public class WriteSync : ShouldBase
		{
			public override bool Test()
			{
				bool result = false;
				AdbOption option = AdbOptions.GetOption("debug");
				if (option != null)
				{
					result = option.HasSubOption("sync");
				}
				return result;
			}
		}

		protected enum Role
		{
			This,
			Other
		}

		protected enum FileUsage
		{
			Status,
			Data,
			GuidCache,
			Mutex
		}

		protected string[] _ident = new string[2];

		protected ExpandableMemoryMappedFile[] shareFiles = new ExpandableMemoryMappedFile[3];

		private string _pathToFile;

		protected SyncStatus myStatus = new SyncStatus();

		protected SyncStatus otherStatus = new SyncStatus();

		private static WriteSync _writeSync = new WriteSync();

		protected static long[] filesize = new long[3] { 20480L, 50000000L, 5000000L };

		private Mutex fileMutex;

		public string[] Ident
		{
			get
			{
				return _ident;
			}
			set
			{
				_ident = value;
				if (_ident != null && _ident.Any())
				{
					DebugLog.Role = _ident[0];
				}
			}
		}

		public string PathToFile
		{
			get
			{
				return _pathToFile;
			}
			set
			{
				_pathToFile = Path.GetFileNameWithoutExtension(value);
				DebugLog.PathToFile = _pathToFile;
			}
		}

		public static bool ShouldWriteSyncStuff => _writeSync.Should;

		public override bool ShouldBeInRecordMap => false;

		public AdbSynchronizer(AdbTop top, Guid? existingGuid)
			: base(top, existingGuid)
		{
		}

		public AdbSynchronizer(AdbTop top)
			: base(top)
		{
		}

		public AdbSynchronizer()
		{
		}

		public static int NumComponents(AdbTop top)
		{
			int result = 0;
			IAdbRecord adbRecord = top.TopRecord();
			if (adbRecord != null)
			{
				IAdbRecord adbRecord2 = adbRecord.ScalarReference("6139AB55-36B8-4F11-B965-BE9AF1398548");
				if (adbRecord2 != null)
				{
					result = (int)adbRecord2.ExecutePath(new object[2]
					{
						"C833291D-678B-43FB-BEE6-DDA52261EFCC",
						AdbArrayMethodGuids.Length
					}, null);
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected string ShareFileName(FileUsage usage)
		{
			return PathToFile + usage;
		}

		protected ExpandableMemoryMappedFile ShareFile(FileUsage usage)
		{
			ExpandableMemoryMappedFile expandableMemoryMappedFile = shareFiles[(int)usage];
			if (expandableMemoryMappedFile == null)
			{
				expandableMemoryMappedFile = new ExpandableMemoryMappedFile(ShareFileName(usage), filesize[(int)usage]);
				shareFiles[(int)usage] = expandableMemoryMappedFile;
			}
			return expandableMemoryMappedFile;
		}

		protected SyncStatus GetSyncState()
		{
			SyncStatus syncStatus = new SyncStatus();
			try
			{
				using MemoryMappedViewStream memoryMappedViewStream = ShareFile(FileUsage.Status).CreateViewStream();
				if (memoryMappedViewStream.Length >= 24)
				{
					using BinaryReader binaryReader = new BinaryReader(memoryMappedViewStream);
					syncStatus.NextChangeNumber = binaryReader.ReadInt64();
					syncStatus.NextOpenGuidLocation = binaryReader.ReadInt64();
					syncStatus.NextOpenDataLocation = binaryReader.ReadInt64();
					syncStatus.NumberOfSections = binaryReader.ReadInt32();
					for (int i = 0; i < syncStatus.NumberOfSections; i++)
					{
						StatusSection statusSection = new StatusSection();
						statusSection.FirstChangeNumber = binaryReader.ReadInt64();
						statusSection.LastChangeNumber = binaryReader.ReadInt64();
						statusSection.GuidFileOffset = binaryReader.ReadInt64();
						statusSection.GuidFileLength = binaryReader.ReadInt64();
						statusSection.DataFileOffset = binaryReader.ReadInt64();
						statusSection.DataFileLength = binaryReader.ReadInt64();
						statusSection.NewGuidFileExpandCount = binaryReader.ReadInt64();
						statusSection.NewDataFileExpandCount = binaryReader.ReadInt64();
						syncStatus.Sections.Add(statusSection);
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				_ = ex.Message;
			}
			return syncStatus;
		}

		protected void PutSyncState(SyncStatus status)
		{
			try
			{
				using MemoryMappedViewStream output = ShareFile(FileUsage.Status).CreateViewStream();
				using BinaryWriter binaryWriter = new BinaryWriter(output);
				binaryWriter.Write(status.NextChangeNumber);
				binaryWriter.Write(status.NextOpenGuidLocation);
				binaryWriter.Write(status.NextOpenDataLocation);
				binaryWriter.Write(status.NumberOfSections);
				foreach (StatusSection section in status.Sections)
				{
					binaryWriter.Write(section.FirstChangeNumber);
					binaryWriter.Write(section.LastChangeNumber);
					binaryWriter.Write(section.GuidFileOffset);
					binaryWriter.Write(section.GuidFileLength);
					binaryWriter.Write(section.DataFileOffset);
					binaryWriter.Write(section.DataFileLength);
					binaryWriter.Write(section.NewGuidFileExpandCount);
					binaryWriter.Write(section.NewDataFileExpandCount);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				_ = ex.Message;
			}
		}

		private void GetFileMutex()
		{
			TakeMutex();
			if (fileMutex == null)
			{
				string text = ShareFileName(FileUsage.Mutex);
				if (!Mutex.TryOpenExisting(text, out fileMutex))
				{
					if (ShouldWriteSyncStuff)
					{
						DebugLog.WriteDebug("    Creating Mutex: " + text);
					}
					fileMutex = new Mutex(initiallyOwned: true, text);
				}
				else
				{
					fileMutex.WaitOne();
					if (ShouldWriteSyncStuff)
					{
						DebugLog.WriteDebug("    Acquired Mutex: " + text);
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				fileMutex.WaitOne();
				if (ShouldWriteSyncStuff)
				{
					DebugLog.WriteDebug("    Acquired Mutex");
				}
			}
		}

		private void ReleaseFileMutex()
		{
			if (fileMutex != null)
			{
				if (ShouldWriteSyncStuff)
				{
					DebugLog.WriteDebug("    Releasing Mutex: ");
				}
				fileMutex.ReleaseMutex();
			}
			ReleaseMutex();
		}

		[AdbMethodIdent("E57E37A3-EFC3-4A66-AAC3-F74C9357C693")]
		public void SyncOut()
		{
			GetFileMutex();
			try
			{
				_ = DateTime.Now;
				myStatus = GetSyncState();
				if (ShouldWriteSyncStuff)
				{
					DebugLog.WriteDebug("     SyncOut MyNextChange " + base.Top.NextChangeNumber + " fileNextChange " + myStatus.NextChangeNumber);
				}
				if (base.Top.NextChangeNumber > myStatus.NextChangeNumber)
				{
					base.Top.PurgeCacheIncremental(myStatus, ShareFileName(FileUsage.Data), ShareFile(FileUsage.Data), ShareFile(FileUsage.GuidCache));
					PutSyncState(myStatus);
					if (ShouldWriteSyncStuff)
					{
						StatusSection statusSection = myStatus.Sections[myStatus.NumberOfSections - 1];
						DebugLog.WriteDebug("    Wrote section number " + (myStatus.NumberOfSections - 1) + " from " + statusSection.FirstChangeNumber + " to " + statusSection.LastChangeNumber);
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				if (ShouldWriteSyncStuff)
				{
					DebugLog.WriteDebug("Exception: " + ex.Message);
				}
			}
			ReleaseFileMutex();
		}

		[AdbMethodIdent("EFB6E63E-D29B-4495-B056-BAA7DF249A1A")]
		public void SyncIn()
		{
			GetFileMutex();
			try
			{
				_ = DateTime.Now;
				myStatus = GetSyncState();
				ShareFile(FileUsage.Data).WasExpanded((myStatus.Sections.Count == 0) ? 0 : myStatus.Sections.Last().NewDataFileExpandCount);
				ShareFile(FileUsage.GuidCache).WasExpanded((myStatus.Sections.Count == 0) ? 0 : myStatus.Sections.Last().NewGuidFileExpandCount);
				if (ShouldWriteSyncStuff)
				{
					DebugLog.WriteDebug("       SyncIn MyNextChange " + base.Top.NextChangeNumber + " fileNextChange " + myStatus.NextChangeNumber);
				}
				if (myStatus.NextChangeNumber > base.Top.NextChangeNumber)
				{
					foreach (StatusSection section in myStatus.Sections)
					{
						if (ShouldWriteSyncStuff)
						{
							DebugLog.WriteDebug("   ReadSection from change " + section.FirstChangeNumber + " to " + section.LastChangeNumber);
						}
						base.Top.Reconnect(section, ShareFile(FileUsage.Data), ShareFile(FileUsage.GuidCache));
						if (ShouldWriteSyncStuff)
						{
							DebugLog.WriteDebug("       Now my next change is " + base.Top.NextChangeNumber);
						}
					}
					myStatus.NextOpenDataLocation = 0L;
					myStatus.NextOpenGuidLocation = 0L;
					myStatus.NumberOfSections = 0;
					myStatus.Sections.Clear();
					PutSyncState(myStatus);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				if (ShouldWriteSyncStuff)
				{
					DebugLog.WriteDebug("Exception: " + ex.Message);
				}
			}
			ReleaseFileMutex();
		}

		[AdbMethodIdent("288A4ABC-20D2-4A18-8D99-486ACE375643")]
		public void ShutDown()
		{
			myStatus = new SyncStatus();
			PutSyncState(myStatus);
		}

		[AdbMethodIdent("36CEB77F-CD03-44D0-BAE9-773E6418635C")]
		public int RemoveOrphanRecords()
		{
			return base.Top.Compact();
		}
	}
	[ComVisible(true)]
	[Guid("04EFB39B-F3E2-451F-8D03-914319B89772")]
	public class AdbBase : AdbBaseRecord
	{
		private static Mutex adbMutex = new Mutex(initiallyOwned: false);

		public AdbBase(AdbTop top, Guid? existingGuid)
			: base(top, existingGuid)
		{
		}

		public AdbBase(AdbTop top)
			: base(top)
		{
		}

		public AdbBase()
		{
		}

		[AdbMethodIdent("5FF7AEE2-5679-46EB-A8E6-E205B961539E")]
		public bool CanCastTo(string guid)
		{
			bool result = false;
			Type type = GetType();
			Type typeForGuid = base.Top.GetTypeForGuid(guid);
			if (typeForGuid != null && typeForGuid.IsAssignableFrom(type))
			{
				result = true;
			}
			return result;
		}

		[AdbMethodIdent("3458A085-2781-4D09-AF89-24C171C35D42")]
		public string ObjectType()
		{
			return GetType().Name.Substring(3);
		}

		[AdbMethodIdent("B39F4736-2D05-4098-81A4-977E287CDA0D")]
		public object ObjectAtPath(string path)
		{
			return GetType().Name.Substring(3);
		}

		[AdbMethodIdent("21DE9BF1-C9A5-41E5-B3BE-2C8B63397A3C")]
		public int NumberOfVariables()
		{
			return base.GetFieldList.Count;
		}

		[AdbMethodIdent("A0DF097F-C7C9-4CA4-8052-426CDFC4450E", Comment1 = "int \"i\" bool \"b\" double \"r\"  string \"c\" reference \"f\" array \"a--\" array with value \"A--\" where -- are id and value types (  = noid)")]
		public string VariableTypeAtLocation(int index)
		{
			string result = "";
			if (index >= 0 && index < base.GetFieldList.Count)
			{
				FieldInfo f = base.GetFieldList[index].F;
				if (f.FieldType.IsGenericType && f.FieldType.GetGenericTypeDefinition() == typeof(Nullable<>))
				{
					result = AdbCppWriter.CppTypePrefix(f.FieldType.GetGenericArguments()[0]);
				}
				else if (!f.FieldType.IsGenericType)
				{
					result = ((!f.FieldType.Equals(typeof(AdbIOReal))) ? AdbCppWriter.CppTypePrefix(f.FieldType) : "Acr");
				}
				else
				{
					Type[] genericArguments = f.FieldType.GetGenericArguments();
					result = "a" + AdbCppWriter.CppTypePrefix(genericArguments[0]) + AdbCppWriter.CppTypePrefix(genericArguments[1]);
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		[AdbMethodIdent("8D807CF8-38E2-44F7-958B-4A56CFABCD31")]
		public object ValueAtLocation(int index)
		{
			object result = null;
			if (index >= 0 && index < base.GetFieldList.Count)
			{
				AdbFieldAttribute adbFieldAttribute = base.GetFieldList[index];
				result = ((!(adbFieldAttribute.GetterMethod != null)) ? adbFieldAttribute.F.GetValue(this) : adbFieldAttribute.GetterMethod.Invoke(this, null));
			}
			return result;
		}

		[AdbMethodIdent("7625742A-12C9-4038-839C-EBADFF7BB126")]
		public object ValueAt(string id)
		{
			object result = null;
			for (int i = 0; i < base.GetFieldList.Count; i++)
			{
				AdbFieldAttribute adbFieldAttribute = base.GetFieldList[i];
				FieldInfo f = adbFieldAttribute.F;
				if (string.Compare(f.Name, id, ignoreCase: true) == 0)
				{
					result = ((!(adbFieldAttribute.GetterMethod != null)) ? f.GetValue(this) : adbFieldAttribute.GetterMethod.Invoke(this, null));
					break;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		[AdbMethodIdent("7A428B8A-C4AB-4A82-85CB-536C55E7BD0C")]
		public int LocationOfID(string id)
		{
			int result = -1;
			for (int i = 0; i < base.GetFieldList.Count; i++)
			{
				AdbFieldAttribute adbFieldAttribute = base.GetFieldList[i];
				Guid.TryParse(id, out var result2);
				if (string.Compare(adbFieldAttribute.F.Name, id, ignoreCase: true) == 0 || (result2 != Guid.Empty && result2.Equals(adbFieldAttribute.Attribute.Id)))
				{
					result = i;
					break;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		[AdbMethodIdent("E8F4019E-B756-4257-A889-CEB57B477AAB")]
		public string IDAtLocation(int index)
		{
			string result = null;
			if (index >= 0 && index < base.GetFieldList.Count)
			{
				result = base.GetFieldList[index].F.Name;
			}
			return result;
		}

		[AdbMethodIdent("E931D7E9-BE61-434C-9776-1AFE6E76530C")]
		public string GuidAtLocation(int index)
		{
			string result = null;
			if (index >= 0 && index < base.GetFieldList.Count)
			{
				result = base.GetFieldList[index].Attribute.Id.ToString();
			}
			return result;
		}

		[AdbMethodIdent("E418B3BF-CE7E-4158-93C8-4D4E7DB6FC56")]
		public int UnitRow(string variableGuid)
		{
			int result = 0;
			AdbFieldAttribute fieldAttribute = GetFieldAttribute(Guid.Parse(variableGuid));
			if (fieldAttribute.DimensionVariable != null)
			{
				object value = fieldAttribute.DimensionVariable.GetValue(this);
				if (value != null)
				{
					try
					{
						result = (int)value;
					}
					catch
					{
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				result = (int)fieldAttribute.Attribute.PhysicalType;
			}
			return result;
		}

		[AdbMethodIdent("61A2B5F8-725C-445D-B68F-3E4957D3D3CA")]
		public string EconVariableName(string variableGuid)
		{
			string result = null;
			AdbFieldAttribute fieldAttribute = GetFieldAttribute(Guid.Parse(variableGuid));
			if (fieldAttribute != null)
			{
				result = fieldAttribute.Attribute.EconFieldName;
			}
			return result;
		}

		[AdbMethodIdent("606098A3-FB0E-49BB-9675-C406B3ECF2BD")]
		public string APAccessName(string variableGuid)
		{
			string result = null;
			AdbFieldAttribute fieldAttribute = GetFieldAttribute(Guid.Parse(variableGuid));
			if (fieldAttribute != null)
			{
				result = fieldAttribute.Attribute.APAccessName;
			}
			return result;
		}

		[AdbMethodIdent("D2894120-D2BD-4414-BCBC-28BB7824608F")]
		public AdbDataMover GetConverterByName(string myPrefix, string myObjectType)
		{
			AdbDataMover result = null;
			if (_top.DataMapper != null)
			{
				Objects val = _top.DataMapper.ObjectTable(myPrefix);
				if (val != null)
				{
					ObjectRow val2 = val.FindRowByName(myObjectType);
					if (val2 != null)
					{
						result = GetConverter(myPrefix, val2.vGuid);
					}
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		[AdbMethodIdent("CE51FE56-CA64-4ED4-8ECA-B078E59E02EB")]
		public AdbDataMover GetConverterByGuidString(string myPrefix, string guid)
		{
			AdbDataMover result = null;
			if (Guid.TryParse(guid, out var result2))
			{
				result = GetConverter(myPrefix, result2);
			}
			return result;
		}

		[AdbMethodIdent("C2BF74FB-0BAB-4938-A64D-A648BADCBE45")]
		public object GetArgumentData(string xmlDesc)
		{
			XElement argElem = XDocument.Parse(xmlDesc).Element("Args");
			return GetArgs(argElem);
		}

		protected AdbDataMover GetConverter(string myPrefix, Guid guid)
		{
			//IL_0027: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_002d: Expected O, but got Unknown
			AdbDataMover result = null;
			if (_top.DataMapper != null)
			{
				ConvertersBetweenObjects val = new ConvertersBetweenObjects(_top.DataMapper, myPrefix, guid, "Ads", base.ClassGuid);
				val.ConnectToConverters(_top.ConverterDictionary);
				result = new AdbDataMover(_top, null)
				{
					ConverterSet = val,
					TargetRecord = this
				};
			}
			return result;
		}

		[AdbMethodIdent("498F744D-B9B0-40AC-9A1D-4607D116B614")]
		public object TypeAtLocation(int index)
		{
			object result = null;
			if (index >= 0 && index < base.GetFieldList.Count)
			{
				result = base.GetFieldList[index].F.FieldType;
			}
			return result;
		}

		[AdbMethodIdent("A289FA90-F614-480E-899A-7225ADFC53C1")]
		public string StringId()
		{
			return GetStringId();
		}

		public virtual string GetStringId()
		{
			return null;
		}

		[AdbMethodIdent("BD37CE8C-9366-4395-8DCC-7E640809A931")]
		public void TakeMutex()
		{
			adbMutex.WaitOne();
		}

		[AdbMethodIdent("C4443BDC-1196-427D-82B0-BD4B9F85249C")]
		public void ReleaseMutex()
		{
			adbMutex.ReleaseMutex();
		}

		[AdbMethodIdent("0617F15E-5D0B-4D9B-B43B-E14FF917AF5E")]
		public void SetSavePullData(bool save)
		{
			base.SaveFormattedPutData = save;
			if (save)
			{
				SavedData = new List<string>();
			}
			else
			{
				SavedData = null;
			}
		}

		[AdbMethodIdent("5494D083-A7A0-4CA4-9F49-8874D95ABA1F")]
		public void PullData()
		{
			if (SavedData == null)
			{
				base.Top.NotifyOnRequestData(this);
			}
			else
			{
				base.Top.NotifyOnRequestDataNoDirtyFlag(this);
			}
		}

		[AdbMethodIdent("736B0032-CB6B-4BCC-9567-80717014477F")]
		public string GetLatestPulledData()
		{
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			if (SavedData != null)
			{
				foreach (string savedDatum in SavedData)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(savedDatum);
				}
			}
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		private void ArgDataToString(StringBuilder builder, StorePathNode storePath, IArgumentData args, string indent, bool newLine)
		{
			if (storePath == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			string text = "";
			if (newLine)
			{
				text = indent;
			}
			if (!storePath.IsEnumerator)
			{
				builder.Append(text + storePath.NodeName);
				if (storePath.Next == null)
				{
					if (args.IsScalar && args.ScalarValue != null)
					{
						builder.AppendLine(" " + args.ScalarValue.ToString());
					}
					else
					{
						builder.AppendLine("");
					}
				}
				else
				{
					ArgDataToString(builder, storePath.Next, args, indent + "  ", newLine: false);
				}
				return;
			}
			int num = 0;
			foreach (KeyValuePair<string, IArgumentData> idValue in args.IdValues)
			{
				if (idValue.Key != null)
				{
					builder.Append(indent + idValue.Key);
				}
				else
				{
					builder.Append(text + "#" + num);
				}
				if (idValue.Value.IsScalar && idValue.Value.ScalarValue != null)
				{
					builder.AppendLine("    " + idValue.Value.ScalarValue.ToString());
				}
				else
				{
					ArgDataToString(builder, storePath.Next, idValue.Value, indent + "  ", newLine: false);
				}
			}
		}
	}
	public class AdbChange : IAdbChange
	{
		private string _description;

		public string VariableName { get; set; }

		public Guid VariableGuid { get; set; }

		public AdbChangeType changeType { get; set; }

		public int currentLocation { get; set; }

		internal IAdbRecord record { get; set; }

		internal AdbFieldAttribute attr { get; set; }

		internal object value { get; set; }

		internal object id { get; set; }

		internal object old { get; set; }

		public string Description
		{
			get
			{
				if (_description == null)
				{
					switch (changeType)
					{
					case AdbChangeType.ArrayAppend:
						_description = $"<SingleChange record=\"{record.RecordGuid()}\"><Variable name=\"{attr.F.Name}\" guid=\"{VariableGuid.ToString()}\" /><Change><ArrayAppend>{FormatID(id)}{FormatValue(value)}</ArrayAppend></Change></SingleChange>";
						break;
					case AdbChangeType.ArrayInsertBefore:
						_description = string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "<SingleChange record=\"{0}\"><Variable name=\"{1}\" guid=\"{2}\" /><Change><ArrayInsertBefore><Location>{3}</Location>{4}{5}</ArrayInsertBefore></Change></SingleChange>", record.RecordGuid(), attr.F.Name, VariableGuid.ToString(), currentLocation, FormatID(id), FormatValue(value));
						break;
					case AdbChangeType.ArrayRemove:
						_description = string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "<SingleChange record=\"{0}\"><Variable name=\"{1}\" guid=\"{2}\" /><Change><ArrayRemove><Location>{3}</Location><Id>{4}</Id></ArrayRemove></Change></SingleChange>", record.RecordGuid(), attr.F.Name, VariableGuid.ToString(), currentLocation, FormatID(id));
						break;
					case AdbChangeType.ArraySetId:
						_description = string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "<SingleChange record=\"{0}\"><Variable name=\"{1}\" guid=\"{2}\" /><Change><ArraySetId><Location>{3}</Location>{4}</ArraySetId></Change></SingleChange>", record.RecordGuid(), attr.F.Name, VariableGuid.ToString(), currentLocation, FormatID(id));
						break;
					case AdbChangeType.ArraySetValue:
						_description = string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "<SingleChange record=\"{0}\"><Variable name=\"{1}\" guid=\"{2}\" /><Change><ArraySetValue><Location>{3}</Location>{4}</ArraySetValue></Change></SingleChange>", record.RecordGuid(), attr.F.Name, VariableGuid.ToString(), currentLocation, FormatValue(value));
						break;
					case AdbChangeType.RecordDelete:
						_description = $"<DeleteRecord record=\"{record.RecordGuid()}\"/>";
						break;
					case AdbChangeType.ValueChange:
						_description = string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "<SingleChange record=\"{0}\"><Variable name=\"{1}\" guid=\"{2}\" /><Change><ValueChange>{3}{4}</ValueChange></Change></SingleChange>", record.RecordGuid(), attr.F.Name, VariableGuid.ToString(), FormatNewValue(value), FormatOldValue(old));
						break;
					}
				}
				return _description;
			}
			set
			{
				_description = value;
			}
		}

		private static string FormatID(object id)
		{
			if (id != null)
			{
				if (id is AdbNoId)
				{
					return "";
				}
				if (id is string)
				{
					return $"<Id datatype=\"string\">{id}</Id>";
				}
				if (id is int)
				{
					return string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "<Id datatype=\"int\">{0}</Id>", id);
				}
				if (id is double)
				{
					return string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "<Id datatype=\"double\">{0}</Id>", id);
				}
				if (id is AdbBaseRecord)
				{
					return $"<Id datatype=\"{id.GetType().Name}\">{(id as AdbBaseRecord).RecordGuid()}</Id>";
				}
			}
			return "";
		}

		private static string FormatValue(object value)
		{
			if (value == null)
			{
				return "<Value datatype=\"{x:Null}\" />";
			}
			if (value is string)
			{
				return $"<Value datatype=\"string\">{value}</Value>";
			}
			if (value is int)
			{
				return string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "<Value datatype=\"int\">{0}</Value>", value);
			}
			if (value is double)
			{
				return string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "<Value datatype=\"double\">{0}</Value>", value);
			}
			if (value is AdbBaseRecord)
			{
				return $"<Value datatype=\"{value.GetType().Name}\">{(value as AdbBaseRecord).RecordGuid()}</Value>";
			}
			return "";
		}

		private static string FormatNewValue(object value)
		{
			if (value == null)
			{
				return "<NewValue datatype=\"{x:Null}\" />";
			}
			if (value is string)
			{
				return $"<NewValue datatype=\"string\">{value}</NewValue>";
			}
			if (value is int)
			{
				return string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "<NewValue datatype=\"int\">{0}</NewValue>", value);
			}
			if (value is double)
			{
				return string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "<NewValue datatype=\"double\">{0}</NewValue>", value);
			}
			if (value is AdbBaseRecord)
			{
				return $"<NewValue datatype=\"{value.GetType().Name}\">{(value as AdbBaseRecord).RecordGuid()}</NewValue>";
			}
			return "";
		}

		private static string FormatOldValue(object value)
		{
			if (value == null)
			{
				return "";
			}
			if (value is string)
			{
				return $"<OldValue datatype=\"string\">{value}</OldValue>";
			}
			if (value is int)
			{
				return string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "<OldValue datatype=\"int\">{0}</OldValue>", value);
			}
			if (value is double)
			{
				return string.Format(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, "<OldValue datatype=\"double\">{0}</OldValue>", value);
			}
			if (value is AdbBaseRecord)
			{
				return $"<OldValue datatype=\"{value.GetType().Name}\">{(value as AdbBaseRecord).RecordGuid()}</OldValue>";
			}
			return "";
		}
	}
	public class AdbNotification : IAdbNotification
	{
		public IAdbTop Top { get; set; }

		public IAdbRecord Record { get; set; }

		public IAdbChange Change { get; set; }

		public object notifyTag { get; set; }
	}
	public class AdbNotificationHolder : IAdbNotificationHolder
	{
		private List<IAdbNotification> _notifies;

		public int NotificationCount => _notifies.Count;

		public IEnumerable<IAdbNotification> Notifications => _notifies;

		public AdbNotificationHolder()
		{
			_notifies = new List<IAdbNotification>();
		}

		public IAdbNotification[] GetNotifications()
		{
			return _notifies.ToArray();
		}

		public void AddNotify(AdbNotification newNotify)
		{
			_notifies.Add(newNotify);
		}

		public void ClearNotifies()
		{
			_notifies.Clear();
		}
	}
	public class AdbRecordNotifyList
	{
		public List<AdbChange> Pending { get; private set; }

		public Dictionary<Guid, AdbChange> ValueChanges { get; private set; }

		public Dictionary<KeyValuePair<Guid, int>, AdbChange> ArrayValueChanges { get; private set; }

		public object Tag { get; set; }

		public AdbRecordNotifyList()
		{
			Pending = new List<AdbChange>();
			ValueChanges = new Dictionary<Guid, AdbChange>();
			ArrayValueChanges = new Dictionary<KeyValuePair<Guid, int>, AdbChange>();
		}

		public void Add(AdbChange change)
		{
			switch (change.changeType)
			{
			case AdbChangeType.RecordDelete:
				Pending.Clear();
				ValueChanges.Clear();
				break;
			case AdbChangeType.ValueChange:
				if (ValueChanges.ContainsKey(change.VariableGuid))
				{
					Pending.Remove(ValueChanges[change.VariableGuid]);
				}
				ValueChanges.Add(change.VariableGuid, change);
				break;
			case AdbChangeType.ArraySetValue:
				if (ArrayValueChanges.ContainsKey(new KeyValuePair<Guid, int>(change.VariableGuid, change.currentLocation)))
				{
					ArrayValueChanges.Remove(new KeyValuePair<Guid, int>(change.VariableGuid, change.currentLocation));
				}
				ArrayValueChanges.Add(new KeyValuePair<Guid, int>(change.VariableGuid, change.currentLocation), change);
				break;
			}
			Pending.Add(change);
		}
	}
	public class AdbNotifyBucket : IDisposable
	{
		private Dictionary<IAdbRecord, AdbRecordNotifyList> _list = new Dictionary<IAdbRecord, AdbRecordNotifyList>();

		private AdbNotifyTarget _target;

		private AdbTop _top;

		public string Key { get; private set; }

		public AdbNotifyBucket(AdbTop top, AdbNotifyTarget target)
		{
			_top = top;
			_target = target;
			Key = Guid.NewGuid().ToString();
		}

		public bool AddNotify(IAdbRecord record, object notifyTag)
		{
			if (_list.ContainsKey(record))
			{
				return false;
			}
			_list.Add(record, new AdbRecordNotifyList
			{
				Tag = notifyTag
			});
			return true;
		}

		public void SendPending()
		{
			if (_target == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			AdbNotificationHolder adbNotificationHolder = new AdbNotificationHolder();
			foreach (KeyValuePair<IAdbRecord, AdbRecordNotifyList> item in _list)
			{
				if ((item.Key as AdbBaseRecord).Dead || item.Value.Pending.Count <= 0)
				{
					continue;
				}
				foreach (AdbChange item2 in item.Value.Pending)
				{
					AdbNotification newNotify = new AdbNotification
					{
						Top = _top,
						Record = item.Key,
						notifyTag = item.Value.Tag,
						Change = item2
					};
					adbNotificationHolder.AddNotify(newNotify);
				}
				item.Value.Pending.Clear();
				item.Value.ValueChanges.Clear();
				item.Value.ArrayValueChanges.Clear();
			}
			_target(adbNotificationHolder);
		}

		public void Dispose()
		{
			Dispose(disposing: true);
		}

		public void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (disposing)
			{
				_target = null;
				_list.Clear();
			}
		}

		public void OnRequestDataNoDirtyFlag(IAdbRecord record)
		{
			DoRequestData(record, useDirtyFlag: false);
		}

		public void OnRequestData(IAdbRecord record)
		{
			DoRequestData(record, useDirtyFlag: true);
		}

		private void DoRequestData(IAdbRecord record, bool useDirtyFlag)
		{
			if (_list.ContainsKey(record))
			{
				AdbChangeType changeType = (AdbChangeType)8;
				if (!useDirtyFlag)
				{
					changeType = (AdbChangeType)9;
				}
				AdbChange change = new AdbChange
				{
					changeType = changeType,
					record = record
				};
				if (_top.NotifiesHeld)
				{
					_list[record].Add(change);
					return;
				}
				AdbNotification newNotify = new AdbNotification
				{
					Top = _top,
					Record = record,
					notifyTag = _list[record].Tag,
					Change = change
				};
				AdbNotificationHolder adbNotificationHolder = new AdbNotificationHolder();
				adbNotificationHolder.AddNotify(newNotify);
				_target(adbNotificationHolder);
			}
		}

		public void OnRequestAllData()
		{
			foreach (IAdbRecord key in _list.Keys)
			{
				AdbChange change = new AdbChange
				{
					changeType = (AdbChangeType)8,
					record = key
				};
				if (_top.NotifiesHeld)
				{
					_list[key].Add(change);
					continue;
				}
				AdbNotification newNotify = new AdbNotification
				{
					Top = _top,
					Record = key,
					notifyTag = _list[key].Tag,
					Change = change
				};
				AdbNotificationHolder adbNotificationHolder = new AdbNotificationHolder();
				adbNotificationHolder.AddNotify(newNotify);
				_target(adbNotificationHolder);
			}
		}

		public void OnValueChange(IAdbRecord record, string field, object value, object oldValue)
		{
			if (_list.ContainsKey(record) && Guid.TryParse(field, out var result) && (record as AdbBaseRecord).ClassInfo.fields.ContainsKey(result))
			{
				AdbFieldAttribute adbFieldAttribute = (record as AdbBaseRecord).ClassInfo.fields[result];
				AdbChange change = new AdbChange
				{
					changeType = AdbChangeType.ValueChange,
					VariableGuid = result,
					VariableName = adbFieldAttribute.F.Name,
					record = record,
					attr = adbFieldAttribute,
					value = value,
					old = oldValue
				};
				if (_top.NotifiesHeld)
				{
					_list[record].Add(change);
					return;
				}
				AdbNotification newNotify = new AdbNotification
				{
					Top = _top,
					Record = record,
					notifyTag = _list[record].Tag,
					Change = change
				};
				AdbNotificationHolder adbNotificationHolder = new AdbNotificationHolder();
				adbNotificationHolder.AddNotify(newNotify);
				_target(adbNotificationHolder);
			}
		}

		public void OnArrayAppend(IAdbRecord record, string field, object id, object value)
		{
			if (_list.ContainsKey(record) && Guid.TryParse(field, out var result) && (record as AdbBaseRecord).ClassInfo.fields.ContainsKey(result))
			{
				AdbFieldAttribute adbFieldAttribute = (record as AdbBaseRecord).ClassInfo.fields[result];
				AdbChange change = new AdbChange
				{
					changeType = AdbChangeType.ArrayAppend,
					VariableGuid = result,
					VariableName = adbFieldAttribute.F.Name,
					record = record,
					attr = adbFieldAttribute,
					value = value,
					id = id
				};
				if (_top.NotifiesHeld)
				{
					_list[record].Add(change);
					return;
				}
				AdbNotification newNotify = new AdbNotification
				{
					Top = _top,
					Record = record,
					notifyTag = _list[record].Tag,
					Change = change
				};
				AdbNotificationHolder adbNotificationHolder = new AdbNotificationHolder();
				adbNotificationHolder.AddNotify(newNotify);
				_target(adbNotificationHolder);
			}
		}

		public void OnArrayInsertBefore(IAdbRecord record, string field, int? location, object id, object value)
		{
			if (_list.ContainsKey(record) && Guid.TryParse(field, out var result) && (record as AdbBaseRecord).ClassInfo.fields.ContainsKey(result))
			{
				AdbFieldAttribute adbFieldAttribute = (record as AdbBaseRecord).ClassInfo.fields[result];
				AdbChange change = new AdbChange
				{
					changeType = AdbChangeType.ArrayInsertBefore,
					VariableGuid = result,
					VariableName = adbFieldAttribute.F.Name,
					currentLocation = (location ?? 0),
					record = record,
					attr = adbFieldAttribute,
					id = id,
					value = value
				};
				if (_top.NotifiesHeld)
				{
					_list[record].Add(change);
					return;
				}
				AdbNotification newNotify = new AdbNotification
				{
					Top = _top,
					Record = record,
					notifyTag = _list[record].Tag,
					Change = change
				};
				AdbNotificationHolder adbNotificationHolder = new AdbNotificationHolder();
				adbNotificationHolder.AddNotify(newNotify);
				_target(adbNotificationHolder);
			}
		}

		public void OnArraySetValueAt(IAdbRecord record, string field, int? location, object value)
		{
			if (_list.ContainsKey(record) && Guid.TryParse(field, out var result) && (record as AdbBaseRecord).ClassInfo.fields.ContainsKey(result))
			{
				AdbFieldAttribute adbFieldAttribute = (record as AdbBaseRecord).ClassInfo.fields[result];
				AdbChange change = new AdbChange
				{
					changeType = AdbChangeType.ArraySetValue,
					VariableGuid = result,
					VariableName = adbFieldAttribute.F.Name,
					currentLocation = (location ?? 0),
					record = record,
					attr = adbFieldAttribute,
					value = value
				};
				if (_top.NotifiesHeld)
				{
					_list[record].Add(change);
					return;
				}
				AdbNotification newNotify = new AdbNotification
				{
					Top = _top,
					Record = record,
					notifyTag = _list[record].Tag,
					Change = change
				};
				AdbNotificationHolder adbNotificationHolder = new AdbNotificationHolder();
				adbNotificationHolder.AddNotify(newNotify);
				_target(adbNotificationHolder);
			}
		}

		public void OnArraySetId(IAdbRecord record, string field, int? location, object id)
		{
			if (_list.ContainsKey(record) && Guid.TryParse(field, out var result) && (record as AdbBaseRecord).ClassInfo.fields.ContainsKey(result))
			{
				AdbFieldAttribute adbFieldAttribute = (record as AdbBaseRecord).ClassInfo.fields[result];
				AdbChange change = new AdbChange
				{
					changeType = AdbChangeType.ArraySetId,
					VariableGuid = result,
					VariableName = adbFieldAttribute.F.Name,
					currentLocation = (location ?? 0),
					record = record,
					attr = adbFieldAttribute,
					id = id
				};
				if (_top.NotifiesHeld)
				{
					_list[record].Add(change);
					return;
				}
				AdbNotification newNotify = new AdbNotification
				{
					Top = _top,
					Record = record,
					notifyTag = _list[record].Tag,
					Change = change
				};
				AdbNotificationHolder adbNotificationHolder = new AdbNotificationHolder();
				adbNotificationHolder.AddNotify(newNotify);
				_target(adbNotificationHolder);
			}
		}

		public void OnArrayRemoveAt(IAdbRecord record, string field, int? location, object id)
		{
			if (_list.ContainsKey(record) && Guid.TryParse(field, out var result) && (record as AdbBaseRecord).ClassInfo.fields.ContainsKey(result))
			{
				AdbFieldAttribute adbFieldAttribute = (record as AdbBaseRecord).ClassInfo.fields[result];
				AdbChange change = new AdbChange
				{
					changeType = AdbChangeType.ArrayRemove,
					VariableGuid = result,
					VariableName = adbFieldAttribute.F.Name,
					currentLocation = (location ?? 0),
					record = record,
					attr = adbFieldAttribute,
					id = id
				};
				if (_top.NotifiesHeld)
				{
					_list[record].Add(change);
					return;
				}
				AdbNotification newNotify = new AdbNotification
				{
					Top = _top,
					Record = record,
					notifyTag = _list[record].Tag,
					Change = change
				};
				AdbNotificationHolder adbNotificationHolder = new AdbNotificationHolder();
				adbNotificationHolder.AddNotify(newNotify);
				_target(adbNotificationHolder);
			}
		}

		public void OnRecordDelete(IAdbRecord record)
		{
			if (!_list.ContainsKey(record))
			{
				return;
			}
			AdbChange change = new AdbChange
			{
				changeType = AdbChangeType.RecordDelete,
				record = record
			};
			if (_top.NotifiesHeld)
			{
				_list[record].Add(change);
				return;
			}
			AdbNotification newNotify = new AdbNotification
			{
				Top = _top,
				Record = record,
				notifyTag = _list[record].Tag,
				Change = change
			};
			if (_list.ContainsKey(record))
			{
				_list.Remove(record);
			}
			AdbNotificationHolder adbNotificationHolder = new AdbNotificationHolder();
			adbNotificationHolder.AddNotify(newNotify);
			_target(adbNotificationHolder);
		}
	}

	[ComVisible(true)]
	[Guid("1FB94A84-550D-42C9-BC5C-18A2F04BEBF3")]
	public class AdbManager : IAdbManager
	{
		private bool bNoADSReg;

		public AdbManager()
		{
			try
			{
				using RegistryKey registryKey = Registry.CurrentUser.OpenSubKey("Software\\AspenTech\\Common Components\\AspenDataShare\\37.0");
				if ((registryKey?.GetValue("NoAds"))?.ToString() == "1")
				{
					bNoADSReg = true;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
		}

		public IAdbTop OpenDatabase(string connectionString, bool createIfNecessary)
		{
			IAdbTop result = null;
			if (!AdbOptions.HasOption("NoAds") && !bNoADSReg)
			{
				try
				{
					result = AdbTop.OpenCreate(connectionString, forceNew: false, !createIfNecessary);
				}
				catch
				{
					result = CreateDatabase(connectionString);
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public IAdbTop CreateDatabase(string connectionString)
		{
			IAdbTop result = null;
			if (!AdbOptions.HasOption("NoAds") && !bNoADSReg)
			{
				result = AdbTop.OpenCreate(connectionString, forceNew: true, forceExist: false);
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
	public class AdbValueDescriptor : IAdbBaseObject, IAdbRecord, IAdsDataStorer
	{
		public enum ValueType
		{
			Int,
			Real,
			String,
			Bool,
			Array,
			Reference,
			IOReal,
			None
		}

		protected AdbBaseRecord _record;

		public ValueType VType { get; set; }

		public IAdbBaseObject ArrayObject { get; set; }

		public AdbSetObjectValueDelegate setValue { get; set; }

		public AdbSetObjectValueDelegate restoreValue { get; set; }

		public AdbGetObjectValueDelegate getValue { get; set; }

		public AdbOnSetObjectValueDelegate onSetValue { get; set; }

		public AdbTop Top { get; set; }

		public AdbFieldAttribute Af { get; set; }

		private AdbValueDescriptor()
		{
		}

		public AdbValueDescriptor(AdbBaseRecord record)
		{
			_record = record;
		}

		public object Value(object[] id, int currentIndex)
		{
			object obj = null;
			if (ArrayObject != null)
			{
				obj = ((currentIndex != id.Length) ? ArrayObject.Value(id, currentIndex) : this);
			}
			else if (getValue != null)
			{
				obj = getValue();
				if (currentIndex < id.Length && obj is IAdbBaseObject adbBaseObject)
				{
					obj = adbBaseObject.Value(id, currentIndex);
				}
			}
			return obj;
		}

		public void RestoreValue(object[] id, int currentIndex, object newValue)
		{
			if (ArrayObject != null)
			{
				ArrayObject.RestoreValue(id, currentIndex, newValue);
			}
			else if (restoreValue != null)
			{
				restoreValue(newValue);
				if (onSetValue != null)
				{
					onSetValue();
				}
			}
			else if (setValue != null)
			{
				setValue(newValue);
				if (onSetValue != null)
				{
					onSetValue();
				}
			}
		}

		public void SetValue(object[] id, int currentIndex, object newValue)
		{
			if (ArrayObject != null)
			{
				ArrayObject.SetValue(id, currentIndex, newValue);
			}
			else if (setValue != null)
			{
				setValue(newValue);
				if (onSetValue != null)
				{
					onSetValue();
				}
			}
		}

		public void SetValueFromPersistence(string sval, AdbGuidCache guidCache, IFormatProvider provider = null)
		{
			object obj = null;
			if (provider == null)
			{
				provider = CultureInfo.CurrentCulture;
			}
			switch (VType)
			{
			case ValueType.Int:
				obj = int.Parse(sval, provider);
				break;
			case ValueType.Real:
				if (sval != null)
				{
					obj = double.Parse(sval, provider);
				}
				break;
			case ValueType.Bool:
				obj = bool.Parse(sval);
				break;
			case ValueType.String:
				obj = sval;
				break;
			case ValueType.Reference:
				if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(sval))
				{
					guidCache.Fixups.AddReferenceFixup(guidCache.GetGuidForIndex(int.Parse(sval)), (restoreValue == null) ? setValue : restoreValue, onSetValue);
				}
				break;
			}
			if (VType == ValueType.None || VType == ValueType.Reference || VType == ValueType.Array)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (restoreValue != null)
			{
				restoreValue(obj);
				if (onSetValue != null)
				{
					onSetValue();
				}
			}
			else if (setValue != null)
			{
				setValue(obj);
				if (onSetValue != null)
				{
					onSetValue();
				}
			}
		}

		public void SetValue(XmlReader reader, AdbGuidCache guidCache)
		{
			if (VType == ValueType.Array)
			{
				ArrayObject.SetValue(reader, guidCache);
				return;
			}
			reader.Read();
			if (reader.NodeType == XmlNodeType.Text && !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(reader.Value))
			{
				string text = reader.Value;
				if (text != null && text.Equals("1E+35") && Af != null && Af.F != null && Af.F.FieldType == Type.GetType("System.Nullable`1[System.Double]"))
				{
					text = null;
				}
				SetValueFromPersistence(text, guidCache, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
			}
			else if (reader.NodeType == XmlNodeType.Element)
			{
				string sval = reader.ReadInnerXml();
				SetValueFromPersistence(sval, null);
			}
		}

		public object ExecuteMethod(object[] id, int currentIndex, object[] arguments)
		{
			object result = null;
			if (ArrayObject != null)
			{
				result = ArrayObject.ExecuteMethod(id, currentIndex, arguments);
			}
			return result;
		}

		public string OutputString(AdbGuidCache guidCache)
		{
			string result = null;
			if (ArrayObject != null)
			{
				result = ArrayObject.OutputString(guidCache);
			}
			else if (getValue != null)
			{
				object obj = getValue();
				result = ((obj == null) ? "" : ((VType != ValueType.Reference) ? obj.ToString() : ((IAdbBaseObject)obj).OutputString(guidCache)));
			}
			return result;
		}

		public bool DeleteAllOutput(int mask = 3)
		{
			bool result = false;
			if (ArrayObject != null)
			{
				result = ArrayObject.DeleteAllOutput(mask);
			}
			else if (VType == ValueType.Reference)
			{
				bool flag = false;
				if (getValue() is AdbBaseRecord { ExemptFromThisPurge: false } adbBaseRecord)
				{
					if (adbBaseRecord != null)
					{
						flag = adbBaseRecord.DeleteAllOutput(mask);
					}
					if (flag || (_record.IsOutput(Af) & mask) != 0)
					{
						if (setValue != null)
						{
							setValue(null);
						}
						result = true;
					}
				}
			}
			else if ((_record.IsOutput(Af) & mask) != 0)
			{
				if (setValue != null)
				{
					setValue(null);
				}
				result = true;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public void Traverse()
		{
		}

		public object Value(object[] path)
		{
			return Value(path, 0);
		}

		public IAdbRecord Reference(object[] path)
		{
			return Value(path, 0) as IAdbRecord;
		}

		public void SetValue(object[] path, object newValue)
		{
			SetValue(path, 0, newValue);
		}

		public object ScalarValue(string guid, out string units)
		{
			units = null;
			return null;
		}

		public IAdbRecord ScalarReference(string guid)
		{
			return null;
		}

		public void SetScalarValue(string guid, object newValue)
		{
		}

		public object Execute(string guid, object[] arguments)
		{
			object result = null;
			if (ArrayObject != null)
			{
				result = ArrayObject.ExecuteMethod(new object[1] { guid }, 0, arguments);
			}
			return result;
		}

		public object ExecutePath(object[] path, object[] arguments)
		{
			return null;
		}

		public string RecordGuid()
		{
			return null;
		}

		public bool StoreData(StorePathNode path, IArgumentData pathData, StringBuilder formattedData)
		{
			bool flag = pathData != null;
			if (VType == ValueType.Reference)
			{
				bool flag2 = false;
				object obj = getValue();
				if (obj == null)
				{
					if (setValue == null)
					{
						throw new Exception("trying to store into read only object");
					}
					flag2 = true;
					obj = Af.F.FieldType.Create(Top, _record, null);
					setValue(obj);
				}
				flag = ((IAdsDataStorer)((obj is IAdsDataStorer) ? obj : null)).StoreData(path.Next, pathData, formattedData);
				if (!flag && flag2)
				{
					if (setValue != null)
					{
						setValue(null);
					}
					if (obj is AdbBaseRecord adbBaseRecord)
					{
						adbBaseRecord.CleanUp();
						adbBaseRecord.ExecuteMethod(new object[1] { "29F5B8E6-0D25-438A-A737-25C677293C63" }, 0, null);
					}
				}
			}
			else if (VType == ValueType.Array)
			{
				IAdbBaseObject arrayObject = ArrayObject;
				flag = ((IAdsDataStorer)((arrayObject is IAdsDataStorer) ? arrayObject : null)).StoreData(path.Next, pathData, formattedData);
			}
			else if (pathData != null)
			{
				if (Af.F.FieldType == typeof(int) || Af.F.FieldType == typeof(int?))
				{
					if (!pathData.iVal.HasValue || pathData.iVal.Value == -32767 || pathData.iVal.Value == -32768)
					{
						if (Af.F.FieldType == typeof(int?))
						{
							if (setValue != null)
							{
								setValue(null);
							}
							flag = false;
						}
						else
						{
							if (setValue != null)
							{
								setValue(99999999);
							}
							formattedData?.Append(" = 99999999\n");
							flag = true;
						}
					}
					else
					{
						if (setValue != null)
						{
							setValue(pathData.iVal.Value);
						}
						formattedData?.Append(" = " + pathData.sVal + "\n");
						flag = true;
					}
				}
				else if (Af.F.FieldType == typeof(double) || Af.F.FieldType == typeof(double?))
				{
					if (!pathData.iVal.HasValue || !pathData.rVal.HasValue || pathData.iVal.Value == -32767 || pathData.rVal.Value == 1E+38)
					{
						if (Af.F.FieldType == typeof(double?))
						{
							if (setValue != null)
							{
								setValue(null);
							}
							flag = false;
						}
						else
						{
							if (setValue != null)
							{
								setValue(1E+38);
							}
							formattedData?.Append(" = 1E38\n");
							flag = true;
						}
					}
					else
					{
						if (setValue != null)
						{
							setValue(pathData.rVal.Value);
						}
						formattedData?.Append(" = " + pathData.sVal + "\n");
						flag = true;
					}
				}
				else if (Af.F.FieldType == typeof(string))
				{
					if (pathData.sVal == null || pathData.sVal.Equals("-32767"))
					{
						if (setValue != null)
						{
							setValue(null);
						}
						flag = false;
					}
					else
					{
						if (setValue != null)
						{
							setValue(pathData.sVal);
						}
						formattedData?.Append(" = " + pathData.sVal + "\n");
						flag = true;
					}
				}
				else if (Af.F.FieldType == typeof(bool) || Af.F.FieldType == typeof(bool?))
				{
					if (setValue != null)
					{
						setValue(pathData.bVal);
					}
					if (pathData.bVal.HasValue)
					{
						if (formattedData != null)
						{
							formattedData.Append(" = " + pathData.sVal + "\n");
							flag = true;
						}
					}
					else
					{
						flag = false;
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (setValue != null)
				{
					setValue(null);
				}
				flag = false;
			}
			return flag;
		}
	}
