	public class AdbSummaryFileReader
	{
		internal class LineServer
		{
			private FileStream _fs;

			private StreamReader _sr;

			private string[] _lines;

			private static char[] trimchar = new char[1] { '\r' };

			public IEnumerable<string> Lines
			{
				get
				{
					if (_lines != null)
					{
						string[] lines = _lines;
						for (int i = 0; i < lines.Length; i++)
						{
							yield return lines[i].Trim(trimchar);
						}
					}
					else if (_sr != null)
					{
						for (string text = _sr.ReadLine(); text != null; text = _sr.ReadLine())
						{
							yield return text;
						}
						_sr.Close();
					}
				}
			}

			public void SetFile(string filename)
			{
				_fs = new FileStream(filename, FileMode.Open, FileAccess.Read);
				_sr = new StreamReader(_fs);
			}

			public void SetString(string summaryFileString)
			{
				_lines = summaryFileString.Split(new char[1] { '\n' }, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
			}
		}

		private List<DSet> _dSets = new List<DSet>();

		private string _filename;

		private static int tcount = 0;

		private static TimeSpan grandTotal = new TimeSpan(0L);

		public static IdSets IdSetMap { get; protected set; }

		public static LSets LSetMap { get; protected set; }

		public DSetSets DsetMap { get; protected set; }

		public AdbTop Top { get; protected set; }

		public AdbSummaryFileReader(AdbTop top, string filename)
		{
			Top = top;
			_filename = filename;
		}

		public void ReadSum(string hierarchyLevel, string summaryFile)
		{
			if (Top.DataMapper == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			Top.IsReadingOutput = 2;
			LineServer lineServer = new LineServer();
			if (summaryFile != null)
			{
				lineServer.SetString(summaryFile);
			}
			else
			{
				lineServer.SetFile(_filename);
			}
			DateTime now = DateTime.Now;
			if (IdSetMap == null)
			{
				IdSetMap = new IdSets();
			}
			if (LSetMap == null)
			{
				LSetMap = new LSets();
			}
			DsetMap = new DSetSets(this);
			SummarySet summarySet = null;
			foreach (string line in lineServer.Lines)
			{
				if (line.StartsWith(" IDSET") || line.StartsWith("IDSET"))
				{
					ClosePrevious(summarySet);
					summarySet = new IdSet(this, line);
				}
				else if (line.StartsWith(" DSET") || line.StartsWith("DSET"))
				{
					ClosePrevious(summarySet);
					DSet dSet = new DSet(this, line);
					summarySet = dSet;
					_dSets.Add(dSet);
				}
				else if (line.StartsWith(" LSET") || line.StartsWith("LSET"))
				{
					ClosePrevious(summarySet);
					summarySet = new LSet(this, line);
				}
				else
				{
					summarySet?.Append(line);
				}
			}
			summarySet?.Finish();
			foreach (SimilarDSets item in DsetMap)
			{
				item.ProcessSummary();
			}
			Top.IsReadingOutput = 0;
			TimeSpan timeSpan = DateTime.Now - now;
			grandTotal += timeSpan;
			tcount++;
		}

		private void ClosePrevious(SummarySet current)
		{
			current?.Finish();
		}
	}
	public class DSetHeader
	{
		public string pkw { get; set; }

		public string pkwType { get; set; }

		public string path { get; set; }

		public string dsetName { get; set; }

		public string lsetName { get; set; }

		public string DispName => pkw + " " + pkwType + " " + path;

		public bool isSimilar(DSetHeader other)
		{
			bool result = false;
			if (path.Equals(other.path) && isSameType(other))
			{
				result = true;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public bool isSameType(DSetHeader other)
		{
			bool result = false;
			if (pkw.Equals(other.pkw) && pkwType.Equals(other.pkwType))
			{
				result = true;
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public class DSetSets : List<SimilarDSets>
	{
		private AdbSummaryFileReader _reader;

		private static char[] trimQuotes = new char[1] { '"' };

		public DSetSets(AdbSummaryFileReader reader)
		{
			_reader = reader;
		}

		public void AddDSet(DSet newDset)
		{
			newDset.Header.path = newDset.Header.path.Trim(trimQuotes);
			bool flag = false;
			using (Enumerator enumerator = GetEnumerator())
			{
				while (enumerator.MoveNext())
				{
					SimilarDSets current = enumerator.Current;
					if (current.isSimilar(newDset.Header))
					{
						flag = true;
						current.Add(newDset);
					}
				}
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				SimilarDSets similarDSets = new SimilarDSets(_reader);
				similarDSets.Add(newDset);
				Insert(0, similarDSets);
			}
		}
	}
	public class ArgCollab
	{
		private List<SumIdList> _idLists = new List<SumIdList>();

		public SumIdList MergedList;

		public void Add(SumIdList idList)
		{
			_idLists.Add(idList);
		}

		public void Merge()
		{
			SumIdList sumIdList = null;
			bool flag = true;
			bool flag2 = false;
			bool flag3 = false;
			int num = 0;
			foreach (SumIdList idList in _idLists)
			{
				if (flag && sumIdList != null && sumIdList != idList)
				{
					flag = false;
				}
				if (idList.IntArray == -1)
				{
					flag3 = true;
				}
				else
				{
					flag2 = true;
					if (idList.Length > num)
					{
						num = idList.Length;
					}
				}
				sumIdList = idList;
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				MergedList = _idLists.First();
				return;
			}
			if (flag2 && !flag3)
			{
				SumIdList sumIdList2 = new SumIdList();
				sumIdList2.IntArray = num;
				sumIdList2.Collab = _idLists.First().Collab;
				MergedList = sumIdList2;
				return;
			}
			if (flag3 && !flag2)
			{
				SumIdList sumIdList3 = (MergedList = new SumIdList());
				bool flag4 = true;
				{
					foreach (SumIdList idList2 in _idLists)
					{
						int length = idList2.Length;
						idList2.IndexInParent = new int[length];
						idList2.Parent = sumIdList3;
						if (flag4)
						{
							sumIdList3.Collab = idList2.Collab;
							sumIdList3._ids = new List<string>(idList2.Ids);
							for (int i = 0; i < length; i++)
							{
								idList2.IndexInParent[i] = i;
							}
							flag4 = false;
							continue;
						}
						int num2 = 0;
						foreach (string id in idList2.Ids)
						{
							if (id.Equals(sumIdList3._ids[num2]))
							{
								idList2.IndexInParent[num2] = num2;
							}
							else
							{
								int num3 = sumIdList3._ids.IndexOf(id);
								if (num3 >= 0)
								{
									idList2.IndexInParent[num2] = num3;
								}
								else
								{
									idList2.IndexInParent[num2] = sumIdList3.Length;
									sumIdList3.Add(id);
								}
							}
							num2++;
						}
					}
					return;
				}
			}
			throw new Exception("Mixed numeric and nonNumeric Collab");
		}
	}
	public class ArgumentCollabs
	{
		private Dictionary<string, ArgCollab> _collabs = new Dictionary<string, ArgCollab>();

		public int numDsets;

		public SumIdList ParentCollab;

		public void Add(SumIdList newList)
		{
			if (!_collabs.TryGetValue(newList.Collab, out var value))
			{
				value = new ArgCollab();
				_collabs.Add(newList.Collab, value);
			}
			value.Add(newList);
		}

		public void MergeCollabs()
		{
			foreach (ArgCollab value in _collabs.Values)
			{
				value.Merge();
			}
		}

		public ArgumentMatrixData CreateArg(RequestProperty argProp)
		{
			//IL_00a9: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00af: Expected O, but got Unknown
			ArgumentMatrixData val = null;
			int[] array = new int[(argProp.DsetIsArray ? 1 : 0) + argProp.Ids.Count()];
			int num = 0;
			bool flag = false;
			if (argProp.DsetIsArray)
			{
				array[0] = numDsets;
				if (numDsets == 0)
				{
					flag = true;
				}
				num = 1;
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				RequestId[] ids = argProp.Ids;
				foreach (RequestId requestId in ids)
				{
					if (_collabs.TryGetValue(requestId.collabName, out var value))
					{
						array[num] = value.MergedList.Length;
						if (array[num++] == 0)
						{
							flag = true;
						}
					}
					else
					{
						array[num++] = 0;
						flag = true;
					}
				}
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				val = new ArgumentMatrixData(array);
				num = (argProp.DsetIsArray ? 1 : 0);
				RequestId[] ids = argProp.Ids;
				foreach (RequestId requestId2 in ids)
				{
					SumIdList mergedList = _collabs[requestId2.collabName].MergedList;
					int num2 = 0;
					foreach (string id in mergedList.Ids)
					{
						val.SetId(num, num2++, id);
					}
					num++;
				}
			}
			return val;
		}
	}
	public class CollabCollector
	{
		public ArgumentCollabs[] CollabsForArguments;

		private static char[] splitter = new char[1] { '.' };

		public CollabCollector(int numArgs)
		{
			CollabsForArguments = new ArgumentCollabs[numArgs];
		}

		public void Add(int argNum, SumIdList newList)
		{
			if (CollabsForArguments[argNum] == null)
			{
				CollabsForArguments[argNum] = new ArgumentCollabs();
			}
			CollabsForArguments[argNum].Add(newList);
		}

		public void MergeCollabs()
		{
			for (int i = 0; i < CollabsForArguments.Length; i++)
			{
				if (CollabsForArguments[i] != null)
				{
					CollabsForArguments[i].MergeCollabs();
				}
			}
		}

		public ArgumentArrayData CreateArgs(Request req, DSetHeader header)
		{
			//IL_0006: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_000c: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_00c4: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00cb: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_00a8: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00af: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_008c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0093: Expected O, but got Unknown
			ArgumentArrayData val = new ArgumentArrayData(req.NumArgs);
			for (int i = 0; i < req.NumArgs; i++)
			{
				if (req.ArgProps[i] != null && req.ArgProps[i].PropNum < 0)
				{
					int argumentIndex = req.ArgProps[i].ArgumentIndex;
					int propNum = req.ArgProps[i].PropNum;
					IArgumentData argumentData = null;
					switch (propNum)
					{
					case -1:
						if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(header.path))
						{
							argumentData = (IArgumentData)new ArgumentStringData(header.path.Split(splitter, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries).Last());
						}
						break;
					case -2:
						if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(header.pkwType))
						{
							argumentData = (IArgumentData)new ArgumentStringData(header.pkwType);
						}
						break;
					case -3:
						if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(header.pkw))
						{
							argumentData = (IArgumentData)new ArgumentStringData(header.pkw);
						}
						break;
					}
					if (argumentData != null)
					{
						val.SetValue(argumentIndex, argumentData);
					}
				}
				else if (req.ArgProps[i] != null && CollabsForArguments[req.ArgProps[i].ArgumentIndex] != null)
				{
					int argumentIndex2 = req.ArgProps[i].ArgumentIndex;
					IArgumentData argumentData2 = (IArgumentData)CollabsForArguments[argumentIndex2].CreateArg(req.ArgProps[i]);
					val.SetValue(argumentIndex2, argumentData2);
				}
			}
			return val;
		}
	}
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public class SimilarDSets : List<DSet>
	{
		public class DebugPropInfo
		{
			public int propnum;

			public string dsetname;

			public int idcount;

			public int argnum;

			public int valcount;

			public bool Matches(int pnum, string dset, int ids)
			{
				bool result = false;
				if (pnum == propnum && dset.ToLower().StartsWith(dsetname.ToLower()) && (idcount == -1 || ids == idcount))
				{
					result = true;
				}
				return result;
			}
		}

		private AdbSummaryFileReader _reader;

		private CollabCollector _argCollabs;

		private static bool _propinit;

		private static DebugPropInfo _debugProp;

		public static DebugPropInfo DebugProp
		{
			get
			{
				if (!_propinit)
				{
					_propinit = true;
					if (DebugLog.ShouldWrite)
					{
						AdbOption option = AdbOptions.GetOption("dsetdbg");
						if (option != null)
						{
							DebugPropInfo debugPropInfo = new DebugPropInfo();
							string text = option.SubOption("propnum");
							if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text) && int.TryParse(text, out debugPropInfo.propnum))
							{
								debugPropInfo.dsetname = option.SubOption("dset");
								string text2 = option.SubOption("idcount");
								if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text2) && !int.TryParse(text2, out debugPropInfo.idcount))
								{
									debugPropInfo.idcount = -1;
								}
								_debugProp = debugPropInfo;
							}
						}
					}
				}
				return _debugProp;
			}
		}

		public SimilarDSets(AdbSummaryFileReader reader)
		{
			_reader = reader;
		}

		public bool isSimilar(DSetHeader newDset)
		{
			bool result = false;
			if (this.Any())
			{
				result = base[0].Header.isSimilar(newDset);
			}
			return result;
		}

		public bool ProcessSummary()
		{
			bool result = false;
			try
			{
				if (DebugProp != null)
				{
					DebugProp.argnum = -1;
					DebugProp.valcount = 0;
				}
				SupportedType[] supported = SupportedTypes.Supported;
				foreach (SupportedType supportedType in supported)
				{
					if (!supportedType.Match.isSameType(this.First().Header))
					{
						continue;
					}
					AdbDataMover converterByName = supportedType.GetObj(_reader, this.First().Header.path).GetConverterByName("ApCxs", supportedType.ObjectName);
					if (supportedType.StructuredRequest == null)
					{
						string variables = converterByName.GetVariables();
						supportedType.StructuredRequest = new Request(variables);
					}
					Request structuredRequest = supportedType.StructuredRequest;
					_argCollabs = new CollabCollector(structuredRequest.NumArgs);
					DSetToProcessList dSetToProcessList = new DSetToProcessList();
					using (Enumerator enumerator = GetEnumerator())
					{
						while (enumerator.MoveNext())
						{
							DSet current = enumerator.Current;
							List<RequestDset> list = new List<RequestDset>();
							RequestDset value = null;
							if (structuredRequest.FullNames.TryGetValue(current.Header.dsetName, out value))
							{
								list.Add(value);
							}
							foreach (RequestDset partialName in structuredRequest.PartialNames)
							{
								if (current.Header.dsetName.StartsWith(partialName.Name))
								{
									list.Add(partialName);
								}
							}
							foreach (RequestDset item2 in list)
							{
								if (!AdbSummaryFileReader.LSetMap.TryGetValue(current.Header.lsetName, out var value2))
								{
									continue;
								}
								PNode node = value2.Node;
								if (node == null)
								{
									continue;
								}
								DSetToProcess dSetToProcess = new DSetToProcess(current, value2);
								bool flag = false;
								foreach (KeyValuePair<int, PropertyNode> item3 in node.PropertyMap)
								{
									PropertyNode value3 = item3.Value;
									if (!item2.Properties.TryGetValue(item3.Key, out var value4))
									{
										continue;
									}
									List<PNodeIdList> idlists = new List<PNodeIdList>();
									value3.GetIdLists(idlists, item3.Value);
									Tuple<int, RequestProperty> tuple = value4.Find((Tuple<int, RequestProperty> p) => p.Item1 == idlists.Count);
									if (tuple == null)
									{
										continue;
									}
									RequestProperty item = tuple.Item2;
									if (!flag)
									{
										flag = true;
										dSetToProcessList.Add(dSetToProcess);
									}
									dSetToProcess.AddProperty(value3, item);
									foreach (PNodeIdList item4 in idlists)
									{
										_argCollabs.Add(item.ArgumentIndex, item4.IdList);
									}
									if (DebugProp != null && DebugProp.Matches(item.PropNum, current.Header.dsetName, item.Ids.Length))
									{
										DebugProp.argnum = item.ArgumentIndex;
										DebugLog.WriteDebug("PropMatch " + item.PropNum + " Dset " + current.Header.path + " " + current.Header.dsetName + " Ids " + item.Ids.Length + "            ArgNumber: " + item.ArgumentIndex);
									}
									if (idlists.Any())
									{
										_argCollabs.CollabsForArguments[item.ArgumentIndex].numDsets++;
									}
								}
							}
						}
					}
					DebugLog.WriteDebug("   Summary file for " + this.First().Header.DispName + " Dset count: " + dSetToProcessList._toProcess.Count);
					_argCollabs.MergeCollabs();
					ArgumentArrayData val = _argCollabs.CreateArgs(structuredRequest, this.First().Header);
					foreach (DSetToProcess item5 in dSetToProcessList._toProcess)
					{
						PNode node2 = item5.Lset.Node;
						LSetValue current7 = new LSetValue();
						int[] array = new int[0];
						int num = 0;
						int curloc = 0;
						foreach (LSetValue item6 in node2.LSetValues(current7))
						{
							if (item5.Properties.TryGetValue(item6.Property.PropNum, out var value5))
							{
								int num2 = (value5.Value.DsetIsArray ? 1 : 0);
								int num3 = num2 + item6.Ids.Count;
								if (num3 != num)
								{
									num = num3;
									array = new int[num];
								}
								if (num2 == 1)
								{
									array[0] = item5.Index;
								}
								foreach (LSetValueIdSpec id in item6.Ids)
								{
									for (int num4 = 0; num4 < value5.Value.Ids.Length; num4++)
									{
										if (id.Collab.Equals(value5.Value.Ids[num4].collabName))
										{
											array[num2 + num4] = id.LabelIndex;
											break;
										}
									}
								}
								IArgumentData argumentData = item5.Dset.NextValue(ref curloc, value5);
								if (argumentData == null)
								{
									continue;
								}
								if (DebugProp != null && DebugProp.argnum == value5.Value.ArgumentIndex)
								{
									DebugProp.valcount++;
								}
								if (array.Length == 0)
								{
									val.SetValue(value5.Value.ArgumentIndex, argumentData);
									continue;
								}
								IArgumentData argumentData2 = ((ArgumentNonScalarData)val).ValueAt(value5.Value.ArgumentIndex);
								ArgumentMatrixData val2 = (ArgumentMatrixData)((argumentData2 is ArgumentMatrixData) ? argumentData2 : null);
								if (val2 != null)
								{
									val2.SetValue(array, argumentData);
								}
							}
							else
							{
								item5.Dset.SkipValue(ref curloc);
							}
						}
					}
					converterByName.PutDataLocal(val);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				_ = ex.Message;
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
	public class DSetToProcessList
	{
		public List<DSetToProcess> _toProcess = new List<DSetToProcess>();

		private Dictionary<string, int> _indexes = new Dictionary<string, int>();

		public void Add(DSetToProcess dsetProc)
		{
			_toProcess.Add(dsetProc);
			if (!_indexes.TryGetValue(dsetProc.Dset.Header.dsetName, out var value))
			{
				value = -1;
			}
			dsetProc.Index = value + 1;
			_indexes[dsetProc.Dset.Header.dsetName] = value + 1;
		}
	}
	public class DSetToProcess
	{
		public DSet Dset;

		public LSet Lset;

		public int Index;

		public Dictionary<int, KeyValuePair<PropertyNode, RequestProperty>> Properties = new Dictionary<int, KeyValuePair<PropertyNode, RequestProperty>>();

		public DSetToProcess(DSet dset, LSet lset)
		{
			Dset = dset;
			Lset = lset;
		}

		public void AddProperty(PropertyNode propNode, RequestProperty reqProp)
		{
			Properties.Add(propNode.PropNum, new KeyValuePair<PropertyNode, RequestProperty>(propNode, reqProp));
		}
	}
	public class RequestId
	{
		public string collabName { get; set; }

		public int wordInId { get; set; }
	}
	public class RequestProperty
	{
		public int PropNum;

		public bool DsetIsArray;

		public RequestId[] Ids;

		public int ArgumentIndex;
	}
	public class RequestDset
	{
		public string Name;

		public int SubstrLen;

		public Dictionary<int, List<Tuple<int, RequestProperty>>> Properties = new Dictionary<int, List<Tuple<int, RequestProperty>>>();
	}
	public class Request
	{
		public Dictionary<string, RequestDset> FullNames = new Dictionary<string, RequestDset>();

		public List<RequestDset> PartialNames = new List<RequestDset>();

		public RequestProperty[] ArgProps;

		private static char[] pathSplit = new char[1] { '\\' };

		public int NumArgs { get; protected set; }

		public Request(string xmlDesc)
		{
			XElement xElement = XDocument.Parse(xmlDesc).Element("Args");
			int num = 0;
			IEnumerable<XElement> enumerable = xElement.Elements("Arg");
			ArgProps = new RequestProperty[enumerable.Count()];
			foreach (XElement item in enumerable)
			{
				string[] array = item.Attribute("path").Value.Split(pathSplit, StringSplitOptions.None);
				if (array.Length > 3)
				{
					string dsetName = array[1];
					int dNameLen = int.Parse(array[2]);
					RequestDset value;
					if (dNameLen >= 100)
					{
						if (!FullNames.TryGetValue(dsetName, out value))
						{
							value = new RequestDset
							{
								Name = dsetName,
								SubstrLen = 100
							};
							FullNames[dsetName] = value;
						}
					}
					else
					{
						value = PartialNames.Find((RequestDset x) => x.Name == dsetName && x.SubstrLen == dNameLen);
						if (value == null)
						{
							value = new RequestDset
							{
								Name = dsetName,
								SubstrLen = dNameLen
							};
							PartialNames.Add(value);
						}
					}
					RequestProperty requestProperty = new RequestProperty();
					int num2 = (array.Length - 4) / 2;
					requestProperty.PropNum = int.Parse(array[0]);
					requestProperty.DsetIsArray = !string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(array[3]);
					requestProperty.Ids = new RequestId[num2];
					requestProperty.ArgumentIndex = num;
					ArgProps[num] = requestProperty;
					int num3 = 0;
					int num4 = 4;
					while (num3 < num2)
					{
						requestProperty.Ids[num3] = new RequestId
						{
							collabName = array[num4],
							wordInId = (string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(array[num4 + 1]) ? (-1) : int.Parse(array[num4 + 1]))
						};
						num3++;
						num4 += 2;
					}
					if (!value.Properties.TryGetValue(requestProperty.PropNum, out var value2))
					{
						value2 = new List<Tuple<int, RequestProperty>>();
						value.Properties[requestProperty.PropNum] = value2;
					}
					value2.Add(new Tuple<int, RequestProperty>(num2, requestProperty));
				}
				else
				{
					if (array.Length == 1 && "__RECORD_ID".Equals(array[0]))
					{
						ArgProps[num] = new RequestProperty
						{
							PropNum = -1,
							ArgumentIndex = num
						};
					}
					if (array.Length == 1 && "__RECORD_TYPE".Equals(array[0]))
					{
						ArgProps[num] = new RequestProperty
						{
							PropNum = -2,
							ArgumentIndex = num
						};
					}
					if (array.Length == 1 && "__PKWNAME".Equals(array[0]))
					{
						ArgProps[num] = new RequestProperty
						{
							PropNum = -3,
							ArgumentIndex = num
						};
					}
				}
				num++;
			}
			NumArgs = num;
		}
	}
	public class DSet : SummarySet
	{
		private int _firstValue;

		private string[] _tokens;

		private static char[] trimChars = new char[2] { '>', '<' };

		public DSetHeader Header { get; protected set; }

		public DSet(AdbSummaryFileReader rdr, string line)
			: base(rdr, line)
		{
		}

		protected DSetHeader ParseHeader()
		{
			DSetHeader dSetHeader = null;
			string[] array = base.SetAsString.Split(SummarySet.blankSeparator, 8, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries);
			dSetHeader = new DSetHeader();
			if (array.Length >= 8 && array[7].StartsWith("@"))
			{
				dSetHeader.pkw = array[1];
				dSetHeader.pkwType = array[2];
				dSetHeader.path = array[3];
				dSetHeader.dsetName = array[4] + "." + array[5] + "." + array[6];
				dSetHeader.lsetName = array[7].Substring(1);
				_firstValue = 7;
			}
			else if (array.Length >= 7 && array[6].StartsWith("@"))
			{
				dSetHeader.pkw = array[1];
				dSetHeader.pkwType = array[2];
				dSetHeader.path = array[3];
				dSetHeader.dsetName = array[4] + "." + array[5];
				dSetHeader.lsetName = array[6].Substring(1);
				_firstValue = 7;
			}
			else if (array.Length >= 6 && array[5].StartsWith("@"))
			{
				dSetHeader.pkw = array[1];
				dSetHeader.pkwType = array[2];
				dSetHeader.path = array[3];
				dSetHeader.dsetName = array[4];
				dSetHeader.lsetName = array[5].Substring(1);
				_firstValue = 7;
			}
			else if (array.Length >= 5 && array[4].StartsWith("@"))
			{
				dSetHeader.pkw = array[1];
				dSetHeader.pkwType = "";
				dSetHeader.path = array[2];
				dSetHeader.dsetName = array[3];
				dSetHeader.lsetName = array[4].Substring(1);
				_firstValue = 6;
			}
			else if (array.Length >= 4 && array[3].StartsWith("@"))
			{
				dSetHeader.pkw = array[1];
				dSetHeader.pkwType = "";
				dSetHeader.path = "";
				dSetHeader.dsetName = array[2];
				dSetHeader.lsetName = array[3].Substring(1);
				_firstValue = 5;
			}
			else
			{
				if (array.Length < 3 || !array[2].StartsWith("@"))
				{
					throw new Exception("Unknown DSET header format: " + base.SetAsString.Substring(0, 60));
				}
				dSetHeader.pkw = "";
				dSetHeader.pkwType = "";
				dSetHeader.path = "";
				dSetHeader.dsetName = array[1];
				dSetHeader.lsetName = array[2].Substring(1);
				_firstValue = 4;
			}
			return dSetHeader;
		}

		public override void Finish()
		{
			base.Finish();
			Header = ParseHeader();
			if (AdbSummaryFileReader.LSetMap.ContainsKey(Header.lsetName))
			{
				base.Reader.DsetMap.AddDSet(this);
			}
		}

		public void SkipValue(ref int curloc)
		{
			if (_tokens == null)
			{
				_tokens = Tokenizer.MySplit(base.SetAsString).ToArray();
			}
			if (curloc < _firstValue)
			{
				curloc = _firstValue;
			}
			if (curloc < _tokens.Length && !_tokens[curloc].Equals(")"))
			{
				curloc++;
				if (_tokens[curloc].StartsWith("<"))
				{
					curloc++;
				}
			}
		}

		public IArgumentData NextValue(ref int curloc, KeyValuePair<PropertyNode, RequestProperty> property)
		{
			//IL_01ad: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_01b3: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_01e1: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_01e7: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_018d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0193: Expected O, but got Unknown
			IArgumentData result = null;
			if (_tokens == null)
			{
				_tokens = Tokenizer.MySplit(base.SetAsString).ToArray();
			}
			if (curloc < _firstValue)
			{
				curloc = _firstValue;
			}
			if (curloc < _tokens.Length && !_tokens[curloc].Equals(")"))
			{
				if (_tokens[curloc].Equals("*"))
				{
					curloc++;
					if (_tokens[curloc].StartsWith("<") && property.Key.DataType == PropDataType.Double && property.Key.UnitRow > 0)
					{
						curloc++;
					}
				}
				else if (property.Key.DataType == PropDataType.Double)
				{
					string s = _tokens[curloc].Replace('D', 'E');
					curloc++;
					int num = 0;
					if (property.Key.UnitRow > 0 || (_tokens[curloc].StartsWith("<") && _tokens[curloc].EndsWith(">")))
					{
						num = int.Parse(_tokens[curloc].Trim(trimChars));
						curloc++;
					}
					if (double.TryParse(s, NumberStyles.Float, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture, out var result2))
					{
						if (property.Key.UnitRow > 0 && num > 0)
						{
							result2 = base.Reader.Top.ConvertValueToSI(property.Key.UnitRow, result2, num);
						}
						result = (IArgumentData)new ArgumentRealData((double?)result2);
					}
				}
				else if (property.Key.DataType == PropDataType.String)
				{
					result = (IArgumentData)new ArgumentStringData(_tokens[curloc]);
					curloc++;
				}
				else if (property.Key.DataType == PropDataType.Int)
				{
					if (int.TryParse(_tokens[curloc], out var result3))
					{
						result = (IArgumentData)new ArgumentIntData((int?)result3);
					}
					curloc++;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
	public class DSetValue
	{
		public int _unitCol;

		public double? Rval { get; set; }

		public string Sval { get; set; }

		public int? Ival { get; set; }

		public bool Missing { get; set; }
	}
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public class IdSets : Dictionary<string, IdSet>
	{
		public void Add(IdSet idset)
		{
			base[idset.Name] = idset;
		}
	}
	public class IdList
	{
	}
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public class IdSet : SummarySet
	{
		protected bool _triedToParse;

		protected PNode _pNode;

		protected bool _parsed;

		public string Name { get; protected set; }

		protected bool Parsed
		{
			get
			{
				if (!_triedToParse)
				{
					_triedToParse = true;
					ParseIdSet();
				}
				return _parsed;
			}
		}

		public PNode Node
		{
			get
			{
				PNode result = null;
				if (Parsed && _pNode != null)
				{
					result = _pNode.Copy();
				}
				return result;
			}
		}

		public IdSet(AdbSummaryFileReader rdr, string line)
			: base(rdr, line)
		{
		}

		public override void Finish()
		{
			base.Finish();
			Name = FindSimpleHeader();
			AdbSummaryFileReader.IdSetMap.Add(this);
		}

		private PropertyNode ReadProperty(string[] tokens, ref int curloc)
		{
			PropertyNode propertyNode = new PropertyNode();
			propertyNode.PropNum = int.Parse(tokens[curloc]);
			curloc++;
			if (tokens[curloc].StartsWith("*"))
			{
				if (tokens[curloc].Equals("*1"))
				{
					propertyNode.DataType = PropDataType.Int;
				}
				else if (tokens[curloc].Equals("*2"))
				{
					propertyNode.DataType = PropDataType.Double;
				}
				else
				{
					if (!tokens[curloc].Equals("*6") && !tokens[curloc].Equals("*5"))
					{
						throw new Exception("Idset unknown property type");
					}
					propertyNode.DataType = PropDataType.String;
				}
				curloc++;
				if (tokens[curloc].StartsWith("<"))
				{
					propertyNode.UnitRow = int.Parse(tokens[curloc].Substring(1, tokens[curloc].Length - 2));
					curloc++;
				}
				else
				{
					propertyNode.UnitRow = 0;
				}
				return propertyNode;
			}
			throw new Exception("No dataType in Properties IDSET");
		}

		private void ParseIdSet()
		{
			_parsed = false;
			string[] array = Tokenizer.MySplit(base.SetAsString).ToArray();
			if (!array[2].Equals("(") || !array[4].Equals(")") || !array[5].Equals("("))
			{
				PNodeList pNodeList = new PNodeList();
				int i = 4;
				List<string> list = new List<string>();
				list.Add(array[3]);
				for (; array[i].Equals(","); i += 2)
				{
					list.Add(array[i + 1]);
				}
				if (!array[i].Equals(")") || !array[i + 1].Equals("("))
				{
					throw new Exception("Bad IDSET format");
				}
				i += 2;
				while (!array[i].Equals(")"))
				{
					PCrossSet pCrossSet = new PCrossSet();
					foreach (string item in list)
					{
						PNode pNode = null;
						if (item.Equals("PROPERTIES"))
						{
							PropertyNode propertyNode = ReadProperty(array, ref i);
							pNode = propertyNode;
							pCrossSet.PropertyMap.Add(new KeyValuePair<int, PropertyNode>(propertyNode.PropNum, propertyNode));
						}
						else
						{
							SumIdList sumIdList = new SumIdList();
							sumIdList.Collab = item;
							sumIdList.Add(array[i]);
							pNode = new PNodeIdList(sumIdList);
							i++;
						}
						pCrossSet.AddNode(pNode);
						if (array[i].Equals(","))
						{
							i++;
						}
					}
					pNodeList.AddNode(pCrossSet);
				}
				_pNode = pNodeList;
			}
			else if (array[3].Equals("PROPERTIES"))
			{
				PNodeList pNodeList2 = new PNodeList();
				int curloc = 6;
				while (!array[curloc].Equals(")"))
				{
					PropertyNode propertyNode2 = ReadProperty(array, ref curloc);
					propertyNode2.PropertyMap.Add(new KeyValuePair<int, PropertyNode>(propertyNode2.PropNum, propertyNode2));
					pNodeList2.AddNode(propertyNode2);
				}
				_pNode = pNodeList2;
			}
			else
			{
				SumIdList sumIdList2 = new SumIdList();
				sumIdList2.Collab = array[3];
				if (array[7].Equals("?"))
				{
					sumIdList2.Add(array[6]);
					sumIdList2.IsVariable = true;
				}
				else if (array[6].StartsWith("#"))
				{
					sumIdList2.IntArray = int.Parse(array[6].Substring(1));
					if (array.Length != 8)
					{
						throw new Exception("Numeric IDSET with extra stuff");
					}
				}
				else
				{
					for (int j = 6; !array[j].Equals(")"); j++)
					{
						sumIdList2.Add(array[j]);
					}
				}
				PNodeIdList pNode2 = new PNodeIdList(sumIdList2);
				_pNode = pNode2;
			}
			_parsed = true;
		}
	}
	public class SumIdList
	{
		public string Collab;

		public SumIdList Parent;

		public List<string> _ids = new List<string>();

		public bool IsVariable;

		public int[] IndexInParent;

		public int IntArray = -1;

		public IEnumerable<string> Ids
		{
			get
			{
				if (IntArray == -1)
				{
					foreach (string id in _ids)
					{
						yield return id;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					for (int i = 1; i <= IntArray; i++)
					{
						yield return i.ToString();
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public int Length
		{
			get
			{
				int num = 0;
				if (IsVariable)
				{
					throw new Exception("Trying to use variable length IdSet");
				}
				if (IntArray >= 0)
				{
					return IntArray;
				}
				return _ids.Count;
			}
		}

		public int IndexInBase(int localIndex)
		{
			int num = localIndex;
			if (Parent != null)
			{
				num = Parent.IndexInBase(IndexInParent[num]);
			}
			return num;
		}

		public void Add(string id)
		{
			_ids.Add(id);
		}
	}
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public class LSets : Dictionary<string, LSet>
	{
		public void Add(LSet lset)
		{
			base[lset.Name] = lset;
		}
	}
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public class LSet : SummarySet
	{
		protected bool _triedToParse;

		protected PNode _pNode;

		protected bool _parsed;

		public Dictionary<int, PropertyNode> PropertyMap = new Dictionary<int, PropertyNode>();

		public string Name { get; protected set; }

		protected bool Parsed
		{
			get
			{
				if (!_triedToParse)
				{
					_triedToParse = true;
					ParseLSet();
				}
				return _parsed;
			}
		}

		public PNode Node
		{
			get
			{
				PNode result = null;
				if (Parsed)
				{
					result = _pNode;
				}
				return result;
			}
		}

		public IEnumerable<LSetValue> Values
		{
			get
			{
				new LSetValue();
				PNode node = Node;
				while (node != null)
				{
					_ = node.NodeType;
				}
				yield return null;
			}
		}

		public LSet(AdbSummaryFileReader rdr, string line)
			: base(rdr, line)
		{
		}

		public override void Finish()
		{
			base.Finish();
			Name = FindSimpleHeader();
			AdbSummaryFileReader.LSetMap.Add(this);
		}

		protected void ParseLSet()
		{
			_parsed = false;
			string[] tokens = Tokenizer.MySplit(base.SetAsString).ToArray();
			int curloc = 3;
			_pNode = ParseExpression(tokens, ref curloc);
			_parsed = true;
		}

		protected PNode ParseNextNode(string[] tokens, ref int curloc)
		{
			PNode pNode = null;
			if (!tokens[curloc].Equals(")"))
			{
				if (tokens[curloc].Equals("("))
				{
					curloc++;
					pNode = ParseExpression(tokens, ref curloc);
				}
				else
				{
					if (!tokens[curloc].StartsWith("%"))
					{
						throw new Exception("Bad LSET format");
					}
					string key = tokens[curloc].Substring(1);
					if (!AdbSummaryFileReader.IdSetMap.TryGetValue(key, out var value))
					{
						throw new Exception("Unrecognized IDSET name");
					}
					pNode = value.Node;
					curloc++;
					if (pNode == null)
					{
						throw new Exception("Cannot parse IDSET");
					}
				}
			}
			return pNode;
		}

		protected PNode ParseExpression(string[] tokens, ref int curloc)
		{
			PNode pNode = null;
			PNodeList pNodeList = null;
			PCrossSet pCrossSet = null;
			PNode pNode2 = ParseNextNode(tokens, ref curloc);
			while (!tokens[curloc].Equals(")"))
			{
				if (tokens[curloc].Equals("&"))
				{
					curloc++;
					if (pCrossSet == null)
					{
						pCrossSet = new PCrossSet();
						pCrossSet.AddNode(pNode2);
						pNode2 = pCrossSet;
					}
					PNode pNode3 = ParseNextNode(tokens, ref curloc);
					if (pNode3 == null)
					{
						throw new Exception("Bad LSET crossing format");
					}
					if (pNodeList != null && !tokens[curloc].Equals("&"))
					{
						pNodeList.AddNode(pNode2);
					}
					pCrossSet.AddNode(pNode3);
				}
				else
				{
					if (pCrossSet != null)
					{
						pCrossSet = null;
					}
					if (pNodeList == null)
					{
						pNodeList = new PNodeList();
						pNodeList.AddNode(pNode2);
					}
					pNode2 = ParseNextNode(tokens, ref curloc);
					if (!tokens[curloc].Equals("&"))
					{
						pNodeList.AddNode(pNode2);
					}
				}
			}
			curloc++;
			if (pNodeList != null)
			{
				return pNodeList;
			}
			return pNode2;
		}
	}
	public class LSetValueIdSpec
	{
		public string Collab { get; set; }

		public int LabelIndex { get; set; }
	}
	public class LSetValue
	{
		public Stack<LSetValueIdSpec> Ids { get; set; }

		public PropertyNode Property { get; set; }

		public LSetValue()
		{
			Ids = new Stack<LSetValueIdSpec>();
		}
	}
	public enum PNodeType
	{
		IdList,
		Property,
		NodeList,
		CrossSet
	}
	public abstract class PNode
	{
		public PNodeType NodeType;

		public PNode CrossParent;

		public PNode ListParent;

		public int Location;

		public List<KeyValuePair<int, PropertyNode>> PropertyMap = new List<KeyValuePair<int, PropertyNode>>();

		public abstract int SizeInDset { get; }

		public virtual void GetIdLists(List<PNodeIdList> lists, PNode requester)
		{
			if (CrossParent != null)
			{
				CrossParent.GetIdLists(lists, this);
			}
			if (ListParent != null)
			{
				ListParent.GetIdLists(lists, this);
			}
		}

		public abstract PNode Copy();

		public abstract IEnumerable<LSetValue> LSetValues(LSetValue current);
	}
	public class PNodeIdList : PNode
	{
		private int _sizeInDset;

		public SumIdList IdList;

		public override int SizeInDset => _sizeInDset;

		public PNodeIdList(SumIdList list)
		{
			IdList = list;
			NodeType = PNodeType.IdList;
			_sizeInDset = list.Length;
		}

		public override void GetIdLists(List<PNodeIdList> lists, PNode requester)
		{
			lists.Add(this);
		}

		public override PNode Copy()
		{
			return new PNodeIdList(IdList);
		}

		public override IEnumerable<LSetValue> LSetValues(LSetValue current)
		{
			current.Ids.Peek().Collab = IdList.Collab;
			for (int i = 0; i < IdList.Length; i++)
			{
				current.Ids.Peek().LabelIndex = IdList.IndexInBase(i);
				yield return current;
			}
		}
	}
	public enum PropDataType
	{
		Int,
		Double,
		String
	}
	public class PropertyNode : PNode
	{
		public int PropNum;

		public PropDataType DataType;

		public int UnitRow;

		public override int SizeInDset => 1;

		public PropertyNode()
		{
			NodeType = PNodeType.Property;
		}

		public override PNode Copy()
		{
			PropertyNode propertyNode = new PropertyNode();
			propertyNode.PropNum = PropNum;
			propertyNode.DataType = DataType;
			propertyNode.UnitRow = UnitRow;
			propertyNode.PropertyMap.Add(new KeyValuePair<int, PropertyNode>(PropNum, propertyNode));
			return propertyNode;
		}

		public override IEnumerable<LSetValue> LSetValues(LSetValue current)
		{
			current.Property = this;
			yield return current;
		}
	}
	public class PNodeList : PNode
	{
		public List<PNode> Nodes = new List<PNode>();

		public override int SizeInDset
		{
			get
			{
				int num = 0;
				foreach (PNode node in Nodes)
				{
					num += node.SizeInDset;
				}
				return num;
			}
		}

		public PNodeList()
		{
			NodeType = PNodeType.NodeList;
		}

		public void AddNode(PNode newNode)
		{
			newNode.ListParent = this;
			newNode.Location = Nodes.Count;
			Nodes.Add(newNode);
			foreach (KeyValuePair<int, PropertyNode> item in newNode.PropertyMap)
			{
				PropertyMap.Add(item);
			}
		}

		public override PNode Copy()
		{
			PNodeList pNodeList = new PNodeList();
			foreach (PNode node in Nodes)
			{
				pNodeList.AddNode(node.Copy());
			}
			return pNodeList;
		}

		public override IEnumerable<LSetValue> LSetValues(LSetValue current)
		{
			foreach (PNode node in Nodes)
			{
				foreach (LSetValue item in node.LSetValues(current))
				{
					yield return item;
				}
			}
		}
	}
	public class PCrossSet : PNode
	{
		public List<PNode> Nodes = new List<PNode>();

		public override int SizeInDset
		{
			get
			{
				int num = 1;
				foreach (PNode node in Nodes)
				{
					num *= node.SizeInDset;
				}
				return num;
			}
		}

		public PCrossSet()
		{
			NodeType = PNodeType.CrossSet;
		}

		public void AddNode(PNode newNode)
		{
			newNode.CrossParent = this;
			newNode.Location = Nodes.Count;
			Nodes.Add(newNode);
			foreach (KeyValuePair<int, PropertyNode> item in newNode.PropertyMap)
			{
				PropertyMap.Add(item);
			}
		}

		public override void GetIdLists(List<PNodeIdList> lists, PNode requester)
		{
			base.GetIdLists(lists, requester);
			foreach (PNode node in Nodes)
			{
				if (node != requester)
				{
					node.GetIdLists(lists, this);
				}
			}
		}

		public override PNode Copy()
		{
			PCrossSet pCrossSet = new PCrossSet();
			foreach (PNode node in Nodes)
			{
				pCrossSet.AddNode(node.Copy());
			}
			return pCrossSet;
		}

		protected IEnumerable<LSetValue> LSetValuesAtOneLevel(LSetValue current, int level)
		{
			if (level >= 0)
			{
				PNode child = Nodes[level];
				if (child.NodeType == PNodeType.IdList)
				{
					current.Ids.Push(new LSetValueIdSpec());
				}
				foreach (LSetValue item in child.LSetValues(current))
				{
					_ = item;
					foreach (LSetValue item2 in LSetValuesAtOneLevel(current, level - 1))
					{
						yield return item2;
					}
				}
				if (child.NodeType == PNodeType.IdList)
				{
					current.Ids.Pop();
				}
			}
			else
			{
				yield return current;
			}
		}

		public override IEnumerable<LSetValue> LSetValues(LSetValue current)
		{
			foreach (LSetValue item in LSetValuesAtOneLevel(current, Nodes.Count - 1))
			{
				yield return item;
			}
		}
	}
	public class SummarySet
	{
		private StringBuilder _buildingString;

		public static char[] blankSeparator = new char[1] { ' ' };

		public string SetAsString { get; protected set; }

		public AdbSummaryFileReader Reader { get; protected set; }

		public SummarySet(AdbSummaryFileReader rdr, string line1)
		{
			Reader = rdr;
			_buildingString = new StringBuilder(line1);
		}

		public void Append(string line)
		{
			if (_buildingString != null)
			{
				_buildingString.Append(" ");
				_buildingString.Append(line);
			}
		}

		public virtual void Finish()
		{
			SetAsString = _buildingString.ToString();
			_buildingString = null;
		}

		protected string FindSimpleHeader()
		{
			return SetAsString.Split(blankSeparator, 3, StringSplitOptions.RemoveEmptyEntries)[1];
		}
	}
	public delegate AdbBase FindAdsObject(AdbSummaryFileReader reader, string path);
	public class SupportedType
	{
		public DSetHeader Match { get; set; }

		public FindAdsObject GetObj { get; set; }

		public string ObjectName { get; set; }

		public Request StructuredRequest { get; set; }
	}
	internal class SupportedTypes
	{
		public static SupportedType[] Supported = new SupportedType[12]
		{
			new SupportedType
			{
				Match = new DSetHeader
				{
					pkw = "BLOCK",
					pkwType = "RADFRAC"
				},
				GetObj = FindBlock,
				ObjectName = "BLOCK_RADFRAC"
			},
			new SupportedType
			{
				Match = new DSetHeader
				{
					pkw = "UTILITY",
					pkwType = ""
				},
				GetObj = FindUtility,
				ObjectName = "UTILITY"
			},
			new SupportedType
			{
				Match = new DSetHeader
				{
					pkw = "UTILITY",
					pkwType = ""
				},
				GetObj = FindFlowsheet,
				ObjectName = "UTILITY"
			},
			new SupportedType
			{
				Match = new DSetHeader
				{
					pkw = "BLOCK",
					pkwType = "COMPR"
				},
				GetObj = FindBlock,
				ObjectName = "BLOCK_COMPR"
			},
			new SupportedType
			{
				Match = new DSetHeader
				{
					pkw = "BLOCK",
					pkwType = "MCOMPR"
				},
				GetObj = FindBlock,
				ObjectName = "BLOCK_MCOMPR"
			},
			new SupportedType
			{
				Match = new DSetHeader
				{
					pkw = "BLOCK",
					pkwType = "PUMP"
				},
				GetObj = FindBlock,
				ObjectName = "BLOCK_PUMP"
			},
			new SupportedType
			{
				Match = new DSetHeader
				{
					pkw = "BLOCK",
					pkwType = "HEATX"
				},
				GetObj = FindBlock,
				ObjectName = "BLOCK_HEATX"
			},
			new SupportedType
			{
				Match = new DSetHeader
				{
					pkw = "BLOCK",
					pkwType = "PETROFRAC"
				},
				GetObj = FindBlock,
				ObjectName = "BLOCK_PETROFRAC"
			},
			new SupportedType
			{
				Match = new DSetHeader
				{
					pkw = "SENSITIVITY",
					pkwType = ""
				},
				GetObj = FindSensitivity,
				ObjectName = "SENSITIVITY"
			},
			new SupportedType
			{
				Match = new DSetHeader
				{
					pkw = "BLOCK",
					pkwType = "VALVE"
				},
				GetObj = FindBlock,
				ObjectName = "BLOCK_VALVE"
			},

			{

				{
					pkw = "BLOCK",
					pkwType = "FLASH2"
				},

				ObjectName = "BLOCK_FLASH2"

		private static AdbBase FindBlock(AdbSummaryFileReader reader, string path)
		{
			return reader.Top.TopRecord().ScalarReference("149049BB-DB00-453C-9F0A-CE5F829DFA6A").Execute("2E57A94E-2E85-45CC-AFC7-5CEF4B87AC24", new object[1] { path }) as AdbBase;
		}

		private static AdbBase FindFlowsheet(AdbSummaryFileReader reader, string path)
		{
			return reader.Top.TopRecord().ScalarReference("149049BB-DB00-453C-9F0A-CE5F829DFA6A") as AdbBase;
		}

		private static AdbBase FindUtility(AdbSummaryFileReader reader, string path)
		{
			IAdbRecord adbRecord = reader.Top.TopRecord();
			IAdbRecord adbRecord2 = adbRecord.Reference(new object[2] { "33e94165-5c8b-440a-ba1c-a454c994b8ae", path });
			if (adbRecord2 == null)
			{
				adbRecord2 = reader.Top.CreateRecord("257c8c40-c2b9-4637-b569-06ae41819899");
				adbRecord.ExecutePath(new object[2] { "33e94165-5c8b-440a-ba1c-a454c994b8ae", "3F9CB685-35D7-4328-B775-09BD12A7A1AE" }, new object[2] { path, adbRecord2 });
			}
			return adbRecord2 as AdbBase;
		}

		private static AdbBase FindSensitivity(AdbSummaryFileReader reader, string path)
		{
			return reader.Top.TopRecord().ScalarReference("149049BB-DB00-453C-9F0A-CE5F829DFA6A").Execute("0A576EED-2ABC-42FB-BEBB-C20C1EE53849", new object[1] { path }) as AdbBase;
		}
	}
	internal class Tokenizer
	{
		private static char[] quotes = new char[1] { '"' };

		private static char[] delims = new char[4] { ' ', '(', ')', ',' };

		public static List<string> MySplit(string inputString)
		{
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			int num = -1;
			int num2 = -1;
			for (int i = 0; i < inputString.Length; i++)
			{
				if (inputString[i] == '"')
				{
					if (num == -1)
					{
						num = i;
						if (num2 != -1)
						{
							list.Add(inputString.Substring(num2, i - num2));
							num2 = -1;
						}
					}
					else
					{
						list.Add(inputString.Substring(num + 1, i - num - 1));
						num = -1;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					if (num != -1)
					{
						continue;
					}
					if (delims.Contains(inputString[i]))
					{
						if (num2 != -1)
						{
							list.Add(inputString.Substring(num2, i - num2));
							num2 = -1;
						}
						if (inputString[i] != ' ')
						{
							list.Add(inputString[i].ToString());
						}
					}
					else if (num2 == -1)
					{
						num2 = i;
					}
				}
			}
			if (num2 != -1)
			{
				list.Add(inputString.Substring(num2));
			}
			return list;
		}
	}

	public class AdbCppWriter
	{
		protected class TypePair
		{
			public string type1 { get; set; }

			public string type2 { get; set; }

			public TypePair(string t1, string t2)
			{
				type1 = t1;
				type2 = t2;
			}
		}

		private SortedSet<Type> _generatedTypes = new SortedSet<Type>();

		public void Generate(string[] dllNames, StringBuilder hfile, StringBuilder cppfile, StringBuilder incfile, StringBuilder chfile, StringBuilder cfile, Guid topRecordGuid, StringBuilder fincfile, StringBuilder ffile, StringBuilder f64file, StringBuilder f64incfile)
		{
			HashSet<string> hashSet = new HashSet<string>();
			HashSet<TypePair> hashSet2 = new HashSet<TypePair>();
			List<Assembly> list = new List<Assembly>();
			list.Add(Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly());
			for (int i = 0; i < dllNames.Length; i++)
			{
				Assembly assembly = Assembly.LoadFrom(dllNames[i]);
				if (assembly != null)
				{
					list.Add(assembly);
				}
			}
			string acpHTemplate = Resources.AcpHTemplate;
			string arg = DateTime.Now.ToString();
			hfile.AppendFormat("//   Autogenerated {0}\r\n\r\n", arg);
			hfile.Append(acpHTemplate);
			string acpCPPTemplate = Resources.AcpCPPTemplate;
			cppfile.AppendFormat("//   Autogenerated {0}\r\n\r\n", arg);
			cppfile.Append(acpCPPTemplate);
			string achTemplate = Resources.AchTemplate;
			chfile.AppendFormat("//   Autogenerated {0}\r\n\r\n", arg);
			chfile.Append(achTemplate);
			string acTemplate = Resources.AcTemplate;
			cfile.AppendFormat("//   Autogenerated {0}\r\n\r\n", arg);
			cfile.Append(acTemplate);
			incfile.AppendFormat("#if 0\r\n//   Autogenerated {0}\r\n#endif\r\n\r\n", arg);
			string afTemplate = Resources.AfTemplate;
			ffile.AppendFormat("//    Autogenerated {0}\r\n\r\n", arg);
			ffile.Append(afTemplate);
			string af64Template = Resources.Af64Template;
			f64file.AppendFormat("//    Autogenerated {0}\r\n\r\n", arg);
			f64file.Append(af64Template);
			string afIncTemplate = Resources.AfIncTemplate;
			fincfile.AppendFormat("C    Autogenerated {0}\r\n\r\n", arg);
			fincfile.Append(afIncTemplate);
			string af64IncTemplate = Resources.Af64IncTemplate;
			f64incfile.AppendFormat("C    Autogenerated {0}\r\n\r\n", arg);
			f64incfile.Append(af64IncTemplate);
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			StringBuilder stringBuilder2 = new StringBuilder();
			foreach (Assembly item in list)
			{
				List<Type> types = (from t in item.GetTypes()
					where t.IsSubclassOf(typeof(AdbBaseRecord))
					select t).ToList();
				types = OrderForCppInheritance(types, typeof(AdbBaseRecord).FullName);
				foreach (Type item2 in types)
				{
					stringBuilder.AppendFormat("class {0};\r\n", CppName(item2.Name));
					GenerateCpp(item2, cppfile, stringBuilder2, incfile, chfile, cfile, fincfile, ffile, f64file, f64incfile, hashSet, hashSet2, topRecordGuid);
				}
			}
			hfile.Append(stringBuilder.ToString());
			hfile.Append("\r\n\r\n");
			hfile.Append(stringBuilder2.ToString());
			cppfile.Append("\r\n\r\n");
			foreach (string item3 in hashSet)
			{
				cppfile.AppendFormat("    template class AcpReference<{0}>;\r\n", item3);
			}
			cppfile.Append("\r\n\r\n");
			foreach (TypePair item4 in hashSet2)
			{
				cppfile.AppendFormat("    template class AcpArray<{0},{1}>;\r\n", item4.type1, item4.type2);
			}
			chfile.AppendFormat("\r\n\r\n#endif");
		}

		protected List<Type> OrderForCppInheritance(List<Type> types, string baseRecord)
		{
			Dictionary<string, Type> dictionary = types.ToDictionary((Type t) => t.FullName, (Type t) => t.BaseType);
			dictionary[baseRecord] = null;
			List<Type> list = new List<Type>();
			foreach (Type type in types)
			{
				_orderForCppInheritance(type, dictionary, list);
			}
			return list;
		}

		private void _orderForCppInheritance(Type currentType, Dictionary<string, Type> parents, List<Type> ret)
		{
			if (parents.TryGetValue(currentType.FullName, out var value) && !(value == null))
			{
				_orderForCppInheritance(parents[currentType.FullName], parents, ret);
				ret.Add(currentType);
				parents[currentType.FullName] = null;
			}
		}

		public static string CppName(string AdbName)
		{
			string text = "";
			if (AdbName.StartsWith("Adb"))
			{
				return "Acp" + AdbName.Remove(0, 3);
			}
			if (string.Compare(AdbName, "double", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
			{
				return "AcpReal";
			}
			if (string.Compare(AdbName, "int32", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
			{
				return "AcpInteger";
			}
			if (string.Compare(AdbName, "boolean", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
			{
				return "AcpBool";
			}
			return "Acp" + AdbName;
		}

		public static string CName(string AdbName)
		{
			return CppName(AdbName).Substring(3);
		}

		public static string CppTypeName(Type adbType)
		{
			string name = adbType.Name;
			string result = null;
			if (string.Compare(name, "Void", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
			{
				result = "void";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(name, "Double", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
			{
				result = "double";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(name, "Int", ignoreCase: true) == 0 || string.Compare(name, "Int32", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
			{
				result = "int";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(name, "bool", ignoreCase: true) == 0 || string.Compare(name, "boolean", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
			{
				result = "bool";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(name, "string", ignoreCase: true) == 0 || string.Compare(name, "String", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
			{
				result = "AString";
			}
			else if (name.StartsWith("Adb"))
			{
				result = "Acp" + name.Remove(0, 3);
			}
			else if (string.Compare(name, "object", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
			{
				result = "VARIANT";
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static string FortranDeclareTypeName(Type adbType, bool is64)
		{
			string name = adbType.Name;
			string result = name;
			if (string.Compare(name, "Void", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
			{
				result = null;
			}
			else if (string.Compare(name, "Double", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
			{
				result = "REAL*8";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(name, "Int", ignoreCase: true) == 0 || string.Compare(name, "Int32", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
			{
				result = (is64 ? "INTEGER*8" : "INTEGER");
			}
			else if (string.Compare(name, "bool", ignoreCase: true) == 0 || string.Compare(name, "boolean", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
			{
				result = "LOGICAL";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(name, "string", ignoreCase: true) == 0 || string.Compare(name, "String", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
			{
				result = "! CHARACTER*(caller must declare with length the same as return value) ";
			}
			else if (name.StartsWith("Adb"))
			{
				result = ((!is64) ? "INTEGER" : "INTEGER*8");
			}
			else if (string.Compare(name, "object", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
			{
				result = null;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static string CppTypePrefix(Type adbType)
		{
			string name = adbType.Name;
			string result = name;
			if (string.Compare(name, "Void", ignoreCase: true) == 0 || string.Compare(name, "AdbNoId") == 0)
			{
				result = " ";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(name, "Double", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
			{
				result = "r";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(name, "Int", ignoreCase: true) == 0 || string.Compare(name, "Int32", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
			{
				result = "i";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(name, "bool", ignoreCase: true) == 0 || string.Compare(name, "boolean", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
			{
				result = "b";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(name, "string", ignoreCase: true) == 0 || string.Compare(name, "String", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
			{
				result = "c";
			}
			else if (name.StartsWith("Adb"))
			{
				result = "f";
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static string CppVariantType(Type argType, out string variantUnionElement)
		{
			variantUnionElement = null;
			string result = "VARIANT";
			if (argType == typeof(double) || argType == typeof(double))
			{
				result = "VT_R8";
				variantUnionElement = "dblVal";
			}
			else if (argType == typeof(string) || argType == typeof(string))
			{
				result = "VT_BSTR";
				variantUnionElement = "bstrVal";
			}
			else if (argType == typeof(int))
			{
				result = "VT_I4";
				variantUnionElement = "lVal";
			}
			else if (argType == typeof(bool) || argType == typeof(bool))
			{
				result = "VT_BOOL";
				variantUnionElement = "boolVal";
			}
			else if (argType.IsSubclassOf(typeof(AdbBaseRecord)))
			{
				result = "VT_DISPATCH";
				variantUnionElement = "pdispVal";
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected void GenerateCpp(Type T, StringBuilder cppfile, StringBuilder hfile, StringBuilder incfile, StringBuilder chfile, StringBuilder cfile, StringBuilder fincfile, StringBuilder ffile, StringBuilder f64file, StringBuilder f64incfile, HashSet<string> explicitReferences, HashSet<TypePair> explicitArrays, Guid topRecordGuid)
		{
			string text = CppName(T.BaseType.Name);
			string text2 = CppName(T.Name);
			object[] customAttributes = T.GetCustomAttributes(typeof(GuidAttribute), inherit: false);
			if (customAttributes.Length != 1)
			{
				throw new AdbException(T, "Class " + T.Name + " does not have exactly one Guid Attribute");
			}
			GuidAttribute guidAttribute = (GuidAttribute)customAttributes[0];
			Guid guid = Guid.Parse(guidAttribute.Value);
			incfile.AppendFormat("\r\n#define CLS_{0} \"{1}\"", text2, guid);
			hfile.AppendFormat("class ACPPEXPORT {0} : public {1}\r\n", text2, text);
			hfile.AppendFormat("{{\r\n\ttemplate <class T> friend class AcpReference;\r\n");
			hfile.AppendFormat("    template <class ID, class VALUE> friend class AcpArray;\r\n");
			hfile.AppendFormat("    friend class AcpBaseRecord;\r\n");
			hfile.AppendFormat("\r\npublic:\r\n");
