					}
				}
				else
				{
					for (int l = 0; l < parameters.Length; l++)
					{
						cppfile.AppendFormat("        AcpBaseRecord::MyVariantClear(args + {0});\r\n", l);
					}
					if (flag)
					{
						cfile.AppendFormat("\r\n    }}\r\n}}");
						foreach (string item5 in list)
						{
							ffile.AppendFormat("\r\n      delete[] c_{0};", item5);
							f64file.AppendFormat("\r\n      delete[] c_{0};", item5);
						}
						ffile.AppendFormat("\r\n    }}\r\n}}");
						f64file.AppendFormat("\r\n    }}\r\n}}");
					}
				}
				cppfile.AppendFormat("    }}\r\n");
			}
			hfile.AppendFormat("}};\r\n");
		}
	}
	public class AdbCsWriter
	{
		private SortedSet<Type> _generatedTypes = new SortedSet<Type>();

		public void Generate(string[] dllNames, StringBuilder csfile, Guid topRecordGuid)
		{
			List<Assembly> list = new List<Assembly>();
			list.Add(Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly());
			for (int i = 0; i < dllNames.Length; i++)
			{
				Assembly assembly = Assembly.LoadFrom(dllNames[i]);
				if (assembly != null)
				{
					list.Add(assembly);
				}
			}
			string acsTemplate = Resources.AcsTemplate;
			string arg = DateTime.Now.ToString();
			csfile.AppendFormat("//   Autogenerated {0}\r\n\r\n", arg);
			csfile.Append(acsTemplate);
			foreach (Assembly item in list)
			{
				Type[] types = item.GetTypes();
				foreach (Type type in types)
				{
					if (type.IsSubclassOf(typeof(AdbBaseRecord)))
					{
						GenerateCs(type, csfile, topRecordGuid);
					}
				}
			}
			csfile.Append("\r\n}");
		}

		public static string CsName(string AdbName)
		{
			string text = "";
			if (AdbName.StartsWith("Adb"))
			{
				return "Acs" + AdbName.Remove(0, 3);
			}
			if (string.Compare(AdbName, "double", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
			{
				return "double";
			}
			if (string.Compare(AdbName, "int32", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
			{
				return "int";
			}
			if (string.Compare(AdbName, "boolean", ignoreCase: true) == 0 || string.Compare(AdbName, "bool", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
			{
				return "boolean";
			}
			if (string.Compare(AdbName, "string", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
			{
				return "string";
			}
			return "Acp" + AdbName;
		}

		public static string CsTypeName(Type adbType, out bool isReference)
		{
			string name = adbType.Name;
			string text = name;
			isReference = false;
			if (adbType == typeof(double?))
			{
				text = "double?";
			}
			else if (adbType == typeof(int?))
			{
				text = "int?";
			}
			else if (adbType == typeof(bool?))
			{
				text = "bool?";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(name, "Int", ignoreCase: true) == 0 || string.Compare(name, "Int32", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
			{
				text = "int";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(name, "bool", ignoreCase: true) == 0 || string.Compare(name, "boolean", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
			{
				text = "bool";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(name, "string", ignoreCase: true) == 0 || string.Compare(name, "String", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
			{
				text = "string";
			}
			else if (string.Compare(name, "void", ignoreCase: true) == 0)
			{
				text = "void";
			}
			else if (name.StartsWith("Adb"))
			{
				text = "Acs" + name.Remove(0, 3);
				if (!name.Equals("AdbNoId"))
				{
					isReference = true;
				}
			}
			if (text.Equals("DataTable"))
			{
				text = "System.Data.DataTable";
			}
			return text;
		}

		public void GenerateCs(Type T, StringBuilder csfile, Guid topRecordGuid)
		{
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			StringBuilder stringBuilder2 = new StringBuilder();
			StringBuilder stringBuilder3 = new StringBuilder();
			string arg = CsName(T.BaseType.Name);
			string text = CsName(T.Name);
			object[] customAttributes = T.GetCustomAttributes(typeof(GuidAttribute), inherit: false);
			if (customAttributes.Length != 1)
			{
				throw new AdbException(T, "Class " + T.Name + " does not have exactly one Guid Attribute");
			}
			GuidAttribute guidAttribute = (GuidAttribute)customAttributes[0];
			stringBuilder.AppendFormat("\r\n\r\n    public static partial class AdbTopEnums\r\n    {{");
			stringBuilder.AppendFormat("\r\n        public static IEnumerable<{0}> {1}s(this IAdbTop top)", text, text);
			stringBuilder.AppendFormat("\r\n        {{\r\n            return new AcsEnum<{0}>() {{ top = top, guidString = \"{1}\" }};\r\n        }}\r\n    }}", text, guidAttribute.Value);
			stringBuilder.AppendFormat("\r\n\r\n    public {0}class {1} : {2}\r\n    {{", text.Equals("AcsBase") ? "partial " : "", text, arg);
			stringBuilder.AppendFormat("\r\n        public static {0}string myGuid = \"{1}\";", text.Equals("AcsBase") ? "" : "new ", guidAttribute.Value);
			stringBuilder2.AppendFormat("\r\n\r\n        public {0}()", text);
			stringBuilder2.Append("\r\n        {\r\n            InitializeArrays();\r\n        }\r\n");
			stringBuilder2.AppendFormat("\r\n\r\n        public {0}(IAdbRecord record)", text);
			stringBuilder2.Append("\r\n        {\r\n            Record = record;\r\n            InitializeArrays();\r\n        }\r\n");
			stringBuilder2.AppendFormat("\r\n        public {0}(IAdbTop top)", text);
			if (Guid.Parse(guidAttribute.Value).Equals(topRecordGuid))
			{
				stringBuilder2.Append("\r\n        {\r\n            Top = top;\r\n            Record = top.TopRecord();\r\n            InitializeArrays();\r\n        }\r\n");
			}
			else
			{
				stringBuilder2.Append("\r\n        {\r\n            Top = top;\r\n            Record = top.CreateRecord(myGuid);\r\n            InitializeArrays();\r\n        }\r\n");
			}
			if (!text.Equals("AcsBase"))
			{
				stringBuilder2.Append("\r\n        public override string ClassGuid { get { return myGuid; } }\r\n");
			}
			stringBuilder2.Append("\r\n        protected new void InitializeArrays()\r\n        {\r\n            base.InitializeArrays();");
			FieldInfo[] fields = T.GetFields(BindingFlags.DeclaredOnly | BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.Public);
			foreach (FieldInfo fieldInfo in fields)
			{
				object[] customAttributes2 = fieldInfo.GetCustomAttributes(typeof(AdbFieldIdent), inherit: false);
				if (customAttributes2.Length != 1)
				{
					continue;
				}
				object obj = customAttributes2[0];
				stringBuilder.AppendFormat("\r\n        protected static string {0}Guid = \"{1}\";", fieldInfo.Name, ((AdbFieldIdent)obj).Id.ToString());
				if (fieldInfo.FieldType.IsGenericType && fieldInfo.FieldType.GetGenericTypeDefinition() != typeof(Nullable<>))
				{
					Type[] genericArguments = fieldInfo.FieldType.GetGenericArguments();
					bool isReference;
					string text2 = CsTypeName(genericArguments[0], out isReference);
					bool isReference2;
					string text3 = CsTypeName(genericArguments[1], out isReference2);
					string text4 = "ValueArray";
					if (isReference && !isReference2)
					{
						text4 = "IDRfArray";
					}
					else if (!isReference && isReference2)
					{
						text4 = "ReferenceArray";
					}
					else if (isReference && isReference2)
					{
						text4 = "IDRfReferenceArray";
					}
					stringBuilder2.AppendFormat("\r\n            {0} = new {1}<{2}, {3}>() {{ Guid = {4}Guid, Container = this }};", fieldInfo.Name, text4, text2, text3, fieldInfo.Name);
					stringBuilder3.AppendFormat("\r\n\r\n        public {0}<{1}, {2}> {3};", text4, text2, text3, fieldInfo.Name);
					continue;
				}
				bool isReference3;
				string text5 = CsTypeName(fieldInfo.FieldType, out isReference3);
				if (isReference3)
				{
					if (text5.Equals("AcsIOReal"))
					{
						stringBuilder3.AppendFormat("\r\n\r\n        public {0} {1}\r\n        {{\r\n            get\r\n            {{", text5, fieldInfo.Name);
						stringBuilder3.AppendFormat("\r\n                return new {0}() {{ Top = Top, Record = Record, _guid={1}Guid }};\r\n            }}\r\n        }}", text5, fieldInfo.Name);
						continue;
					}
					stringBuilder3.AppendFormat("\r\n\r\n        public {0} {1}\r\n        {{\r\n            get\r\n            {{", text5, fieldInfo.Name);
					stringBuilder3.AppendFormat("\r\n                return new {0}() {{ Top = Top, Record = Record.ScalarReference({1}Guid) }};", text5, fieldInfo.Name);
					stringBuilder3.Append("\r\n            }\r\n            set\r\n            {");
					stringBuilder3.AppendFormat("\r\n                Record.SetScalarValue({0}Guid, value.Record);\r\n            }}\r\n        }}", fieldInfo.Name);
				}
				else
				{
					stringBuilder3.AppendFormat("\r\n\r\n        public {0} {1}\r\n        {{\r\n            get\r\n            {{", text5, fieldInfo.Name);
					stringBuilder3.Append("\r\n                string units;");
					stringBuilder3.AppendFormat("\r\n                object x = Record.ScalarValue({0}Guid, out units);", fieldInfo.Name);
					stringBuilder3.AppendFormat("\r\n                return ({0})x;", text5);
					stringBuilder3.Append("\r\n            }\r\n            set\r\n            {");
					stringBuilder3.AppendFormat("\r\n                Record.SetScalarValue({0}Guid, value);\r\n            }}\r\n        }}", fieldInfo.Name);
				}
			}
			MethodInfo[] methods = T.GetMethods(BindingFlags.DeclaredOnly | BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.Public);
			foreach (MethodInfo methodInfo in methods)
			{
				object[] customAttributes3 = methodInfo.GetCustomAttributes(typeof(AdbMethodIdent), inherit: false);
				if (customAttributes3.Length != 1)
				{
					continue;
				}
				object obj2 = customAttributes3[0];
				bool isReference4;
				string text6 = CsTypeName(methodInfo.ReturnType, out isReference4);
				stringBuilder3.AppendFormat("\r\n\r\n        public {0} {1}(", text6, methodInfo.Name);
				bool flag = true;
				ParameterInfo[] parameters = methodInfo.GetParameters();
				bool[] array = new bool[parameters.Length];
				int num = 0;
				ParameterInfo[] array2 = parameters;
				foreach (ParameterInfo parameterInfo in array2)
				{
					if (!flag)
					{
						stringBuilder3.Append(",");
					}
					flag = false;
					stringBuilder3.AppendFormat("{0} {1}", CsTypeName(parameterInfo.ParameterType, out array[num]), parameterInfo.Name);
					num++;
				}
				stringBuilder3.Append(")\r\n        {\r\n            ");
				if (!text6.Equals("void"))
				{
					if (isReference4)
					{
						stringBuilder3.AppendFormat("return new {0}() {{ Top = Top, Record = (IAdbRecord)", text6);
					}
					else
					{
						stringBuilder3.AppendFormat("return ({0})", text6);
					}
				}
				stringBuilder3.AppendFormat("Record.Execute(\"{0}\",", ((AdbMethodIdent)obj2).Id.ToString());
				if (parameters.Length == 0)
				{
					stringBuilder3.Append(" null");
				}
				else
				{
					stringBuilder3.Append(" new object[] { ");
					flag = true;
					int num2 = 0;
					array2 = parameters;
					foreach (ParameterInfo parameterInfo2 in array2)
					{
						if (!flag)
						{
							stringBuilder3.Append(", ");
						}
						flag = false;
						stringBuilder3.Append(parameterInfo2.Name);
						if (array[num2++])
						{
							stringBuilder3.Append("== null? null: " + parameterInfo2.Name + ".Record");
						}
					}
					stringBuilder3.Append(" }");
				}
				stringBuilder3.Append(" )");
				if (isReference4)
				{
					stringBuilder3.Append(" }");
				}
				stringBuilder3.Append(";\r\n        }\r\n");
			}
			stringBuilder3.Append("\r\n    }");
			stringBuilder2.Append("\r\n        }");
			csfile.Append(stringBuilder.ToString());
			csfile.Append(stringBuilder2.ToString());
			csfile.Append(stringBuilder3.ToString());
		}
	}
	public class AdbExcelWriter
	{
		private class ClassDesc
		{
			private Dictionary<string, ClassDesc> _descendents = new Dictionary<string, ClassDesc>();

			public Type type { get; set; }

			public ClassDesc Parent { get; set; }

			public Dictionary<string, ClassDesc> Descendents => _descendents;
		}

		private static string _fileNameSuffix = ".xlsx";

		private _Application _excelApplication;

		private _Workbook _currentWorkbook;

		private string _excelFileName = string.Empty;

		private bool _workbookOpened;

		private object _objectMissing = Missing.Value;

		private List<Assembly> _assemblies = new List<Assembly>();

		private Dictionary<string, ClassDesc> _classes = new Dictionary<string, ClassDesc>();

		public AdbExcelWriter(string excelFileName, string[] dllNames)
		{
			_assemblies.Add(Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly());
			for (int i = 0; i < dllNames.Length; i++)
			{
				Assembly assembly = Assembly.LoadFrom(dllNames[i]);
				if (assembly != null)
				{
					_assemblies.Add(assembly);
				}
			}
			if (!excelFileName.EndsWith(_fileNameSuffix))
			{
				excelFileName += _fileNameSuffix;
			}
			_excelFileName = Path.GetFullPath(excelFileName);
			CreateClassHierarchy();
		}

		public void CreateClassReportAuto()
		{
			try
			{
				byte[] dataModelTemplate = Resources.DataModelTemplate;
				FileStream fileStream = new FileStream(_excelFileName, FileMode.Create);
				fileStream.Write(dataModelTemplate, 0, dataModelTemplate.Length);
				fileStream.Close();
				_excelApplication = (Application)Activator.CreateInstance(Marshal.GetTypeFromCLSID(new Guid("00024500-0000-0000-C000-000000000046")));
				_currentWorkbook = null;
				_currentWorkbook = _excelApplication.Workbooks.Open(_excelFileName, _objectMissing, false, _objectMissing, _objectMissing, _objectMissing, _objectMissing, _objectMissing, _objectMissing, _objectMissing, _objectMissing, _objectMissing, _objectMissing, _objectMissing, _objectMissing);
				_workbookOpened = true;
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				_ = ex.Message;
			}
			ReportClassHierarchy();
			ReportClasses(null);
			Close();
		}

		public void CreateClassReportXML()
		{
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			int row = 1;
			int maxcolumn = 0;
			StringBuilder stringBuilder2 = new StringBuilder();
			ReportClassHierarchyLevel(null, ref row, 1, _classes["AdbBaseRecord"].Descendents, stringBuilder2, ref maxcolumn);
			int num = row - 1;
			string excelHeader = Resources.ExcelHeader;
			stringBuilder.AppendFormat(excelHeader, DateTime.Now.ToString("s"), DateTime.Now.ToString("s"), maxcolumn + 1, num);
			stringBuilder.Append(stringBuilder2);
			StringBuilder stringBuilder3 = new StringBuilder();
			int num2 = ReportClasses(stringBuilder3);
			string excelMiddle = Resources.ExcelMiddle;
			stringBuilder.AppendFormat(excelMiddle, num2);
			stringBuilder.Append(stringBuilder3);
			string excelEnd = Resources.ExcelEnd;
			stringBuilder.Append(excelEnd);
			string text = _excelFileName + ".xml";
			StreamWriter streamWriter = new StreamWriter(new FileStream(text, FileMode.Create, FileAccess.ReadWrite));
			streamWriter.Write(stringBuilder);
			streamWriter.Flush();
			streamWriter.Close();
			File.Delete(_excelFileName);
			_excelApplication = (Application)Activator.CreateInstance(Marshal.GetTypeFromCLSID(new Guid("00024500-0000-0000-C000-000000000046")));
			_currentWorkbook = null;
			_currentWorkbook = _excelApplication.Workbooks.Open(text, _objectMissing, false, _objectMissing, _objectMissing, _objectMissing, _objectMissing, _objectMissing, _objectMissing, _objectMissing, _objectMissing, _objectMissing, _objectMissing, _objectMissing, _objectMissing);
			Worksheet worksheet = (dynamic)_currentWorkbook.Sheets["Classes"];
			worksheet.ListObjects.Add(XlListObjectSourceType.xlSrcRange, worksheet.Range[(dynamic)worksheet.Cells[1, 1], (dynamic)worksheet.Cells[num2, 5]], _objectMissing, XlYesNoGuess.xlYes).Name = "ClassTable";
			_currentWorkbook.SaveAs(_excelFileName, XlFileFormat.xlOpenXMLWorkbook, _objectMissing, _objectMissing, _objectMissing, _objectMissing, XlSaveAsAccessMode.xlNoChange, XlSaveConflictResolution.xlLocalSessionChanges, _objectMissing, _objectMissing, _objectMissing, _objectMissing);
			_currentWorkbook.Close(Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing);
			_excelApplication.Quit();
			_excelApplication = null;
		}

		public void ReportClassHierarchy()
		{
			if (_workbookOpened)
			{
				Worksheet worksheet = (dynamic)_currentWorkbook.Sheets["Class Hierarchy"];
				if (worksheet != null)
				{
					int row = 1;
					int maxcolumn = 0;
					ReportClassHierarchyLevel(worksheet, ref row, 1, _classes["AdbBaseRecord"].Descendents, null, ref maxcolumn);
				}
			}
		}

		public void Close()
		{
			if (_workbookOpened)
			{
				_currentWorkbook.Save();
				_currentWorkbook.Close(Type.Missing, Type.Missing, Type.Missing);
				_workbookOpened = false;
			}
			if (_excelApplication != null)
			{
				_excelApplication.Quit();
				_excelApplication = null;
			}
		}

		private ClassDesc FindClassDesc(string name, Type tp)
		{
			ClassDesc value = null;
			if (!_classes.TryGetValue(name, out value))
			{
				value = new ClassDesc
				{
					type = tp,
					Parent = null
				};
				_classes[name] = value;
			}
			return value;
		}

		private void CreateClassHierarchy()
		{
			foreach (Assembly assembly in _assemblies)
			{
				Type[] types = assembly.GetTypes();
				foreach (Type type in types)
				{
					if (type.IsSubclassOf(typeof(AdbBaseRecord)))
					{
						string name = type.Name;
						ClassDesc classDesc = FindClassDesc(name, type);
						string name2 = type.BaseType.Name;
						if (!name.Equals("AdbBaseRecord"))
						{
							ClassDesc classDesc2 = FindClassDesc(name2, type.BaseType);
							classDesc2.Descendents[name] = classDesc;
							classDesc.Parent = classDesc2;
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private void ReportClassHierarchyLevel(Worksheet sheet, ref int row, int column, Dictionary<string, ClassDesc> list, StringBuilder xml, ref int maxcolumn)
		{
			SortedList<string, ClassDesc> sortedList = new SortedList<string, ClassDesc>(list);
			if (column > maxcolumn)
			{
				maxcolumn = column;
			}
			foreach (KeyValuePair<string, ClassDesc> item in sortedList)
			{
				if (xml != null)
				{
					xml.AppendFormat("\r\n   <Row>\r\n    <Cell ss:Index=\"{0}\"><Data ss:Type=\"String\">{1}</Data></Cell>\r\n   </Row>", column, item.Key);
				}
				else
				{
					((dynamic)sheet.Cells[row, column]).Value = item.Key;
				}
				row++;
				ReportClassHierarchyLevel(sheet, ref row, column + 1, item.Value.Descendents, xml, ref maxcolumn);
			}
		}

		private int ReportClassDirectVariables(Worksheet sheet, string rptClass, string parentClass, Type tp, ref int row, StringBuilder xml)
		{
			int num = 0;
			FieldInfo[] fields = tp.GetFields(BindingFlags.DeclaredOnly | BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.Public);
			foreach (FieldInfo fieldInfo in fields)
			{
				if (fieldInfo.GetCustomAttributes(typeof(AdbFieldIdent), inherit: false).Length != 0)
				{
					string text = fieldInfo.FieldType.Name;
					if (text.StartsWith("Nullable") && fieldInfo.FieldType.IsGenericType)
					{
						text = fieldInfo.FieldType.GetGenericArguments()[0].Name + "?";
					}
					else if (fieldInfo.FieldType.IsGenericType)
					{
						Type[] genericArguments = fieldInfo.FieldType.GetGenericArguments();
						text = ((!genericArguments[0].Equals(typeof(AdbNoId))) ? (genericArguments[1].Name + "[" + genericArguments[0].Name + "]") : (genericArguments[1].Name + "[]"));
					}
					if (xml != null)
					{
						num++;
						xml.AppendFormat("\r\n   <Row>");
						xml.AppendFormat("\r\n    <Cell ss:StyleID=\"s22\"><Data ss:Type=\"String\">{0}</Data></Cell>", rptClass);
						xml.AppendFormat("\r\n    <Cell ss:StyleID=\"s23\"><Data ss:Type=\"String\">{0}</Data></Cell>", parentClass);
						xml.AppendFormat("\r\n    <Cell ss:StyleID=\"s22\"><Data ss:Type=\"String\">{0}</Data></Cell>", fieldInfo.Name);
						xml.AppendFormat("\r\n    <Cell ss:StyleID=\"s23\"><Data ss:Type=\"String\">{0}</Data></Cell>", text);
						xml.AppendFormat("\r\n    <Cell ss:StyleID=\"s22\"><Data ss:Type=\"String\">{0}</Data></Cell>", tp.Name);
						xml.AppendFormat("\r\n   </Row>");
					}
					else
					{
						Range obj = sheet.Range[(dynamic)sheet.Cells[row, 1], (dynamic)sheet.Cells[row, 5]];
						obj.Cells[1, 1] = rptClass;
						obj.Cells[1, 2] = parentClass;
						obj.Cells[1, 3] = fieldInfo.Name;
						obj.Cells[1, 4] = text;
						obj.Cells[1, 5] = tp.Name;
					}
					row++;
				}
			}
			MethodInfo[] methods = tp.GetMethods(BindingFlags.DeclaredOnly | BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.Public);
			foreach (MethodInfo methodInfo in methods)
			{
				if (methodInfo.GetCustomAttributes(typeof(AdbMethodIdent), inherit: false).Length != 0)
				{
					if (xml != null)
					{
						num++;
						xml.AppendFormat("\r\n   <Row>");
						xml.AppendFormat("\r\n    <Cell ss:StyleID=\"s22\"><Data ss:Type=\"String\">{0}</Data></Cell>", rptClass);
						xml.AppendFormat("\r\n    <Cell ss:StyleID=\"s23\"><Data ss:Type=\"String\">{0}</Data></Cell>", parentClass);
						xml.AppendFormat("\r\n    <Cell ss:StyleID=\"s22\"><Data ss:Type=\"String\">{0}</Data></Cell>", methodInfo.Name);
						xml.AppendFormat("\r\n    <Cell ss:StyleID=\"s23\"><Data ss:Type=\"String\">{0}</Data></Cell>", "METHOD");
						xml.AppendFormat("\r\n    <Cell ss:StyleID=\"s22\"><Data ss:Type=\"String\">{0}</Data></Cell>", tp.Name);
						xml.AppendFormat("\r\n   </Row>");
					}
					else
					{
						Range obj2 = sheet.Range[(dynamic)sheet.Cells[row, 1], (dynamic)sheet.Cells[row, 5]];
						obj2.Cells[1, 1] = rptClass;
						obj2.Cells[1, 2] = parentClass;
						obj2.Cells[1, 3] = methodInfo.Name;
						obj2.Cells[1, 4] = "METHOD";
						obj2.Cells[1, 5] = tp.Name;
					}
					row++;
				}
			}
			return num;
		}

		private int ReportClassAllVariables(Worksheet sheet, string rptClass, string parentClass, Type tp, ref int row, StringBuilder xml)
		{
			int num = 0;
			if (!tp.Equals(typeof(AdbBaseRecord)))
			{
				num = ReportClassAllVariables(sheet, rptClass, parentClass, tp.BaseType, ref row, xml);
			}
			return num + ReportClassDirectVariables(sheet, rptClass, parentClass, tp, ref row, xml);
		}

		public int ReportClasses(StringBuilder xml)
		{
			int num = 0;
			Worksheet sheet = null;
			if (_currentWorkbook != null)
			{
				sheet = (dynamic)_currentWorkbook.Sheets["Classes"];
			}
			SortedList<string, ClassDesc> sortedList = new SortedList<string, ClassDesc>(_classes);
			int row = 2;
			num = 1;
			foreach (KeyValuePair<string, ClassDesc> item in sortedList)
			{
				num += ReportClassAllVariables(sheet, item.Key, item.Value.type.BaseType.Name, item.Value.type, ref row, xml);
			}
			return num;
		}
	}
	public class AdbException : Exception
	{
		public AdbException(object source, string error)
			: base("Adb Exception: " + error)
		{
		}
	}
	public class AdbGuidCache
	{
		public class RecordClassGuidInfo
		{
			public Type classType;

			public Guid classGuid;

			public int classIndex;

			public int recordIndex;

			public AdbBaseRecord record;
		}

		private Dictionary<Guid, int> _guidToIndex = new Dictionary<Guid, int>();

		private int _currentCount;

		private Guid[] _indexToGuid;

		public AdbReferenceFixups Fixups { get; set; }

		public Guid GetGuidForIndex(int index)
		{
			Guid result = Guid.Empty;
			if (index < _currentCount && index >= 0)
			{
				result = _indexToGuid[index];
			}
			return result;
		}

		public int GetIndexForGuid(Guid guid)
		{
			if (!_guidToIndex.TryGetValue(guid, out var value))
			{
				value = _currentCount++;
				_guidToIndex[guid] = value;
			}
			return value;
		}

		public int ExistingIndexForGuid(Guid guid)
		{
			if (!_guidToIndex.TryGetValue(guid, out var value))
			{
				return -1;
			}
			return value;
		}

		public RecordClassGuidInfo AddRecordGuids(Guid recordGuid, AdbRecordRef recRef)
		{
			RecordClassGuidInfo recordClassGuidInfo = new RecordClassGuidInfo();
			recordClassGuidInfo.recordIndex = GetIndexForGuid(recordGuid);
			recordClassGuidInfo.classType = recRef.Record.GetType();
			object[] customAttributes = recordClassGuidInfo.classType.GetCustomAttributes(typeof(GuidAttribute), inherit: false);
			if (customAttributes.Length != 1)
			{
				throw new AdbException(null, "Class " + recRef.GetType().Name + " does not have exactly one Guid Attribute");
			}
			GuidAttribute guidAttribute = (GuidAttribute)customAttributes[0];
			recordClassGuidInfo.classGuid = Guid.Parse(guidAttribute.Value);
			recordClassGuidInfo.classIndex = ExistingIndexForGuid(recordClassGuidInfo.classGuid);
			recordClassGuidInfo.record = recRef.Record;
			if (recordClassGuidInfo.classIndex == -1)
			{
				recordClassGuidInfo.classIndex = GetIndexForGuid(recordClassGuidInfo.classGuid);
				recRef.Record.AddFieldsToGuidCache(this);
			}
			return recordClassGuidInfo;
		}

		public void Write(StreamWriter sw)
		{
			sw.Write("\r\n <GuidDictionary count=\"{0}\">", _guidToIndex.Count);
			foreach (KeyValuePair<Guid, int> item in _guidToIndex)
			{
				if (!item.Key.Equals(AdbTop.AdbUnknownGuid))
				{
					sw.Write("\r\n  <Guid value=\"{0}\" int=\"{1}\"/>", item.Key, item.Value);
				}
			}
			sw.Write("\r\n </GuidDictionary>");
		}

		public void Read(XmlReader reader)
		{
			_currentCount = int.Parse(reader["count"]);
			_indexToGuid = new Guid[_currentCount];
			reader.Read();
			int num = 0;
			while (reader.NodeType != XmlNodeType.EndElement)
			{
				if (reader.NodeType == XmlNodeType.Element && num < _currentCount)
				{
					_indexToGuid[int.Parse(reader["int"])] = Guid.Parse(reader["value"]);
					num++;
				}
				reader.Read();
			}
		}
	}
	public delegate void AdbSetObjectValueDelegate(object obj);
	public delegate object AdbGetObjectValueDelegate();
	public delegate void AdbOnSetObjectValueDelegate();
	public class AdbReferenceFixups
	{
		private class referenceFixup
		{
			public Guid reference { get; set; }

			public AdbSetObjectValueDelegate fixup { get; set; }

			public AdbOnSetObjectValueDelegate onset { get; set; }
		}

		private AdbTop _top;

		private List<referenceFixup> _fixups = new List<referenceFixup>();

		public AdbReferenceFixups(AdbTop top)
		{
			_top = top;
		}

		public void AddReferenceFixup(Guid reference, AdbSetObjectValueDelegate fixup, AdbOnSetObjectValueDelegate onset)
		{
			bool flag = true;
			if (_top.RecordMap.TryGetValue(reference, out var value) && value.Current)
			{
				fixup(value.Record);
				onset?.Invoke();
				flag = false;
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				_fixups.Add(new referenceFixup
				{
					reference = reference,
					fixup = fixup,
					onset = onset
				});
			}
		}

		public void DoFixups(bool removeUntouched)
		{
			foreach (referenceFixup fixup in _fixups)
			{
				object obj = null;
				if (_top.RecordMap.TryGetValue(fixup.reference, out var value) && value.Current)
				{
					obj = value.Record;
				}
				fixup.fixup(obj);
				if (fixup.onset != null)
				{
					fixup.onset();
				}
			}
			if (!removeUntouched)
			{
				return;
			}
			List<Guid> list = new List<Guid>();
			foreach (KeyValuePair<Guid, AdbRecordRef> item in _top.RecordMap)
			{
				if (!item.Value.Current)
				{
					list.Add(item.Key);
					item.Value.Record.Dead = true;
				}
			}
			foreach (Guid item2 in list)
			{
				_top.RecordMap.Remove(item2, -1L);
			}
		}
	}

		private static extern int StgIsStorageFile([MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.LPWStr)] string pwcsName);

		[DllImport("ole32.dll", CharSet = CharSet.Unicode)]

		[DllImport("ole32.dll")]
		private static extern int StgOpenStorageEx([MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.LPWStr)] string pwcsName, uint grfMode, uint stgfmt, uint grfAttrs, IntPtr StgOptions, IntPtr reserved2, ref Guid riid, [MarshalAs(UnmanagedType.IUnknown)] out object pObjectOpen);

		public static bool ExtractApw(string filename, out byte[] ads)
		{
			ads = null;
			bool result = false;
			try
			{
				if (StgIsStorageFile(filename) == 0)
				{
					uint grfMode = 32u;
					object pObjectOpen = null;
					STGOPTIONS sTGOPTIONS = new STGOPTIONS
					{
						usVersion = 2
					};
					Guid riid = new Guid("0000000b-0000-0000-C000-000000000046");
					uint stgfmt = 0u;
					uint grfAttrs = 0u;
					if (StgOpenStorageEx(filename, grfMode, stgfmt, grfAttrs, IntPtr.Zero, IntPtr.Zero, ref riid, out pObjectOpen) == 0)
					{
						IStorage obj = pObjectOpen as IStorage;
						IStream stm = null;
						string wcsName = "ADS";
						int grfMode2 = 16;
						if (obj.OpenStream(wcsName, IntPtr.Zero, grfMode2, 0, out stm) == 0)
						{
							stm.Stat(out var statstg, 1);
							if (statstg.cbSize > 0)
							{
								byte[] array = new byte[4];
								stm.Read(array, Convert.ToUInt32(4), out var cbRead);
								if (cbRead == 4)
								{
									uint num = BitConverter.ToUInt32(array, 0);
									if (num + 4 <= statstg.cbSize)
									{
										ads = new byte[num];
										stm.Read(ads, num, out cbRead);
										result = true;
									}
								}
							}
							Marshal.ReleaseComObject(stm);
						}
						Marshal.ReleaseComObject(pObjectOpen);
					}
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			return result;
		}
	}

	internal class NameGuidWrite : ShouldBase
	{
		public Guid traceGuid = Guid.Empty;

		public string traceName = "";

		private string _optionName;

		public NameGuidWrite(string optionName)
		{
			_optionName = optionName;
		}

		public override bool Test()
		{
			bool result = false;
			AdbOption option = AdbOptions.GetOption(_optionName);
			if (option != null)
			{
				string text = option.SubOption("guid");
				if (text != null && Guid.TryParse(text, out traceGuid))
				{
					result = true;
				}
				string text2 = option.SubOption("name");
				if (text2 != null)
				{
					traceName = text2;
					result = true;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}
	}
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public class AdbBaseArray<IdType, ValueType> : UnTypedArray, IAdsDataStorer
	{
		private static int instanceTracker = 0;

		public int InstanceNumber = instanceTracker++;

		private static XmlDocument xmlContainer = new XmlDocument();

		private List<ValueType> _valueList = new List<ValueType>();

		private List<IdType> _idList;

		private List<int> _isOutput = new List<int>();

		private AdbBaseRecord _parent;

		public bool ExemptFromThisPurge;

		private static NameGuidWrite traceSize = new NameGuidWrite("TraceArrayGrowth");

		private char[] AtSplit = new char[1] { '@' };

		public bool Dirty { get; set; }

		public AdbTop Top { get; set; }

		public int Length => _valueList.Count;

		private AdbBaseArray()
		{
		}

		public AdbBaseArray(AdbTop top, AdbBaseRecord parent)
		{
			Top = top;
			_parent = parent;
			if (typeof(IdType) != typeof(AdbNoId))
			{
				_idList = new List<IdType>();
			}
			Dirty = false;
		}

		private void ArrayDebug(string message)
		{
			if (traceSize.Should && traceSize.traceName != null)
			{
				FieldInfo field = _parent.GetType().GetField(traceSize.traceName, BindingFlags.IgnoreCase | BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.NonPublic);
				if (field != null && field.GetValue(_parent) == this)
				{
					DebugLog.WriteDebug(message + " Length " + _valueList.Count);
				}
			}
		}

		protected bool IsIndex(object[] id, int currentIndex, out int index)
		{
			object obj = id[currentIndex];
			bool result = false;
			index = -1;
			Type type = obj.GetType();
			if (type == typeof(int) && typeof(IdType) != typeof(int))
			{
				result = true;
				index = (int)obj;
			}
			if (typeof(IdType) == typeof(AdbNoId))
			{
				if (type == typeof(string))
				{
					int.TryParse((string)obj, out index);
				}
				else
				{
					index = (int)obj;
				}
				result = true;
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected IdType ConvertToIdType(object inVal)
		{
			if (!ConvertFromString(inVal, out var obj))
			{
				return (IdType)Convert.ChangeType(inVal, typeof(IdType));
			}
			return obj;
		}

		protected bool ConvertFromString(object sval, out IdType obj)
		{
			bool result = false;
			obj = default(IdType);
			if (sval != null && sval.GetType().Equals(typeof(string)))
			{
				MethodInfo method = typeof(IdType).GetMethod("AdbConvertFromString", BindingFlags.DeclaredOnly | BindingFlags.Static | BindingFlags.Public);
				if (method != null)
				{
					obj = (IdType)method.Invoke(null, new object[2] { Top, sval });
					result = true;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected int GetValueIndex(object[] id, int currentIndex)
		{
			if (currentIndex >= id.Length)
			{
				throw new AdbException(this, "Not enough of Ids supplied");
			}
			_ = id[currentIndex];
			int index = -1;
			if (!IsIndex(id, currentIndex, out index))
			{
				IdType val = ConvertToIdType(id[currentIndex]);
				if (val != null)
				{
					int num = 0;
					foreach (IdType id2 in _idList)
					{
						if (val.Equals(id2))
						{
							index = num;
							break;
						}
						num++;
					}
				}
			}
			return index;
		}

		public override string OutputString(AdbGuidCache guidCache)
		{
			XmlElement xmlElement = xmlContainer.CreateElement("xxx");
			for (int i = 0; i < _valueList.Count; i++)
			{
				XmlElement xmlElement2 = xmlContainer.CreateElement("Element");
				xmlElement.AppendChild(xmlElement2);
				if (_idList != null)
				{
					object o = _idList[i];
					XmlElement xmlElement3 = xmlContainer.CreateElement("Key");
					xmlElement3.InnerText = AdbBaseRecord.ObjectToString(o, guidCache);
					xmlElement2.AppendChild(xmlElement3);
				}
				object o2 = _valueList[i];
				XmlElement xmlElement4 = xmlContainer.CreateElement("Val");
				xmlElement4.InnerText = AdbBaseRecord.ObjectToString(o2, guidCache);
				xmlElement2.AppendChild(xmlElement4);
				if (_isOutput[i] != 0)
				{
					XmlElement xmlElement5 = xmlContainer.CreateElement("O");
					xmlElement5.SetAttribute("m", _isOutput[i].ToString());
					xmlElement2.AppendChild(xmlElement5);
				}
			}
			return xmlElement.InnerXml;
		}

		public StringBuilder GenerateCpp(StringBuilder target)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}

		public StringBuilder GenerateCs(StringBuilder target)
		{
			throw new NotImplementedException();
		}

		public override object Value(object[] id, int currentIndex)
		{
			object result = null;
			IAdbBaseObject adbBaseObject = null;
			if (currentIndex == id.Length)
			{
				result = this;
			}
			else
			{
				int valueIndex = GetValueIndex(id, currentIndex);
				if (valueIndex >= 0 && valueIndex < _valueList.Count)
				{
					ValueType val = _valueList[valueIndex];
					if (val != null)
					{
						adbBaseObject = (IAdbBaseObject)(object)val;
						if (adbBaseObject != null)
						{
							result = adbBaseObject.Value(id, currentIndex + 1);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public override void SetValue(object[] id, int currentIndex, object newValue)
		{
			IAdbBaseObject adbBaseObject = null;
			int valueIndex = GetValueIndex(id, currentIndex);
			if (valueIndex >= 0 && valueIndex < _valueList.Count)
			{
				ValueType val = _valueList[valueIndex];
				if (val != null)
				{
					((IAdbBaseObject)(object)val)?.SetValue(id, currentIndex + 1, newValue);
				}
			}
		}

		public override void RestoreValue(object[] id, int currentIndex, object newValue)
		{
			IAdbBaseObject adbBaseObject = null;
			int valueIndex = GetValueIndex(id, currentIndex);
			if (valueIndex >= 0 && valueIndex < _valueList.Count)
			{
				ValueType val = _valueList[valueIndex];
				if (val != null)
				{
					((IAdbBaseObject)(object)val)?.RestoreValue(id, currentIndex + 1, newValue);
				}
			}
		}

		private static object defualt(Type t)
		{
			if (t.IsValueType)
			{
				return t.GetConstructor(new Type[0]).Invoke(new object[0]);
			}
			return null;
		}

		public override void SetValue(XmlReader reader, AdbGuidCache guidCache)
		{
			_valueList = new List<ValueType>();
			ArrayDebug("Create during restore");
			if (typeof(IdType) != typeof(AdbNoId))
			{
				_idList = new List<IdType>();
			}
			_isOutput = new List<int>();
			while (reader.Read() && (reader.NodeType != XmlNodeType.EndElement || !reader.Name.Equals("Array")))
			{
				if (reader.NodeType != XmlNodeType.Element)
				{
					continue;
				}
				int item = 0;
				if (!reader.Name.Equals("Element"))
				{
					continue;
				}
				string text = null;
				string text2 = null;
				reader.Read();
				while (reader.NodeType != XmlNodeType.EndElement)
				{
					if (reader.NodeType == XmlNodeType.Element && reader.Name.Equals("Key"))
					{
						if (!reader.IsEmptyElement)
						{
							reader.Read();
							if (reader.NodeType != XmlNodeType.EndElement)
							{
								text = reader.Value;
								reader.Read();
							}
						}
					}
					else if (reader.NodeType == XmlNodeType.Element && reader.Name.Equals("Val"))
					{
						if (!reader.IsEmptyElement)
						{
							reader.Read();
							if (reader.NodeType != XmlNodeType.EndElement)
							{
								text2 = reader.Value;
								reader.Read();
							}
						}
					}
					else if (reader.NodeType == XmlNodeType.Element && reader.Name.Equals("O"))
					{
						item = 2;
						string attribute = reader.GetAttribute("m");
						if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(attribute) && int.TryParse(attribute, out var result))
						{
							item = result;
						}
					}
					reader.Read();
				}
				if (_idList != null)
				{
					_idList.Add(default(IdType));
				}
				_valueList.Add(default(ValueType));
				ArrayDebug("Extend");
				_isOutput.Add(item);
				if (text == null && text2 == null)
				{
					continue;
				}
				int elementNumber = _valueList.Count - 1;
				if (typeof(IdType) != typeof(AdbNoId) && text != null)
				{
					AdbValueDescriptor.ValueType vt = AdbBaseRecord.GetVt(typeof(IdType));
					AdbValueDescriptor adbValueDescriptor = new AdbValueDescriptor(null);
					adbValueDescriptor.Top = Top;
					adbValueDescriptor.VType = vt;
					adbValueDescriptor.restoreValue = delegate(object o)
					{
						_idList[elementNumber] = (IdType)o;
					};
					adbValueDescriptor.SetValueFromPersistence(text, guidCache);
				}
				AdbValueDescriptor.ValueType vt2 = AdbBaseRecord.GetVt(typeof(ValueType));
				AdbValueDescriptor obj = new AdbValueDescriptor(null)
				{
					Top = Top,
					VType = vt2,
					restoreValue = delegate(object o)
					{
						_valueList[elementNumber] = (ValueType)o;
					}
				};
				if (text2 != null && text2.Equals("1E+35") && typeof(ValueType) == Type.GetType("System.Nullable`1[System.Double]"))
				{
					text2 = null;
				}
				obj.SetValueFromPersistence(text2, guidCache, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
			}
			ArrayDebug("Finished restore");
		}

		public object ValueAt(object index)
		{
			object result = null;
			if (_idList != null)
			{
				int num = 0;
				foreach (IdType id in _idList)
				{
					if (id != null && id.Equals(index))
					{
						result = _valueList[num];
						break;
					}
					num++;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public object ValueAtLocation(int index)
		{
			object result = null;

			{
				result = _valueList[index];
			}
			return result;
		}

		public object IDAtLocation(int index)
		{
			object result = null;

			{
				result = _idList[index];
			}
			return result;
		}

		public int? SetValueAt(IdType index, object value)
		{
			int? result = null;
			int num = 0;
			if (_idList != null)
			{
				foreach (IdType id in _idList)
				{
					if (id != null && id.Equals(index))
					{
						Dirty = true;
						_parent.SetNewChange();
						if (value != null)
						{
							_valueList[num] = (ValueType)value;
						}
						else
						{
							_valueList[num] = default(ValueType);
						}
						_isOutput[num] = Top.IsReadingOutput;
						result = num;
						break;
					}
					num++;
				}
			}
			if (!result.HasValue)
			{
				Dirty = true;
				_parent.SetNewChange();
				Append(index, value);
				result = num;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public void SetValueAtLocation(int index, object value)
		{
			if (index >= 0 && index < _valueList.Count)
			{
				if (value != null)
				{
					_valueList[index] = (ValueType)value;
				}
				else
				{
					_valueList[index] = default(ValueType);
				}
				_isOutput[index] = Top.IsReadingOutput;
				Dirty = true;
				_parent.SetNewChange();
			}
		}

		public void SetID(int index, object value)
		{
			if (_idList != null && index >= 0 && index < _idList.Count)
			{
				_idList[index] = (IdType)value;
				Dirty = true;
				_parent.SetNewChange();
			}
		}

		public int FindID(IdType index)
		{

				foreach (IdType id in _idList)

		public int FindGenericID(object indexObject)

		public int FindValue(ValueType value)
		{
			int num = 0;

			foreach (ValueType value2 in _valueList)
			{

					result = num;

				num++;

		public void InsertBeforeLocation(int index, IdType id, object value)
		{
			if (index >= 0 && index < _idList.Count)
			{
				Dirty = true;
				_parent.SetNewChange();
				_valueList.Insert(index, (ValueType)value);
				ArrayDebug("Insert at " + index);
				_isOutput.Insert(index, Top.IsReadingOutput);
				if (_idList != null)
				{
					_idList.Insert(index, id);
				}
			}
		}

		public void Append(IdType index, object value)
		{
			if (_idList != null)
			{
				_idList.Add(index);
			}
			if (value == null)
			{
				_valueList.Add(default(ValueType));
				_isOutput.Add(0);
			}
			else
			{
				_valueList.Add((ValueType)value);
				_isOutput.Add(Top.IsReadingOutput);
			}
			ArrayDebug("Appending with id");
			_parent.SetNewChange();
			Dirty = true;
		}

		public void Append(object value)
		{
			if (_idList != null)
			{
				_idList.Add(default(IdType));
			}
			_valueList.Add((ValueType)value);
			_isOutput.Add(Top.IsReadingOutput);
			ArrayDebug("Appending without id");
			_parent.SetNewChange();
			Dirty = true;
		}

		public void RemoveAtLocation(int index)
		{
			if (index >= 0 && index < _valueList.Count)
			{
				if (_idList != null)
				{
					_idList.RemoveAt(index);
				}
				_valueList.RemoveAt(index);
				_isOutput.RemoveAt(index);
				ArrayDebug("Remove location " + index);
				_parent.SetNewChange();
				Dirty = true;
			}
		}

		public int? RemoveAt(IdType index)
		{
			int? result = null;
			if (_idList != null)
			{
				int num = 0;
				foreach (IdType id in _idList)
				{
					if (id != null && id.Equals(index))
					{
						_idList.RemoveAt(num);
						_valueList.RemoveAt(num);
						_isOutput.RemoveAt(num);
						ArrayDebug("Remove id");
						_parent.SetNewChange();
						Dirty = true;
						result = num;
						return result;
					}
					num++;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public override object ExecuteMethod(object[] id, int currentIndex, object[] arguments)
		{
			object result = null;
			if (currentIndex == id.Length - 1)
			{
				string text = ((string)id[currentIndex]).ToUpper();
				if (text.Equals(AdbArrayMethodGuids.SetValueAt))
				{
					result = SetValueAt((IdType)arguments[0], arguments[1]);
				}
				else if (text.Equals(AdbArrayMethodGuids.SetValueAtLocation))
				{
					SetValueAtLocation((int)arguments[0], arguments[1]);
				}
				else if (text.Equals(AdbArrayMethodGuids.Append))
				{
					if (arguments[0] == null)
					{
						arguments[0] = default(AdbNoId);
					}
					Append((IdType)arguments[0], arguments[1]);
				}
				else if (text.Equals(AdbArrayMethodGuids.ValueAt))
				{
					result = ValueAt((IdType)arguments[0]);
				}
				else if (text.Equals(AdbArrayMethodGuids.ValueAtLocation))
				{
					result = ValueAtLocation((int)arguments[0]);
				}
				else if (text.Equals(AdbArrayMethodGuids.Length))
				{
					result = Length;
				}
				else if (text.Equals(AdbArrayMethodGuids.InsertBeforeLocation))
				{
					InsertBeforeLocation((int)arguments[0], (IdType)arguments[1], arguments[2]);
				}
				else if (text.Equals(AdbArrayMethodGuids.RemoveAtLocation))
				{
					RemoveAtLocation((int)arguments[0]);
				}
				else if (text.Equals(AdbArrayMethodGuids.RemoveAt))
				{
					result = RemoveAt((IdType)arguments[0]);
				}
				else if (text.Equals(AdbArrayMethodGuids.IdAtLocation))
				{
					result = IDAtLocation((int)arguments[0]);
				}
				else if (text.Equals(AdbArrayMethodGuids.SetId))
				{
					SetID((int)arguments[0], arguments[1]);
				}
				else if (text.Equals(AdbArrayMethodGuids.FindId))
				{
					result = FindGenericID(arguments[0]);
				}
				else if (text.Equals(AdbArrayMethodGuids.FindValue))
				{
					result = FindValue((ValueType)arguments[0]);
				}
				else if (text.Equals(AdbArrayMethodGuids.Clear))
				{
					if (arguments == null || arguments.Length == 0)
					{
						Clear(deleteRecords: false);
					}
					else
					{
						Clear((bool)arguments[0]);
					}
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public override bool DeleteAllOutput(int mask = 3)
		{
			bool result = true;
			if (ExemptFromThisPurge)
			{
				result = false;
			}
			else
			{
				if (typeof(ValueType).IsSubclassOf(typeof(AdbBaseRecord)))
				{
					foreach (ValueType value in _valueList)
					{
						if (value is IAdbBaseObject adbBaseObject)
						{
							adbBaseObject.DeleteAllOutput(mask);
						}
					}
				}
				List<int> list = new List<int>();
				for (int i = 0; i < _valueList.Count; i++)
				{
					if ((_isOutput[i] & mask) != 0 || _valueList[i] == null)
					{
						list.Insert(0, i);
					}
					else
					{
						result = false;
					}
				}
				foreach (int item in list)
				{
					RemoveAtLocation(item);
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public override void Traverse()
		{
			if (typeof(IdType).IsSubclassOf(typeof(AdbBaseRecord)))
			{
				foreach (IdType id in _idList)
				{
					if (id is IAdbBaseObject adbBaseObject)
					{
						adbBaseObject.Traverse();
					}
				}
			}
			if (!typeof(ValueType).IsSubclassOf(typeof(AdbBaseRecord)))
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (ValueType value in _valueList)
			{
				if (value is IAdbBaseObject adbBaseObject2)
				{
					adbBaseObject2.Traverse();
				}
			}
		}

		public void Clear(bool deleteRecords)
		{
			if (deleteRecords)
			{
				foreach (ValueType value in _valueList)
				{
					if (value is AdbBaseRecord adbBaseRecord)
					{
						adbBaseRecord.CleanUp();
						adbBaseRecord.ExecuteMethod(new object[1] { AdbArrayMethodGuids.Delete }, 0, null);
					}
				}
			}
			if (_idList != null)
			{
				_idList.Clear();
			}
			if (_valueList != null)
			{
				_valueList.Clear();
				ArrayDebug("Clearing");
			}
			if (_isOutput != null)
			{
				_isOutput.Clear();
			}
		}

		}

						if (typeFromHandle.IsSubclassOf(typeof(AdbBaseRecord)))
						{
							object obj = ValueAtLocation(num);
							bool flag4 = false;
							if (obj == null)
							{
								flag4 = true;
								obj = typeFromHandle.Create(Top, _parent, null, 2);
								SetValueAtLocation(num, obj);
							}
							IAdsDataStorer val5 = (IAdsDataStorer)((obj is IAdsDataStorer) ? obj : null);
							if (val5 != null)
							{
								if (!val5.StoreData(path.Next, pathData, formattedData))
								{
									if (flag4 && obj is AdbBaseRecord adbBaseRecord)
									{
										adbBaseRecord.CleanUp();
										adbBaseRecord.ExecuteMethod(new object[1] { AdbArrayMethodGuids.Delete }, 0, null);
									}
									if (flag2)
									{
										RemoveAtLocation(Length - 1);
									}
								}
								else
								{
									flag = true;
									stored = true;
								}
							}
						}
					}
				}
				if (!flag && formattedData != null)
				{
					formattedData.Length = length;
				}
				counter++;
			}
			return flag;
		}

		public bool StoreData(StorePathNode path, IArgumentData pathData, StringBuilder formattedData)
		{
			bool stored = false;
			int counter = 0;
			if (!path.IsEnumerator)
			{
				StoreOneValue(path.NodeName, path, pathData, ref stored, ref counter, formattedData);
			}
			else
			{
				string value = "";
				if (formattedData != null)
				{
					string text = formattedData.ToString();
					int num = 0;
					int num2 = text.LastIndexOf('\n');
					num = ((num2 < 0) ? formattedData.Length : (formattedData.Length - num2));
					value = new string(' ', num);
				}
				bool flag = true;
				int length = -1;
				foreach (KeyValuePair<string, IArgumentData> idValue in pathData.IdValues)
				{
					if (formattedData != null)
					{
						length = formattedData.Length;
						if (!flag)
						{
							formattedData.Append(value);
						}
					}
					bool flag2 = StoreOneValue(idValue.Key, path, idValue.Value, ref stored, ref counter, formattedData);
					if (formattedData != null && !flag2)
					{
						formattedData.Length = length;
					}
					if (flag2)
					{
						flag = false;
					}
				}
			}
			return stored;
		}
	}

		public static AdbClassInfo CreateFieldCache(Type thisType, Guid classGuid)
		{
			AdbClassInfo adbClassInfo = new AdbClassInfo();
			adbClassInfo.fields = new Dictionary<Guid, AdbFieldAttribute>();
			adbClassInfo.methods = new Dictionary<Guid, AdbMethodAttribute>();
			adbClassInfo.fieldList = new List<AdbFieldAttribute>();
			adbClassInfo.directChildren = new List<Guid>();
			adbClassInfo.classRecordMap = new Dictionary<Guid, AdbRecordRef>();
			adbClassInfo.IOSwitchLength = 0;
			FieldInfo[] fields = thisType.GetFields();
			foreach (FieldInfo fieldInfo in fields)
			{
				object[] customAttributes = fieldInfo.GetCustomAttributes(typeof(AdbFieldIdent), inherit: false);
				if (customAttributes.Length != 1)
				{
					continue;
				}
				AdbFieldIdent adbFieldIdent = (AdbFieldIdent)customAttributes[0];
				Guid id = adbFieldIdent.Id;
				if (adbClassInfo.fields.ContainsKey(id))
				{
					throw new AdbException(thisType, "Key " + id.ToString() + " is used more than once");
				}
				AdbFieldAttribute adbFieldAttribute = new AdbFieldAttribute
				{
					F = fieldInfo,
					Attribute = adbFieldIdent
				};
				if (adbFieldIdent.Getter != null)
				{
					adbFieldAttribute.GetterMethod = thisType.GetMethod(adbFieldIdent.Getter);
					if (adbFieldAttribute.GetterMethod == null)
					{
						throw new AdbException(thisType, "Getter method: " + adbFieldIdent.Getter + " for Field: " + fieldInfo.Name + " does not exist");
					}
				}
				if (adbFieldIdent.Setter != null)
				{
					adbFieldAttribute.SetterMethod = thisType.GetMethod(adbFieldIdent.Setter);
					if (adbFieldAttribute.SetterMethod == null)
					{
						throw new AdbException(thisType, "Setter method: " + adbFieldIdent.Setter + " for Field: " + fieldInfo.Name + " does not exist");
					}
				}
				if (adbFieldIdent.OnSet != null)
				{
					adbFieldAttribute.OnSetMethod = thisType.GetMethod(adbFieldIdent.OnSet);
					if (adbFieldAttribute.OnSetMethod == null)
					{
						throw new AdbException(thisType, "OnSet method: " + adbFieldIdent.OnSet + " for Field: " + fieldInfo.Name + " does not exist");
					}
				}
				if (adbFieldIdent.DimensionVariable != null)
				{
					adbFieldAttribute.DimensionVariable = thisType.GetField(adbFieldIdent.DimensionVariable, BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.Public | BindingFlags.NonPublic);
					if (adbFieldAttribute.DimensionVariable == null)
					{
						throw new AdbException(thisType, "DimensionVaraible: " + adbFieldIdent.DimensionVariable + " for Field: " + fieldInfo.Name + " does not exist");
					}
				}
				if (adbFieldIdent.ArrayOverride != null)
				{
					adbFieldAttribute.ArrayBase = thisType.GetField(adbFieldIdent.ArrayOverride);
				}
				if (fieldInfo.FieldType != typeof(AdbIOReal) && adbFieldIdent.ArrayOverride == null && (!fieldInfo.FieldType.IsGenericType || fieldInfo.FieldType.GetGenericTypeDefinition() == typeof(Nullable<>)))
				{
					adbFieldAttribute.IOindex = adbClassInfo.IOSwitchLength++;
				}
				else
				{
					adbFieldAttribute.IOindex = -1;
				}
				adbClassInfo.fields.Add(id, adbFieldAttribute);
				adbClassInfo.fieldList.Add(adbFieldAttribute);
			}
			MethodInfo[] methods = thisType.GetMethods();
			foreach (MethodInfo methodInfo in methods)
			{
				object[] customAttributes2 = methodInfo.GetCustomAttributes(typeof(AdbMethodIdent), inherit: false);
				if (customAttributes2.Length == 1)
				{
					AdbMethodIdent a = (AdbMethodIdent)customAttributes2[0];
					Guid id2 = ((AdbBaseIdent)customAttributes2[0]).Id;
					if (adbClassInfo.methods.ContainsKey(id2))
					{
						throw new AdbException(thisType, "Key " + id2.ToString() + " is used more than once");
					}
					adbClassInfo.methods.Add(id2, new AdbMethodAttribute(methodInfo, a));
				}
			}
			return adbClassInfo;
		}

		protected Guid? GetGuidId(object[] id, int currentIndex)
		{
			if (id[currentIndex] == null)
			{
				throw new AdbException(this, "Null variable id passed to AdbRecord");
			}
			if (id[currentIndex].GetType() != typeof(string))
			{
				throw new AdbException(this, "Type \"" + id[currentIndex].GetType().Name + "\" passed to AdbRecord when Guid required");
			}
			Guid result;
			return Guid.TryParse((string)id[currentIndex], out result) ? new Guid?(result) : ((Guid?)null);
		}

		public object GetActualArray(string guidString)
		{
			object result = null;
			if (Guid.TryParse(guidString, out var result2))
			{
				AdbFieldAttribute fieldAttribute = GetFieldAttribute(result2);
				if (fieldAttribute != null)
				{
					result = fieldAttribute.F.GetValue(this);
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public void SetActualArray(string guidString, object array)
		{
			if (Guid.TryParse(guidString, out var result))
			{
				AdbFieldAttribute fieldAttribute = GetFieldAttribute(result);
				if (fieldAttribute != null && fieldAttribute.F.FieldType.IsAssignableFrom(array.GetType()))
				{
					fieldAttribute.F.SetValue(this, array);
				}
			}
		}

		protected AdbFieldAttribute GetFieldAttribute(Guid gid)

			AdbFieldAttribute value = null;
			GetFieldCache.TryGetValue(gid, out value);
			return value;
		}

		}

		protected AdbFieldAttribute GetFieldAttribute(string name)
		{
			AdbFieldAttribute result = null;
			foreach (KeyValuePair<Guid, AdbFieldAttribute> item in GetFieldCache)
			{
				if (item.Value.F.Name.Equals(name))
				{
					result = item.Value;
					break;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected AdbFieldAttribute GetFieldAttribute(object[] id, int currentIndex)

			AdbFieldAttribute adbFieldAttribute = null;
			Guid? guidId = GetGuidId(id, currentIndex);
			if (guidId.HasValue)
			{
				return GetFieldAttribute(guidId.Value);
			}
			return GetFieldAttribute((string)id[currentIndex]);
		}

		protected AdbMethodAttribute GetMethodAttribute(Guid gid)

		public static object ConvertToObject(Type t, string value)
		{
			object result = null;
			if (_valtypes.TryGetValue(t, out var value2))
			{
				switch (value2)
				{
				case AdbValueDescriptor.ValueType.Int:
					result = int.Parse(value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
					break;
				case AdbValueDescriptor.ValueType.Real:
					result = double.Parse(value, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
					break;
				case AdbValueDescriptor.ValueType.Bool:
					result = bool.Parse(value);
					break;
				case AdbValueDescriptor.ValueType.String:
					result = value;
					break;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static AdbValueDescriptor.ValueType GetVt(Type type)
		{
			if (!_valtypes.TryGetValue(type, out var value))
			{
				if (type.IsSubclassOf(typeof(AdbBaseRecord)))
				{
					return AdbValueDescriptor.ValueType.Reference;
				}
				if (type.IsGenericType && type.GetGenericTypeDefinition() == typeof(AdbBaseArray<, >))
				{
					return AdbValueDescriptor.ValueType.Array;
				}
				if (type.Equals(typeof(AdbIOReal)))
				{
					return AdbValueDescriptor.ValueType.IOReal;
				}
			}
			return value;
		}

		protected IAdbBaseObject GetValueObject(Guid gid, bool create)
		{
			IAdbBaseObject result = null;
			AdbFieldAttribute fieldAttribute = GetFieldAttribute(gid);
			if (fieldAttribute != null)
			{
				AdbValueDescriptor.ValueType vt = GetVt(fieldAttribute.F.FieldType);
				result = GetValueObjectBase(fieldAttribute, vt, create);
			}
			return result;
		}

		protected IAdbBaseObject GetValueObjectBase(AdbFieldAttribute fa, AdbValueDescriptor.ValueType vtype, bool create, object[] id = null, int currentindex = 0)
		{
			IAdbBaseObject adbBaseObject = null;
			switch (vtype)
			{
			case AdbValueDescriptor.ValueType.IOReal:
			{
				object obj = fa.F.GetValue(this);
				if (obj == null && create)
				{
					obj = new AdbIOReal();
					fa.F.SetValue(this, obj);
				}
				adbBaseObject = obj as IAdbBaseObject;
				break;
			}
			case AdbValueDescriptor.ValueType.Array:
			{
				if (fa.ArrayBase != null)
				{
					adbBaseObject = (IAdbBaseObject)fa.ArrayBase.GetValue(this);
					break;
				}
				object obj2 = fa.F.GetValue(this);
				if (obj2 == null && create)
				{
					obj2 = fa.F.FieldType.Create(this);
					fa.F.SetValue(this, obj2);
				}
				adbBaseObject = new AdbValueDescriptor(this)
				{
					Top = Top,
					ArrayObject = (IAdbBaseObject)obj2,
					VType = vtype,
					Af = fa
				};
				break;
			}
			default:
			{
				AdbValueDescriptor adbValueDescriptor = new AdbValueDescriptor(this)
				{
					Top = Top,
					VType = vtype,
					setValue = delegate(object o)
					{
						fa.F.SetValue(this, o);
						SetNewChange();
						if (fa.IOindex >= 0)
						{
							_isOutput[fa.IOindex] = Top.IsReadingOutput;
						}
					},
					restoreValue = delegate(object o)
					{
						fa.F.SetValue(this, o);
					},
					getValue = () => fa.F.GetValue(this),
					onSetValue = null,
					Af = fa
				};
				adbBaseObject = adbValueDescriptor;
				if (fa.SetterMethod != null || fa.GetterMethod != null)
				{
					if (fa.GetterMethod != null)
					{
						adbValueDescriptor.getValue = () => fa.GetterMethod.Invoke(this, null);
					}
					else
					{
						adbValueDescriptor.getValue = null;
					}
					if (fa.SetterMethod != null)
					{
						adbValueDescriptor.setValue = delegate(object o)
						{
							fa.SetterMethod.Invoke(this, new object[1] { o });
						};
					}
					else
					{
						adbValueDescriptor.setValue = null;
					}
				}
				if (fa.OnSetMethod != null)
				{
					adbValueDescriptor.onSetValue = delegate
					{
						fa.OnSetMethod.Invoke(this, null);
					};
				}
				break;
			}
			}
			return adbBaseObject;
		}

		protected IAdbBaseObject GetValueObject(object[] id, int currentIndex, bool create)
		{
			IAdbBaseObject result = null;
			AdbFieldAttribute fieldAttribute = GetFieldAttribute(id, currentIndex);
			if (fieldAttribute != null)
			{
				AdbValueDescriptor.ValueType vt = GetVt(fieldAttribute.F.FieldType);
				result = GetValueObjectBase(fieldAttribute, vt, create, id, currentIndex);
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static string ObjectToString(object o, AdbGuidCache guidCache)
		{
			string result = null;
			if (o != null)
			{
				result = ((!(o is IAdbBaseObject adbBaseObject)) ? ((!(o is int num)) ? ((!(o is double num2)) ? ((!(o is bool flag)) ? AdbTop.CleanForXML(o.ToString()) : flag.ToString().ToLower()) : num2.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)) : num.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)) : adbBaseObject.OutputString(guidCache));
			}
			return result;
		}

		public virtual void AddFieldsToGuidCache(AdbGuidCache guidCache)
		{
			foreach (KeyValuePair<Guid, AdbFieldAttribute> item in GetFieldCache)
			{
				if (item.Value.Attribute.Getter == null && item.Value.Attribute.Setter == null)
				{
					guidCache.GetIndexForGuid(item.Key);
				}
			}
		}

		public virtual void Write(StreamWriter sw, AdbGuidCache guidCache, StreamWriter swreadable)
		{
			if (Dead)
			{
				return;
			}
			Dictionary<Guid, AdbFieldAttribute> getFieldCache = GetFieldCache;
			if (CreatedForOutput != 0)
			{
				sw.Write("<O m=\"" + CreatedForOutput + "\"/>");
				swreadable?.Write("<Output m=\"" + CreatedForOutput + "\"/>");
			}
			foreach (KeyValuePair<Guid, AdbFieldAttribute> item in getFieldCache)
			{
				if (item.Value.Attribute.Getter != null || item.Value.Attribute.Setter != null)
				{
					continue;
				}
				Type fieldType = item.Value.F.FieldType;
				object value = item.Value.F.GetValue(this);
				string text = ObjectToString(value, guidCache);
				bool flag = false;
				if (value is IAdbBaseObject && (!fieldType.IsGenericType || !(fieldType.GetGenericTypeDefinition() == typeof(AdbBaseArray<, >))))
				{
					flag = true;
				}
				if (tracePersist.Should && tracePersist.traceGuid.Equals(item.Key))
				{
					int num = 0;
					if (item.Value.IOindex >= 0)
					{
						num = _isOutput[item.Value.IOindex];
					}
					string text2 = "";
					if (text != null)
					{
						text2 = text.Substring(0, Math.Min(text.Length, 16));
					}
					DebugLog.WriteDebug(" Write Variable:  Value = " + text2 + " Output " + num);
				}
				if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(text))
				{
					continue;
				}
				string text3 = "Value";
				if (fieldType.Equals(typeof(AdbIOReal)))
				{
					int num2 = guidCache.ExistingIndexForGuid(item.Key);
					sw.Write("<{0} guid=\"{1}\">", text3, num2);
					sw.Write(text);
					sw.Write("</{0}>", text3);
				}
				else if (fieldType.IsGenericType && fieldType.GetGenericTypeDefinition() == typeof(AdbBaseArray<, >))
				{
					Type[] genericArguments = item.Value.F.FieldType.GetGenericArguments();
					text3 = "Array";
					int num3 = guidCache.ExistingIndexForGuid(item.Key);
					sw.Write("<{0} guid=\"{1}\"", text3, num3);
					if (!genericArguments[0].Name.Equals("AdbNoId") && genericArguments[0].IsSubclassOf(typeof(AdbBaseRecord)))
					{
						sw.Write(" idguid=\"1\"");
					}
					if (genericArguments[1].IsSubclassOf(typeof(AdbBaseRecord)))
					{
						sw.Write(" valguid=\"1\"");
					}
					sw.Write(">");
					sw.Write(text);
					sw.Write("</{0}>", text3);
				}
				else
				{
					XmlElement xmlElement = xmlContainer.CreateElement(text3);
					xmlElement.SetAttribute("guid", guidCache.GetIndexForGuid(item.Key).ToString());
					if (flag)
					{
						xmlElement.SetAttribute("isguid", "1");
					}
					if (item.Value.IOindex >= 0 && _isOutput[item.Value.IOindex] != 0)
					{
						xmlElement.SetAttribute("o", _isOutput[item.Value.IOindex].ToString());
					}
					xmlElement.InnerText = text;
					sw.Write(xmlElement.OuterXml);
				}
				if (swreadable == null)
				{
					continue;
				}
				swreadable.Write("<{0} name=\"{1}\"", text3, item.Value.F.Name);
				if (item.Value.IOindex >= 0)
				{
					if (_isOutput[item.Value.IOindex] != 0)
					{
						swreadable.Write(" o=\"" + _isOutput[item.Value.IOindex] + "\">");
					}
					else
					{
						swreadable.Write(">");
					}
				}
				swreadable.Write(text);
				swreadable.Write("</{0}>", text3);
			}
			foreach (string unknownScalarField in UnknownScalarFields)
			{
				XmlReader xmlReader = XmlReader.Create(new StringReader(unknownScalarField));
				xmlReader.Read();
				string value2 = AdbUnknownUtility.ParseScalarVariable(xmlReader, guidCache, isRead: false);
				sw.Write(value2);
			}
			foreach (string unknownArrayField in UnknownArrayFields)
			{
				XmlReader xmlReader2 = XmlReader.Create(new StringReader(unknownArrayField));
				xmlReader2.Read();
				string value3 = AdbUnknownUtility.ParseArrayVariable(xmlReader2, guidCache, isRead: false);
				sw.Write(value3);
			}
		}

		public virtual void Read(XmlReader reader, AdbGuidCache guidCache, long changeNumber = -1L)
		{
			CreatedForOutput = 0;
			long nextChangeNumber = Top.NextChangeNumber;
			Clear();
			UnknownScalarFields.Clear();
			UnknownArrayFields.Clear();
			while (reader.Read() && (reader.NodeType != XmlNodeType.EndElement || !reader.Name.Equals("Record")))
			{
				if (reader.NodeType != XmlNodeType.Element)
				{
					continue;
				}
				switch (reader.Name)
				{
				case "O":
				{
					string attribute = reader.GetAttribute("m");
					if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(attribute))
					{
						if (int.TryParse(attribute, out var result2))
						{
							CreatedForOutput = result2;
						}
						else
						{
							CreatedForOutput = 2;
						}
					}
					else
					{
						CreatedForOutput = 2;
					}
					break;
				}
				case "Value":
				{
					Guid guidForIndex = guidCache.GetGuidForIndex(int.Parse(reader["guid"]));
					string value = reader.Value;
					string text = reader["o"];
					IAdbBaseObject valueObject = null;
					int isReadingOutput = Top.IsReadingOutput;
					AdbFieldAttribute fieldAttribute = GetFieldAttribute(guidForIndex);
					if (fieldAttribute == null)
					{
						string g2 = guidCache.GetGuidForIndex(int.Parse(reader["guid"])).ToString();
						Top.UnknownFieldGuids.Add(new Guid(g2));
						string item2 = AdbUnknownUtility.ParseScalarVariable(reader, guidCache, isRead: true);
						UnknownScalarFields.Add(item2);
					}
					else
					{
						AdbValueDescriptor.ValueType vt = GetVt(fieldAttribute.F.FieldType);
						valueObject = GetValueObjectBase(fieldAttribute, vt, create: true);
					}
					if (valueObject != null)
					{
						if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(text))
						{
							if (int.TryParse(text, out var result))
							{
								Top.IsReadingOutput = result;
							}
							else
							{
								Top.IsReadingOutput = 2;
							}
						}
						else
						{
							Top.IsReadingOutput = 0;
						}
						if (tracePersist.Should && tracePersist.traceGuid.Equals(guidForIndex))
						{
							DebugLog.WriteDebug(" Read Variable:  Value = " + value + " Output " + Top.IsReadingOutput);
						}
						valueObject.SetValue(reader, guidCache);
					}
					if (fieldAttribute != null && fieldAttribute.IOindex > -1)
					{
						_isOutput[fieldAttribute.IOindex] = Top.IsReadingOutput;
					}
					Top.IsReadingOutput = isReadingOutput;
					break;
				}
				case "Array":
				{
					Guid guidForIndex = guidCache.GetGuidForIndex(int.Parse(reader["guid"]));
					IAdbBaseObject valueObject = GetValueObject(guidForIndex, create: true);
					if (valueObject == null)
					{
						string g = guidCache.GetGuidForIndex(int.Parse(reader["guid"])).ToString();
						Top.UnknownFieldGuids.Add(new Guid(g));
						string item = AdbUnknownUtility.ParseArrayVariable(reader, guidCache, isRead: true);
						UnknownArrayFields.Add(item);
					}
					else
					{
						valueObject.SetValue(reader, guidCache);
					}
					break;
				}
				}
			}
			if (changeNumber != -1)
			{
				LastChangeNumber = changeNumber;
				Top.NextChangeNumber = Math.Max(nextChangeNumber, changeNumber + 1);
			}
		}

		public object Value(object[] id, int currentIndex)
		{
			object result = null;
			if (!Dead)
			{
				if (currentIndex == id.Length)
				{
					result = this;
				}
				else
				{
					result = GetValueObject(id, currentIndex, create: false);
					result = ((!(result is IAdbBaseObject adbBaseObject)) ? null : adbBaseObject.Value(id, currentIndex + 1));
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public void RestoreValue(object[] id, int currentIndex, object newValue)
		{
			if (!Dead)
			{
				GetValueObject(id, currentIndex, create: true)?.RestoreValue(id, currentIndex + 1, newValue);
			}
		}

				GetValueObject(id, currentIndex, create: true)?.SetValue(id, currentIndex + 1, newValue);
			}
		}

		public object ScalarValue(string guid, out string units)
		{
			units = null;
			object result = null;
			if (!Dead)
			{
				IAdbBaseObject valueObject = GetValueObject(Guid.Parse(guid), create: false);
				if (valueObject != null)
				{
					result = valueObject.Value(new object[0], 0);
					AdbFieldAttribute fieldAttribute = GetFieldAttribute(Guid.Parse(guid));
					if (fieldAttribute != null)
					{
						if (fieldAttribute.DimensionVariable != null)
						{
							object value = fieldAttribute.DimensionVariable.GetValue(this);
							if (value != null)
							{
								try
								{
									units = ((int)value).ToString();
								}
								catch
								{
								}
							}
						}
						else
						{
							int physicalType = (int)fieldAttribute.Attribute.PhysicalType;
							if (physicalType != 0)
							{
								units = physicalType.ToString();
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public IAdbRecord ScalarReference(string guid)
		{
			string units;
			return (IAdbRecord)ScalarValue(guid, out units);
		}

		public void SetScalarValue(string guid, object newValue)
		{
			if (!Dead)
			{
				IAdbBaseObject valueObject = GetValueObject(Guid.Parse(guid), create: true);
				if (valueObject != null)
				{
					object obj = valueObject.Value(new object[0], 0);
					if (newValue != null)
					{
						if (!newValue.Equals(obj) || (obj != null && (obj.Equals(-32767) || obj.Equals(1E+38) || obj.Equals("-32767"))))
						{
							AdbFieldAttribute adbFieldAttribute = ClassInfo.fields[new Guid(guid)];
							if (newValue.Equals(-32767) || newValue.Equals(1E+38) || newValue.Equals("-32767"))
							{
								if (adbFieldAttribute.F.FieldType == typeof(int))
								{
									valueObject.SetValue(new object[0], 0, 99999999);
								}
								else if (adbFieldAttribute.F.FieldType == typeof(double))
								{
									valueObject.SetValue(new object[0], 0, 1E+38);
								}
								else
								{
									valueObject.SetValue(new object[0], 0, null);
								}
							}
							else
							{
								valueObject.SetValue(new object[0], 0, newValue);
								Top.NotifyOnValueChange(this, guid, newValue, obj);
							}
							Dirty = true;
						}
					}
					else if (obj != null)
					{
						valueObject.SetValue(new object[0], 0, null);
						Top.NotifyOnValueChange(this, guid, newValue, obj);
						Dirty = true;
					}
				}
			}
			if (Top.IsReadingOutput < CreatedForOutput)
			{
				CreatedForOutput = Top.IsReadingOutput;
			}
		}

		public object ExecuteMethod(object[] id, int currentIndex, object[] arguments)
		{
			object obj = null;
			if (!Dead)
			{
				if (currentIndex == id.Length - 1)
				{
					if (string.Compare((string)id[currentIndex], AdbArrayMethodGuids.Delete, ignoreCase: true) == 0)
					{
						CleanUp();
						DeleteMe(-1L);
					}
					else
					{
						obj = GetMethodAttribute(Guid.Parse((string)id[currentIndex])).M.Invoke(this, arguments);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					IAdbBaseObject valueObject = GetValueObject(id, currentIndex, create: true);
					if (valueObject != null)
					{
						string text = null;
						if (id.Length > 1 && id[1] != null)
						{
							text = id[1].ToString();
							if (text == AdbArrayMethodGuids.RemoveAtLocation)
							{
								object id2 = valueObject.ExecuteMethod(new object[1] { AdbArrayMethodGuids.IdAtLocation }, 0, arguments);
								Top.NotifyOnArrayRemoveAt(this, id[0].ToString(), (arguments.Length != 0) ? (arguments[0] as int?) : ((int?)null), id2);
							}
							else if (text == AdbArrayMethodGuids.RemoveAt)
							{
								object obj2 = valueObject.ExecuteMethod(new object[1] { AdbArrayMethodGuids.FindId }, 0, arguments);
								if (obj2 != null)
								{
									Top.NotifyOnArrayRemoveAt(this, id[0].ToString(), obj2 as int?, (arguments.Length != 0) ? arguments[0] : null);
								}
							}
						}
						obj = valueObject.ExecuteMethod(id, currentIndex + 1, arguments);
						if (id.Length > 1 && id[1] != null)
						{
							text = id[1].ToString();
							if (text == AdbArrayMethodGuids.Append)
							{
								Top.NotifyOnArrayAppend(this, id[0].ToString(), (arguments.Length != 0) ? arguments[0] : null, (arguments.Length > 1) ? arguments[1] : null);
							}
							else if (text == AdbArrayMethodGuids.InsertBeforeLocation)
							{
								Top.NotifyOnArrayInsertBefore(this, id[0].ToString(), (arguments.Length != 0) ? (arguments[0] as int?) : ((int?)null), (arguments.Length > 1) ? arguments[1] : null, (arguments.Length > 2) ? arguments[2] : null);
							}
							else if (text == AdbArrayMethodGuids.SetValueAt)
							{
								if (obj != null)
								{
									Top.NotifyOnArraySetValueAt(this, id[0].ToString(), obj as int?, (arguments.Length > 1) ? arguments[1] : null);
								}
							}
							else if (text == AdbArrayMethodGuids.SetValueAtLocation)
							{
								Top.NotifyOnArraySetValueAt(this, id[0].ToString(), (arguments.Length != 0) ? (arguments[0] as int?) : ((int?)null), (arguments.Length > 1) ? arguments[1] : null);
							}
							else if (text == AdbArrayMethodGuids.SetId)
							{
								Top.NotifyOnArraySetId(this, id[0].ToString(), (arguments.Length != 0) ? (arguments[0] as int?) : ((int?)null), (arguments.Length > 1) ? arguments[1] : null);
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			return obj;
		}

		public void DeleteMe(long changeNumber = -1L)
		{
			Dead = true;
			Top.NotifyOnRecordDelete(this);
			Top.RecordMap.Remove(MyGuid, changeNumber);
		}

		public virtual bool DeleteAllOutput(int mask = 3)
		{
			bool result = false;
			if (!Reachable)
			{
				Reachable = true;
				foreach (KeyValuePair<Guid, AdbFieldAttribute> item in GetFieldCache)
				{
					AdbFieldAttribute value = item.Value;
					AdbValueDescriptor.ValueType vt = GetVt(value.F.FieldType);
					GetValueObjectBase(value, vt, create: false)?.DeleteAllOutput(mask);
				}
				if ((CreatedForOutput & mask) != 0 && !ExemptFromThisPurge)
				{
					result = true;
					ExecuteMethod(new object[1] { AdbArrayMethodGuids.Delete }, 0, null);
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public virtual void Traverse()
		{
			if (Reachable)
			{
				return;
			}
			Reachable = true;
			if (Dead)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (KeyValuePair<Guid, AdbFieldAttribute> item in GetFieldCache)
			{
				if (item.Value.Attribute.Getter == null && item.Value.Attribute.Setter == null)
				{
					_ = item.Value.F.FieldType;
					object value = item.Value.F.GetValue(this);
					if (value is IAdbBaseObject)
					{
						(value as IAdbBaseObject).Traverse();
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public virtual void Clear()
		{
			if (Dead)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (KeyValuePair<Guid, AdbFieldAttribute> item in GetFieldCache)
			{
				Type fieldType = item.Value.F.FieldType;
				if (fieldType.IsGenericType && fieldType.GetGenericTypeDefinition() == typeof(Nullable<>))
				{
					item.Value.F.SetValue(this, null);
				}
				else if (fieldType.IsSubclassOf(typeof(AdbBaseRecord)))
				{
					item.Value.F.SetValue(this, null);
				}
				else if (fieldType == typeof(string) || fieldType == typeof(string))
				{
					item.Value.F.SetValue(this, null);
				}
				else if (fieldType.IsGenericType && fieldType.GetGenericTypeDefinition() == typeof(AdbBaseArray<, >) && item.Value.F.GetValue(this) is IAdbBaseObject adbBaseObject)
				{
					adbBaseObject.ExecuteMethod(new object[1] { AdbArrayMethodGuids.Clear }, 0, null);
				}
			}
		}

		public virtual void CleanUp()
		{
		}

		private void registerInRecordMap()
		{
			if (ShouldBeInRecordMap)
			{
				if (_top.RecordMap.TryGetValue(_myGuid, out var value))
				{
					value.Current = true;
					value.Record = this;
					return;
				}
				AdbClassInfo adbClassInfo = _top.RecordsClassTable(this);
				AdbRecordRef value2 = new AdbRecordRef(this);
				_top.RecordMap[_myGuid] = value2;
				adbClassInfo.classRecordMap[_myGuid] = value2;
			}
		}
