		public IArgumentData GetArgs(XElement argElem, bool includeNullData)
		{
			//IL_002d: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0033: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_001c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0022: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_052c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0533: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_05e8: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_05ef: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0475: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_047c: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_04af: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_04b6: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0566: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_056d: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_054b: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0552: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_06cf: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_06d6: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0610: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0617: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0494: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_049b: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_04d7: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_04de: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_058e: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0595: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_04f2: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_04f9: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_05ae: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_05b5: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_050c: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0513: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_05cd: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_05d4: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_06be: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_06c5: Expected O, but got Unknown
			IArgumentData argumentData = null;
			IEnumerable<XElement> enumerable = argElem.Elements("Arg");
			if (enumerable.Count() == 0)
			{
				return (IArgumentData)new ArgumentObjectData((object)this);
			}
			ArgumentArrayData val = new ArgumentArrayData(enumerable.Count());
			int num = 0;
			foreach (XElement item in enumerable)
			{
				string value = item.Attribute("path").Value;
				string value2 = item.Attribute("datatype").Value;
				IArgumentData argumentData2 = null;
				object obj = null;
				AdbFieldAttribute fieldAttribute = GetFieldAttribute(value);
				obj = ((fieldAttribute.ArrayBase != null) ? ((IAdbBaseObject)fieldAttribute.ArrayBase.GetValue(this)) : ((!(fieldAttribute.GetterMethod != null)) ? fieldAttribute.F.GetValue(this) : fieldAttribute.GetterMethod.Invoke(this, null)));
				switch (value2)
				{
				case "double":
				case "Double":
					if (includeNullData)
					{
						argumentData2 = (IArgumentData)new ArgumentRealDataOrDefault((double?)(double)obj, (object)string.Empty);
					}
					else if (obj != null)
					{
						argumentData2 = (IArgumentData)new ArgumentRealData((double?)(double)obj);
					}
					break;
				case "double?":
				case "Double?":
					if (includeNullData)
					{
						argumentData2 = (IArgumentData)new ArgumentRealDataOrDefault((double?)obj, (object)string.Empty);
					}
					else if (obj != null)
					{
						argumentData2 = (IArgumentData)new ArgumentRealData((double?)((double?)obj).Value);
					}
					break;
				case "string":
				case "String":
					if (includeNullData)
					{
						argumentData2 = (IArgumentData)new ArgumentStringDataOrDefault((string)obj, (object)string.Empty);
					}
					else if (obj != null)
					{
						argumentData2 = (IArgumentData)new ArgumentStringData((string)obj);
					}
					break;
				case "int":
				case "int32":
				case "Int":
				case "Int32":
					if (includeNullData)
					{
						argumentData2 = (IArgumentData)new ArgumentIntDataOrDefault((int?)(int)obj, (object)string.Empty);
					}
					else if (obj != null)
					{
						argumentData2 = (IArgumentData)new ArgumentIntData((int?)(int)obj);
					}
					break;
				case "int?":
				case "int32?":
				case "Int?":
				case "Int32?":
					if (includeNullData)
					{
						argumentData2 = (IArgumentData)new ArgumentIntDataOrDefault((int?)obj, (object)string.Empty);
					}
					else if (obj != null)
					{
						argumentData2 = (IArgumentData)new ArgumentIntData((int?)((int?)obj).Value);
					}
					break;
				case "bool":
				case "Bool":
				case "Boolean":
					if (includeNullData)
					{
						argumentData2 = (IArgumentData)new ArgumentBoolDataOrDefault((bool?)(bool)obj, (object)string.Empty);
					}
					else if (obj != null)
					{
						argumentData2 = (IArgumentData)new ArgumentBoolData((bool?)(bool)obj);
					}
					break;
				case "bool?":
				case "Bool?":
				case "Boolean?":
					if (includeNullData)
					{
						argumentData2 = (IArgumentData)new ArgumentBoolDataOrDefault((bool?)obj, (object)string.Empty);
					}
					else if (obj != null)
					{
						argumentData2 = (IArgumentData)new ArgumentBoolData((bool?)((bool?)obj).Value);
					}
					break;
				case "Reference":
					if (obj != null)
					{
						argumentData2 = ((AdbBaseRecord)obj).GetArgs(item, includeNullData);
					}
					break;
				case "Array":
					if (obj != null)
					{
						argumentData2 = ((UnTypedArray)obj).GetArgs(item, item.Attribute("arraydatatype").Value, includeNullData);
					}
					break;
				case "AdbIOReal":
					if ((AdbIOReal)obj != null)
					{
						string[] array = new string[2] { "Calculated", "Input" };
						AdbIOReal obj2 = (AdbIOReal)obj;
						object[] id = array;
						double? num2 = obj2.Value(id, 2) as double?;
						if (num2.HasValue)
						{
							argumentData2 = (IArgumentData)new ArgumentRealData((double?)num2.Value);
						}
					}
					break;
				default:
					if (includeNullData)
					{
						argumentData2 = (IArgumentData)new ArgumentStringData(string.Empty);
					}
					break;
				}
				val.SetValue(num, argumentData2);
				val.SetId(num, value);
				num++;
			}
			return (IArgumentData)val;
		}

		private void Die()
		{
			((AdbBaseRecord)null).Die();
		}

		public bool StoreData(StorePathNode path, IArgumentData argData, StringBuilder formattedData)
		{
			bool flag = false;
			if (path != null)
			{
				if (path.IsEnumerator)
				{
					Die();
				}
				else if (path.ConstantValue.Equals(Guid.Empty))
				{
					StringBuilder stringBuilder = null;
					if (SavedData != null)
					{
						stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
					}
					flag = StoreData(path.Next, argData, stringBuilder);
					if (flag && SavedData != null)
					{
						SavedData.Add(stringBuilder.ToString());
					}
				}
				else
				{
					IAdbBaseObject valueObject = GetValueObject(path.ConstantValue, create: true);
					if (valueObject != null)
					{
						IAdsDataStorer val = (IAdsDataStorer)((valueObject is IAdsDataStorer) ? valueObject : null);
						if (val != null)
						{
							int num = 0;
							if (formattedData != null)
							{
								num = formattedData.Length;
								if (num > 0)
								{
									formattedData.Append(".");
								}
								formattedData.Append(path.NodeName);
							}
							flag = val.StoreData(path, argData, formattedData);
							if (!flag && formattedData != null)
							{
								formattedData.Length = num;
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			return flag;
		}
	}
	public static class AdbExtensions
	{
		public static object Create(this Type t, AdbBaseRecord parent)
		{
			return t.Create(parent.Top, parent, null);
		}

		public static object Create(this Type t, AdbTop top, AdbBaseRecord parent, Guid? guid, int output = 0)
		{
			object obj = null;
			if (t.IsSubclassOf(typeof(AdbBaseRecord)))
			{
				ConstructorInfo constructor = t.GetConstructor(new Type[2]
				{
					typeof(AdbTop),
					typeof(Guid?)
				});
				if (constructor != null)
				{
					obj = constructor.Invoke(new object[2] { top, guid });
					if (output != 0 && obj is AdbBaseRecord adbBaseRecord)
					{
						adbBaseRecord.CreatedForOutput = output;
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				ConstructorInfo constructor2 = t.GetConstructor(new Type[0]);
				if (constructor2 != null)
				{
					obj = constructor2.Invoke(null);
				}
				else
				{
					constructor2 = t.GetConstructor(new Type[2]
					{
						typeof(AdbTop),
						typeof(AdbBaseRecord)
					});
					if (constructor2 != null)
					{
						obj = constructor2.Invoke(new object[2] { top, parent });
					}
				}
			}
			return obj;
		}
	}

			public bool Remove(Guid guid, long changeNumber = -1L)
			{
				if (changeNumber == -1)
				{
					changeNumber = parent.NextChangeNumber++;
				}
				parent.DeletedRecords.Add(new KeyValuePair<long, Guid>(changeNumber, guid));
				if (TryGetValue(guid, out var value))
				{
					parent.RecordsClassTable(value.Record)?.classRecordMap.Remove(guid);
				}
				return base.Remove(guid);
			}
		}

		{

			}
		}

		protected void ConnectToDataBase()
		{
			if (!AdbOptions.HasOption("database"))
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				Assembly assembly = Assembly.LoadFrom("AdbDB.dll");
				if (assembly != null)
				{
					Type type = assembly.GetType("AspenTech.AdbDB.Dao.TopRecordDao");
					if (type != null)
					{
						type.GetMethods(BindingFlags.Static | BindingFlags.Public);
						DbUpdate = type.GetMethod("Update", BindingFlags.Static | BindingFlags.Public);
						DbCreate = type.GetMethod("Create", BindingFlags.Static | BindingFlags.Public);
					}
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
		}

		public IAdbRecord CreateRecord(string guid)
		{
			IAdbRecord result = null;
			if (guid.StartsWith("{"))
			{
				guid = guid.Substring(1, 36);
			}
			if (Guid.TryParse(guid, out var _))
			{
				result = CreateRecord(Guid.Parse(guid), null);
			}
			return result;
		}

		public AdbBaseRecord CreateRecord(Guid guid, Guid? objGuid)
		{
			if (guid.Equals(_topRecordGuid) && _topRecord != null)
			{
				return (AdbBaseRecord)_topRecord;
			}
			AdbBaseRecord adbBaseRecord = null;
			typeTable value2;
			if (_recordMap.TryGetValue(guid, out var value))
			{
				adbBaseRecord = value.Record;
			}
			else if (_classTypes.TryGetValue(guid, out value2))
			{
				adbBaseRecord = (AdbBaseRecord)value2.type.Create(this, null, objGuid);
				adbBaseRecord.CreatedForOutput = IsReadingOutput;
			}
			return adbBaseRecord;
		}

		public AdbClassInfo RecordsClassTable(AdbBaseRecord newrec)

		}

		public IAdbRecord TopRecord()

				if (_classTypes.TryGetValue(_topRecordDef.TopRecordGuid(), out var value))

		}

		public static string CleanForXML(string instr)
		{
			return instr;
		}

		private long GetChangeNumber(XmlReader reader)
		{
			long result = -1L;
			string attribute = reader.GetAttribute("change");
			if (!string.IsNullOrWhiteSpace(attribute) && !long.TryParse(attribute, out result))
			{
				result = -1L;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public void Write(List<AdbGuidCache.RecordClassGuidInfo> rcgis, AdbGuidCache guidCache, StreamWriter sw, StreamWriter swreadable, long incremental)
		{
			if (incremental >= 0)
			{
				sw.Write("\r\n <Deletes>");
				swreadable?.Write("\r\n <Deletes>");
				int num = DeletedRecords.BinarySearch(new KeyValuePair<long, Guid>(incremental, Guid.Empty), kvCompare);
				if (num < 0)
				{
					num = ~num;
				}
				foreach (KeyValuePair<long, Guid> item in DeletedRecords.Skip(num))
				{
					sw.Write("\r\n  <Delete record=\"" + item.Value.ToString() + "\" count=\"" + item.Key + "\"/>");
					swreadable?.Write("\r\n  <Delete record=\"" + item.Value.ToString() + "\" count=\"" + item.Key + "\"/>");
				}
				sw.Write("\r\n </Deletes>");
			}
			sw.Write("\r\n <Records>");
			swreadable?.Write("\r\n <Records>");
			foreach (AdbGuidCache.RecordClassGuidInfo rcgi in rcgis)
			{
				if (rcgi.record.ClassGuid.ToString().Equals(AdbUnknownGuid.ToString(), StringComparison.OrdinalIgnoreCase))
				{
					rcgi.record.Write(sw, guidCache, swreadable);
					continue;
				}
				sw.Write("\r\n  <Record typeGuid=\"{0}\" guid=\"{1}\" change=\"{2}\">", rcgi.classIndex, rcgi.recordIndex, rcgi.record.LastChangeNumber.ToString());
				swreadable?.Write("\r\n  <Record type\"{0}\" guid=\"{1}\" change=\"{2}\">", CleanForXML(rcgi.classType.Name), rcgi.recordIndex, rcgi.record.LastChangeNumber.ToString());
				rcgi.record.Write(sw, guidCache, swreadable);
				sw.Write("</Record>");
			}
			sw.Write("\r\n </Records>");
			sw.Write("\r\n <Synchronize>" + NextChangeNumber + "</Synchronize>");
			swreadable?.Write("\r\n <Synchronize>" + NextChangeNumber + "</Synchronize>");
		}

		protected void Read(StatusSection section, ExpandableMemoryMappedFile dataFile, ExpandableMemoryMappedFile guidCacheFile)
		{
			AdbGuidCache adbGuidCache = new AdbGuidCache();
			Fixups = new AdbReferenceFixups(this);
			adbGuidCache.Fixups = Fixups;
			using (MemoryMappedViewStream memoryMappedViewStream = guidCacheFile.CreateViewStream())
			{
				memoryMappedViewStream.Seek(section.GuidFileOffset, SeekOrigin.Begin);
				using StreamReader input = new StreamReader(memoryMappedViewStream);
				XmlReader xmlReader = XmlReader.Create(input);
				bool flag = false;
				bool flag2 = true;
				while (!flag && flag2)
				{
					try
					{
						flag2 = xmlReader.Read();
						if (xmlReader.NodeType == XmlNodeType.Element && xmlReader.Name == "GuidDictionary")
						{
							adbGuidCache.Read(xmlReader);
							flag = true;
						}
					}
					catch (XmlException)
					{
					}
				}
			}
			using MemoryMappedViewStream memoryMappedViewStream2 = dataFile.CreateViewStream();
			memoryMappedViewStream2.Seek(section.DataFileOffset, SeekOrigin.Begin);
			using StreamReader input2 = new StreamReader(memoryMappedViewStream2);
			XmlReader xmlReader2 = XmlReader.Create(input2);
			while (xmlReader2.Read() && (!"AdsIncremental".Equals(xmlReader2.Name) || xmlReader2.NodeType != XmlNodeType.EndElement))
			{
				if (xmlReader2.NodeType != XmlNodeType.Element)
				{
					continue;
				}
				string name = xmlReader2.Name;
				if (!(name == "Deletes"))
				{
					if (name == "Record")
					{
						ReadRecord(xmlReader2, adbGuidCache);
					}
					continue;
				}
				while (xmlReader2.NodeType != XmlNodeType.EndElement)
				{
					xmlReader2.Read();
					if (xmlReader2.NodeType == XmlNodeType.Element)
					{
						Guid guid = Guid.Parse(xmlReader2.GetAttribute("record"));
						long changeNumber = GetChangeNumber(xmlReader2);
						if (_recordMap.TryGetValue(guid, out var value))
						{
							value.Record.DeleteMe(changeNumber);
						}
						else
						{
							DeletedRecords.Add(new KeyValuePair<long, Guid>(changeNumber, guid));
						}
						NextChangeNumber = Math.Max(changeNumber + 1, NextChangeNumber);
						if (AdbSynchronizer.ShouldWriteSyncStuff)
						{
							DebugLog.WriteDebug("              Delete Record changeNumber = " + changeNumber + "  MyNextChange = " + NextChangeNumber);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			Fixups.DoFixups(removeUntouched: false);
		}

		protected void Read(Stream fs)
		{
			if (fs != null && fs.Length > 0)
			{
				try
				{
					Stream stream = null;
					byte[] array = new byte[16];
					byte[] array2 = new byte[12]
					{
						0, 0, 0, 52, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0,
						0, 0
					};
					array[8] = 56;
					array[4] = 95;
					array2[0] = 103;
					array[12] = 254;
					array2[6] = 152;
					array[10] = 155;
					array[7] = 95;
					array2[11] = 2;
					array[2] = 168;
					array2[7] = 165;
					array2[9] = 149;
					array[5] = 142;
					array2[2] = 244;
					array[15] = 249;
					array2[8] = 115;
					array[1] = 87;
					array2[1] = 222;
					array[14] = 34;
					array2[5] = 31;
					array[13] = 135;
					array[0] = 21;
					array2[4] = 135;
					array[3] = 108;
					array[11] = 68;
					array[6] = 20;
					array2[10] = 5;
					array[9] = 90;
					byte[] array3 = new byte[1];
					fs.Read(array3, 0, 1);
					if (array3[0] == 136)
					{
						TripleDESCryptoServiceProvider tripleDESCryptoServiceProvider = new TripleDESCryptoServiceProvider();
						tripleDESCryptoServiceProvider.Mode = CipherMode.ECB;
						tripleDESCryptoServiceProvider.Padding = PaddingMode.PKCS7;
						tripleDESCryptoServiceProvider.Key = array;
						int num = (int)fs.Length - 1;
						byte[] array4 = new byte[num];
						fs.Read(array4, 0, num);
						byte[] buffer;
						try
						{
							buffer = tripleDESCryptoServiceProvider.CreateDecryptor().TransformFinalBlock(array4, 0, num);
						}
						finally
						{
							tripleDESCryptoServiceProvider.Clear();
						}
						stream = new DeflateStream(new MemoryStream(buffer), CompressionMode.Decompress);
					}
					else if (array3[0] == 147 || array3[0] == 149)
					{
						if (array3[0] == 149)
						{
							array = new SHA256CryptoServiceProvider().ComputeHash(array2);
						}
						TripleDESCryptoServiceProvider tripleDESCryptoServiceProvider2 = new TripleDESCryptoServiceProvider();
						tripleDESCryptoServiceProvider2.Mode = CipherMode.CBC;
						tripleDESCryptoServiceProvider2.Padding = PaddingMode.PKCS7;
						tripleDESCryptoServiceProvider2.Key = array;
						byte[] array5 = new byte[tripleDESCryptoServiceProvider2.BlockSize / 8];
						fs.Read(array5, 0, tripleDESCryptoServiceProvider2.BlockSize / 8);
						tripleDESCryptoServiceProvider2.IV = array5;
						ICryptoTransform transform = tripleDESCryptoServiceProvider2.CreateDecryptor();
						stream = new DeflateStream(new CryptoStream(fs, transform, CryptoStreamMode.Read), CompressionMode.Decompress);
					}
					else if (array3[0] == 150)
					{
						stream = new DeflateStream(fs, CompressionMode.Decompress);
					}
					else
					{
						fs.Seek(0L, SeekOrigin.Begin);
						stream = fs;
					}
					XmlReader xmlReader = XmlReader.Create(stream);
					AdbGuidCache adbGuidCache = new AdbGuidCache();
					Fixups = new AdbReferenceFixups(this);
					adbGuidCache.Fixups = Fixups;
					foreach (KeyValuePair<Guid, AdbRecordRef> item in RecordMap)
					{
						item.Value.Current = false;
					}
					UnknownFieldGuids.Clear();
					while (xmlReader.Read())
					{
						if (xmlReader.NodeType != XmlNodeType.Element)
						{
							continue;
						}
						switch (xmlReader.Name)
						{
						case "GuidDictionary":
							adbGuidCache.Read(xmlReader);
							break;
						case "Record":
							ReadRecord(xmlReader, adbGuidCache);
							break;
						case "Synchronize":
						{
							xmlReader.Read();
							if (long.TryParse(xmlReader.Value, out var result))
							{
								NextChangeNumber = result;
								if (AdbSynchronizer.ShouldWriteSyncStuff)
								{
									DebugLog.WriteDebug("      Read Synchronizer " + NextChangeNumber);
								}
							}
							break;
						}
						}
					}
					Fixups.DoFixups(removeUntouched: true);
				}
				catch (XmlException)
				{
					fs.Close();
					throw;
				}
				catch (FormatException)
				{
					fs.Close();
					throw;
				}
				catch (InvalidCastException)
				{
					fs.Close();
					throw;
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					fs.Close();
					throw;
				}
			}
			fs.Close();
		}

		protected void Read(string connectionString)
		{
			_filename = connectionString;
			if (File.Exists(connectionString))
			{
				using (Stream fs = new FileStream(connectionString, FileMode.Open, FileAccess.Read))
				{
					Read(fs);
				}
			}
		}

		protected void ReadRecord(XmlReader reader, AdbGuidCache guidCache)
		{
			int index = int.Parse(reader["guid"]);
			int index2 = int.Parse(reader["typeGuid"]);
			Guid guidForIndex = guidCache.GetGuidForIndex(index);
			Guid guidForIndex2 = guidCache.GetGuidForIndex(index2);
			long changeNumber = GetChangeNumber(reader);
			AdbBaseRecord adbBaseRecord = null;
			if (RecordMap.TryGetValue(guidForIndex, out var value))
			{
				adbBaseRecord = value.Record;
				value.Current = true;
				if (string.Compare(((GuidAttribute)adbBaseRecord.GetType().GetCustomAttributes(typeof(GuidAttribute), inherit: false)[0]).Value, guidForIndex2.ToString(), ignoreCase: true) != 0)
				{
					adbBaseRecord = null;
					value.Current = false;
				}
				else
				{
					adbBaseRecord.Clear();
				}
			}
			if (adbBaseRecord == null)
			{
				long num = -1L;
				if (changeNumber != -1)
				{
					num = NextChangeNumber;
				}
				adbBaseRecord = CreateRecord(guidForIndex2, guidForIndex);
				if (adbBaseRecord == null)
				{
					adbBaseRecord = CreateRecord(AdbUnknownGuid, guidForIndex);
				}
				if (num != -1)
				{
					adbBaseRecord.LastChangeNumber = changeNumber;
					NextChangeNumber = num;
				}
			}
			if (guidForIndex2.Equals(_topRecordGuid))
			{
				_topRecord = adbBaseRecord;
			}
			adbBaseRecord.Read(reader, guidCache, changeNumber);
		}

		public void MoveBlock(string oldPath, string flowsheetPath)

			IAdbRecord adbRecord = TopRecord();
			object[] path = oldPath.Split('.');
			IAdbRecord adbRecord2 = adbRecord.Reference(path);
			IAdbRecord adbRecord3 = TopRecord();
			path = flowsheetPath.Split('.');
			IAdbRecord adbRecord4 = adbRecord3.Reference(path);
			if (adbRecord2 != null && adbRecord4 != null)
			{
				string text = oldPath.Substring(oldPath.LastIndexOf('.') + 1);
				adbRecord4.ExecutePath(new object[1] { "e8494da9-075f-4628-8519-c40f99fb9986" }, new object[2]
				{
					adbRecord2.RecordGuid(),
					text
				});
			}
		}

		public void PurgeCacheIncremental(SyncStatus status, string dataFileName, ExpandableMemoryMappedFile dataFile, ExpandableMemoryMappedFile guidCacheFile)
		{
			long nextChangeNumber = status.NextChangeNumber;
			if (nextChangeNumber == 0L)
			{
				PurgeCache();
			}
			bool flag = false;
			StreamWriter streamWriter = null;
			if (AdbOptions.HasOption("SaveText"))
			{
				flag = true;
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				try
				{
					string extension = Path.GetExtension(dataFileName);
					streamWriter = new StreamWriter(Path.ChangeExtension(dataFileName, extension + ".txt"));
				}
				catch
				{
					streamWriter = null;
					flag = false;
				}
			}
			List<AdbGuidCache.RecordClassGuidInfo> list = new List<AdbGuidCache.RecordClassGuidInfo>();
			AdbGuidCache adbGuidCache = new AdbGuidCache();
			foreach (KeyValuePair<Guid, AdbRecordRef> item in RecordMap)
			{
				if (nextChangeNumber < 0 || item.Value.Record.LastChangeNumber >= nextChangeNumber)
				{
					list.Add(adbGuidCache.AddRecordGuids(item.Key, item.Value));
				}
			}
			StatusSection statusSection = new StatusSection();
			statusSection.DataFileOffset = status.NextOpenDataLocation;
			statusSection.GuidFileOffset = status.NextOpenGuidLocation;
			bool flag2 = false;
			while (!flag2)
			{
				using MemoryMappedViewStream memoryMappedViewStream = dataFile.CreateViewStream();
				memoryMappedViewStream.Seek(status.NextOpenDataLocation, SeekOrigin.Begin);
				using StreamWriter streamWriter2 = new StreamWriter(memoryMappedViewStream);
				try
				{
					streamWriter2.Write("<AdsIncremental>");
					Write(list, adbGuidCache, streamWriter2, streamWriter, nextChangeNumber);
					streamWriter2.Write("</AdsIncremental>");
					streamWriter2.Flush();
					flag2 = true;
					statusSection.DataFileOffset = status.NextOpenDataLocation;
					status.NextOpenDataLocation = memoryMappedViewStream.Position;
					statusSection.DataFileLength = status.NextOpenDataLocation - statusSection.DataFileOffset;
				}
				catch (NotSupportedException)
				{
					dataFile.Expand();
				}
				statusSection.NewDataFileExpandCount = dataFile.ExpandCount;
			}
			flag2 = false;
			while (!flag2)
			{
				using MemoryMappedViewStream memoryMappedViewStream2 = guidCacheFile.CreateViewStream();
				memoryMappedViewStream2.Seek(status.NextOpenGuidLocation, SeekOrigin.Begin);
				using StreamWriter streamWriter3 = new StreamWriter(memoryMappedViewStream2);
				try
				{
					adbGuidCache.Write(streamWriter3);
					streamWriter3.Flush();
					statusSection.GuidFileOffset = status.NextOpenGuidLocation;
					status.NextOpenGuidLocation = memoryMappedViewStream2.Position;
					statusSection.GuidFileLength = status.NextOpenGuidLocation - statusSection.GuidFileOffset;
					flag2 = true;
				}
				catch (NotSupportedException)
				{
					guidCacheFile.Expand();
				}
				statusSection.NewGuidFileExpandCount = guidCacheFile.ExpandCount;
			}
			statusSection.FirstChangeNumber = status.NextChangeNumber;
			statusSection.LastChangeNumber = NextChangeNumber - 1;
			status.NextChangeNumber = NextChangeNumber;
			status.Sections.Add(statusSection);
			status.NumberOfSections++;
			if (streamWriter != null)
			{
				streamWriter.Flush();
				streamWriter.Close();
				streamWriter.Dispose();
			}
		}

		public void PurgeCache()
		{
			PurgeCacheIncremental(-1L);
		}

		public void PurgeCacheIncremental(long incremental)
		{
			try
			{
				string extension = Path.GetExtension(_filename);
				IsBlocked = true;
				if (_filename == null)
				{
					return;
				}
				bool flag = false;
				bool flag2 = true;
				StreamWriter streamWriter = null;
				if (AdbOptions.HasOption("SaveText"))
				{
					flag = true;
				}
				flag2 = false;
				if (flag)
				{
					try
					{
						streamWriter = new StreamWriter(Path.ChangeExtension(_filename, extension + ".txt"));
					}
					catch (ArgumentException)
					{
						streamWriter = null;
						flag = false;
					}
				}
				List<AdbGuidCache.RecordClassGuidInfo> list = new List<AdbGuidCache.RecordClassGuidInfo>();
				AdbGuidCache adbGuidCache = new AdbGuidCache();
				foreach (KeyValuePair<Guid, AdbRecordRef> item in RecordMap)
				{
					if (incremental < 0 || item.Value.Record.LastChangeNumber >= incremental)
					{
						list.Add(adbGuidCache.AddRecordGuids(item.Key, item.Value));
					}
				}
				if (_classTypes.TryGetValue(AdbUnknownGuid, out var value))
				{
					foreach (KeyValuePair<Guid, AdbRecordRef> item2 in value.classInfo.classRecordMap)
					{
						Guid guid = (Guid)item2.Value.Record.GetType().GetProperty("TypeGuid").GetValue(item2.Value.Record);
						adbGuidCache.GetIndexForGuid(guid);
					}
				}
				foreach (Guid unknownFieldGuid in UnknownFieldGuids)
				{
					adbGuidCache.GetIndexForGuid(unknownFieldGuid);
				}
				if (flag2)
				{
					SHA256 sHA = new SHA256CryptoServiceProvider();
					byte[] array = new byte[12];
					array[3] = 52;
					array[0] = 103;
					array[6] = 152;
					array[11] = 2;
					array[7] = 165;
					array[9] = 149;
					array[2] = 244;
					array[8] = 115;
					array[1] = 222;
					array[5] = 31;
					array[4] = 135;
					array[10] = 5;
					byte[] key = sHA.ComputeHash(array);
					TripleDESCryptoServiceProvider tripleDESCryptoServiceProvider = new TripleDESCryptoServiceProvider();
					tripleDESCryptoServiceProvider.Mode = CipherMode.CBC;
					tripleDESCryptoServiceProvider.GenerateIV();
					tripleDESCryptoServiceProvider.Padding = PaddingMode.PKCS7;
					tripleDESCryptoServiceProvider.Key = key;
					using FileStream fileStream = new FileStream(_filename, FileMode.Create, FileAccess.ReadWrite);
					fileStream.WriteByte(149);
					fileStream.Write(tripleDESCryptoServiceProvider.IV, 0, tripleDESCryptoServiceProvider.BlockSize / 8);
					try
					{
						ICryptoTransform transform = tripleDESCryptoServiceProvider.CreateEncryptor();
						using CryptoStream cryptoStream = new CryptoStream(fileStream, transform, CryptoStreamMode.Write);
						using DeflateStream stream = new DeflateStream(cryptoStream, CompressionMode.Compress, leaveOpen: true);
						using StreamWriter streamWriter2 = new StreamWriter(stream);
						streamWriter2.Write("<AdbFile>");
						adbGuidCache.Write(streamWriter2);
						Write(list, adbGuidCache, streamWriter2, streamWriter, incremental);
						streamWriter2.Write("</AdbFile>");
						streamWriter2.Flush();
						streamWriter2.Close();
						cryptoStream.Close();
					}
					finally
					{
						tripleDESCryptoServiceProvider.Clear();
						sHA.Clear();
					}
					fileStream.Close();
				}
				else
				{
					bool flag3 = true;
					if (extension.Equals(".bkp", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase) || extension.Equals(".bk$", StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase))
					{
						if (File.Exists(_filename))
						{
							try
							{
								StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
								bool flag4 = false;
								StreamReader streamReader = new StreamReader(_filename, Encoding.Default);
								while (!streamReader.EndOfStream)
								{
									string text = streamReader.ReadLine();
									if (text.StartsWith("$_ADS_FILE"))
									{
										if (!flag4)
										{
											AddAdsFile(stringBuilder, adbGuidCache, list, streamWriter, incremental);
											flag4 = true;
										}
										while (!text.StartsWith("$_END_ADS_FILE"))
										{
											text = streamReader.ReadLine();
										}
									}
									else
									{
										stringBuilder.Append(text).Append("\r\n");
									}
								}
								streamReader.Close();
								if (!flag4)
								{
									AddAdsFile(stringBuilder, adbGuidCache, list, streamWriter, incremental);
								}
								File.WriteAllText(_filename, stringBuilder.ToString(), Encoding.Default);
								string path = Path.ChangeExtension(_filename, ".ads");
								if (File.Exists(path))
								{
									File.Delete(path);
								}
							}
							catch (Exception)
							{
							}
						}
					}
					else
					{
						if (extension.Equals(".xml"))
						{
							flag3 = false;
						}
						if (flag3)
						{
							try
							{
								using FileStream fileStream2 = new FileStream(_filename, FileMode.Create, FileAccess.ReadWrite);
								fileStream2.WriteByte(150);
								using DeflateStream stream2 = new DeflateStream(fileStream2, CompressionMode.Compress, leaveOpen: true);
								using StreamWriter streamWriter3 = new StreamWriter(stream2);
								streamWriter3.Write("<AdbFile>");
								adbGuidCache.Write(streamWriter3);
								Write(list, adbGuidCache, streamWriter3, streamWriter, incremental);
								streamWriter3.Write("</AdbFile>");
								streamWriter3.Flush();
								streamWriter3.Close();
								fileStream2.Close();
							}
							catch
							{
							}
						}
						else
						{
							try
							{
								using StreamWriter streamWriter4 = new StreamWriter(_filename);
								streamWriter4.Write("<AdbFile>");
								adbGuidCache.Write(streamWriter4);
								Write(list, adbGuidCache, streamWriter4, streamWriter, incremental);
								streamWriter4.Write("</AdbFile>");
								streamWriter4.Close();
							}
							catch
							{
							}
						}
					}
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					streamWriter.Close();
					streamWriter.Dispose();
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
		}

		public void PurgeClassTypes()
		{
			_classTypes.Clear();
		}

		public void ClearBacklog(bool purge)
		{
			if (_backlog.Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (DelayedAction item in _backlog)
			{
				item.Execute();
			}
			if (purge)
			{
				PurgeCache();
				IsBlocked = false;
			}
			_backlog = new List<DelayedAction>();
		}

		public void Reconnect(StatusSection section, ExpandableMemoryMappedFile dataFile, ExpandableMemoryMappedFile guidCacheFile)
		{
			IsBlocked = false;
			if (section.FirstChangeNumber >= NextChangeNumber)
			{
				Read(section, dataFile, guidCacheFile);
				ClearBacklog(purge: false);
			}
		}

		public void Reconnect()
		{
			IsBlocked = false;
			if (_filename != null)
			{
				Read(_filename);
			}
			ClearBacklog(purge: true);
		}

		public void CreateGuidDictionary()
		{
			//IL_0095: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_009a: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_00a7: Expected O, but got Unknown
			//IL_0250: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0255: Unknown result type (might be due to invalid IL or missing references)
			//IL_0262: Expected O, but got Unknown
			Assembly executingAssembly = Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly();
			string text = executingAssembly.CodeBase.Replace("file:///", "").Replace('/', '\\');
			text = text.Substring(0, text.LastIndexOf('\\') + 1);
			Type[] types = executingAssembly.GetTypes();
			IAdbDataModel adbDataModel = null;
			Type[] array = types;
			foreach (Type type in array)
			{
				if (type.IsSubclassOf(typeof(ConverterImplementation)))
				{
					object[] customAttributes = type.GetCustomAttributes(typeof(GuidAttribute), inherit: false);
					if (customAttributes.Length == 1)
					{
						Guid key = Guid.Parse(((GuidAttribute)customAttributes[0]).Value);
						((Dictionary<Guid, ImplementedConverterWrapper>)(object)ConverterDictionary).Add(key, new ImplementedConverterWrapper
						{
							ConverterType = type
						});
					}
				}
				if (type.IsSubclassOf(typeof(AdbBaseRecord)))
				{
					object[] customAttributes2 = type.GetCustomAttributes(typeof(GuidAttribute), inherit: false);
					for (int j = 0; j < customAttributes2.Length; j++)
					{
						Guid guid = Guid.Parse(((GuidAttribute)customAttributes2[j]).Value);
						_classTypes[guid] = new typeTable
						{
							type = type,
							classInfo = AdbBaseRecord.CreateFieldCache(type, guid)
						};
					}
				}
				if (type.GetInterface("IAdbDataModel") != null)
				{
					adbDataModel = (IAdbDataModel)type.GetConstructor(new Type[0]).Invoke(new object[0]);
					ConstructorInfo constructor = Assembly.LoadFrom(text + adbDataModel.topRecordDll).GetType(adbDataModel.topRecordClass).GetConstructor(new Type[0]);
					_topRecordDef = (IAdbTopRecordInfo)constructor.Invoke(new object[0]);
					_topRecordGuid = _topRecordDef.TopRecordGuid();
				}
			}
			if (adbDataModel != null)
			{
				string[] dllNames = adbDataModel.dllNames;
				foreach (string text2 in dllNames)
				{
					Assembly assembly = Assembly.LoadFrom(text + text2);
					if (!(assembly != null))
					{
						continue;
					}
					array = assembly.GetTypes();
					foreach (Type type2 in array)
					{
						if (type2.IsSubclassOf(typeof(ConverterImplementation)))
						{
							object[] customAttributes3 = type2.GetCustomAttributes(typeof(GuidAttribute), inherit: false);
							if (customAttributes3.Length == 1)
							{
								Guid key2 = Guid.Parse(((GuidAttribute)customAttributes3[0]).Value);
								((Dictionary<Guid, ImplementedConverterWrapper>)(object)ConverterDictionary).Add(key2, new ImplementedConverterWrapper
								{
									ConverterType = type2
								});
							}
						}
						if (type2.IsSubclassOf(typeof(AdbBaseRecord)))
						{
							object[] customAttributes2 = type2.GetCustomAttributes(typeof(GuidAttribute), inherit: false);
							for (int k = 0; k < customAttributes2.Length; k++)
							{
								Guid guid2 = Guid.Parse(((GuidAttribute)customAttributes2[k]).Value);
								_classTypes[guid2] = new typeTable
								{
									type = type2,
									classInfo = AdbBaseRecord.CreateFieldCache(type2, guid2)
								};
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			foreach (KeyValuePair<Guid, typeTable> classType in _classTypes)
			{
				object[] customAttributes4 = classType.Value.type.BaseType.GetCustomAttributes(typeof(GuidAttribute), inherit: false);
				if (customAttributes4.Length == 1 && _classTypes.TryGetValue(Guid.Parse(((GuidAttribute)customAttributes4[0]).Value), out var value))
				{
					value.classInfo.directChildren.Add(classType.Key);
				}
			}
		}

		public static IAdbTop OpenCreate(string connectionstring, bool forceNew, bool forceExist)
		{
			IAdbTop result = null;
			if (connectionstring.EndsWith(".hsc", ignoreCase: true, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture))
			{
				string val = connectionstring.Substring(0, connectionstring.Length - 4);
				string tempFileName = Path.GetTempFileName();
				if (ExtractHysysCOM(val, tempFileName))
				{
					if (new FileInfo(tempFileName).Length != 0L)
					{
						result = OpenCreate(tempFileName, forceNew: false, forceExist: true);
					}
					File.Delete(tempFileName);
				}
			}
			else if (connectionstring.EndsWith(".apw", ignoreCase: true, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture))
			{
				result = ReadAdbTopFromApwFile(connectionstring);
			}
			else if (connectionstring.EndsWith(".apwz", ignoreCase: true, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture))
			{
				result = ReadAdbTopFromApwzFile(connectionstring);
			}
			else if (connectionstring.EndsWith(".hscz", ignoreCase: true, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture))
			{
				result = ReadAdbTopFromHsczFile(connectionstring);
			}
			else if (connectionstring.EndsWith(".bkp", ignoreCase: true, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture) || connectionstring.EndsWith(".bk$", ignoreCase: true, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture))
			{
				if (File.Exists(connectionstring))
				{
					StreamReader streamReader = null;
					try
					{
						streamReader = new StreamReader(connectionstring);
						result = ReadAdbTopFromBkpStream(streamReader);
					}
					catch
					{
						streamReader?.Close();
					}
					streamReader.Close();
				}
			}
			else if (connectionstring.IndexOf(".mdf(") != -1)
			{
				AdbTop obj2 = new AdbTop
				{
					_filename = connectionstring
				};
				int num = connectionstring.IndexOf(".mdf(") + 4;
				int num2 = connectionstring.LastIndexOf(")");
				string caseFilePath = connectionstring.Substring(num + 1, num2 - num - 1);
				StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
				stringBuilder.Append("Data Source=(LocalDB)\\MSSQLLocalDB;AttachDbFilename=\"");
				stringBuilder.Append(connectionstring.Substring(0, num));
				stringBuilder.Append("\";Integrated Security=SSPI;Connect Timeout=30");
				connectionstring = stringBuilder.ToString();
				obj2.LoadFromDB(connectionstring, caseFilePath);
				result = obj2;
			}
			else
			{
				if (forceNew && File.Exists(connectionstring))
				{
					File.Delete(connectionstring);
				}
				if (!forceExist || File.Exists(connectionstring))
				{
					AdbTop adbTop = new AdbTop();
					adbTop._filename = connectionstring;
					if (!forceNew)
					{
						adbTop.Read(connectionstring);
					}
					result = adbTop;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private void AddAdsFile(StringBuilder sb, AdbGuidCache guidCache, List<AdbGuidCache.RecordClassGuidInfo> writeRecordInfo, StreamWriter swreadable, long incremental)
		{
			sb.Append("$_ADS_FILE\r\n");
			MemoryStream memoryStream = new MemoryStream();
			using (DeflateStream stream = new DeflateStream(memoryStream, CompressionMode.Compress, leaveOpen: true))
			{
				using StreamWriter streamWriter = new StreamWriter(stream);
				streamWriter.Write("<AdbFile>");
				guidCache.Write(streamWriter);
				Write(writeRecordInfo, guidCache, streamWriter, swreadable, incremental);
				streamWriter.Write("</AdbFile>");
				streamWriter.Flush();
				streamWriter.Close();
			}
			string value = Convert.ToBase64String(memoryStream.ToArray());
			sb.Append(value);
			sb.Append("\r\n$_END_ADS_FILE\r\n");
		}

		public static IAdbTop ReadAdbTopFromHsczFile(string connectionstring)
		{
			IAdbTop result = null;
			ZipArchive val = ZipFile.OpenRead(connectionstring);
			try
			{
				foreach (ZipArchiveEntry entry in val.Entries)
				{
					try
					{
						if (!entry.Name.EndsWith(".hsc"))
						{
							continue;
						}
						using Stream stream = entry.Open();
						string tempFileName = Path.GetTempFileName();
						tempFileName = tempFileName.Replace(".tmp", ".hsc");
						using (FileStream destination = new FileStream(tempFileName, FileMode.Append, FileAccess.Write))
						{
							stream.CopyTo(destination);
						}
						string val2 = tempFileName.Substring(0, tempFileName.Length - 4);
						string tempFileName2 = Path.GetTempFileName();
						if (ExtractHysysCOM(val2, tempFileName2))
						{
							if (new FileInfo(tempFileName2).Length != 0L)
							{
								result = OpenCreate(tempFileName2, forceNew: false, forceExist: true);
							}
							File.Delete(tempFileName);
							File.Delete(tempFileName2);
						}
					}
					catch
					{
					}
				}
				return result;
			}
			finally
			{
				((IDisposable)val)?.Dispose();
			}
		}

		public static IAdbTop ReadAdbTopFromApwzFile(string connectionstring)
		{
			IAdbTop adbTop = null;
			ZipArchive val = ZipFile.OpenRead(connectionstring);
			try
			{
				ZipArchiveEntry val2 = null;
				bool flag = false;
				foreach (ZipArchiveEntry entry in val.Entries)
				{
					if (entry.Name.EndsWith(".ads", ignoreCase: true, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture))
					{
						try
						{
							using Stream stream = entry.Open();
							string tempFileName = Path.GetTempFileName();
							using (FileStream destination = new FileStream(tempFileName, FileMode.Append, FileAccess.Write))
							{
								stream.CopyTo(destination);
							}
							AdbTop adbTop2 = new AdbTop();
							adbTop2.Read(tempFileName);
							adbTop = adbTop2;
						}
						catch
						{
						}
						break;
					}
					if (entry.Name.EndsWith(".apw", ignoreCase: true, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture))
					{
						val2 = entry;
						flag = true;
					}
					else if (entry.Name.EndsWith(".bkp", ignoreCase: true, CultureInfo.CurrentCulture))
					{
						val2 = entry;
						flag = false;
					}
				}
				if (adbTop == null && val2 != null)
				{
					try
					{
						using Stream stream2 = val2.Open();
						if (flag)
						{
							string tempFileName2 = Path.GetTempFileName();
							using (FileStream destination2 = new FileStream(tempFileName2, FileMode.Append, FileAccess.Write))
							{
								stream2.CopyTo(destination2);
							}
							adbTop = ReadAdbTopFromApwFile(tempFileName2);
						}
						else
						{
							using StreamReader sr = new StreamReader(stream2);
							adbTop = ReadAdbTopFromBkpStream(sr);
						}
					}
					catch
					{
					}
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				((IDisposable)val)?.Dispose();
			}
			return adbTop;
		}

		private static IAdbTop ReadAdbTopFromApwFile(string connectionstring)
		{
			IAdbTop result = null;
			byte[] ads = null;
			if (AdbStructuredStorageHelper.ExtractApw(connectionstring, out ads) && ads != null)
			{
				using MemoryStream fs = new MemoryStream(ads);
				AdbTop adbTop = new AdbTop();
				adbTop.Read(fs);
				result = adbTop;
			}
			return result;
		}

		private static AdbTop ReadAdbTopFromBkpStream(StreamReader sr)
		{
			AdbTop adbTop = null;
			try
			{
				string text;
				while ((text = sr.ReadLine()) != null)
				{
					if (text.StartsWith("$_ADS_FILE"))
					{
						text = sr.ReadLine();
						byte[] array = Convert.FromBase64String(text);
						string tempFileName = Path.GetTempFileName();
						byte[] array2 = new byte[1] { 150 };
						using (FileStream fileStream = new FileStream(tempFileName, FileMode.Append, FileAccess.Write))
						{
							fileStream.Write(array2, 0, array2.Length);
							fileStream.Write(array, 0, array.Length);
						}
						adbTop = new AdbTop();
						adbTop.Read(tempFileName);
						File.Delete(tempFileName);
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			return adbTop;
		}

		public void Rename(string newName)
		{
			if (string.IsNullOrEmpty(_filename) || !_filename.Equals(newName))
			{
				_filename = newName;
				if (Synchronizer != null)
				{
					Synchronizer.PathToFile = newName;
				}
			}
		}

		public IAdbRecord[] AllRecords()
		{
			List<IAdbRecord> list = new List<IAdbRecord>();
			foreach (AdbRecordRef value in _recordMap.Values)
			{
				if (value.Record != null && !value.Record.Dead)
				{
					list.Add(value.Record);
				}
			}
			return list.ToArray();
		}

		public IAdbRecord FindRecord(string strguid)
		{
			Guid key = new Guid(strguid);
			if (_recordMap.ContainsKey(key))
			{
				return _recordMap[key].Record;
			}
			return null;
		}

		public void PushHoldNotifies()
		{
			_notifyHold++;
		}

		public void PopHoldNotifies()
		{
			_notifyHold--;
			if (_notifyHold == 0 && _notifiers != null)
			{
				foreach (KeyValuePair<string, AdbNotifyBucket> notifier in _notifiers)
				{
					notifier.Value.SendPending();
				}
				return;
			}
			if (_notifyHold < 0)
			{
				_notifyHold = 0;
			}
		}

		public string NotifyRequest(AdbNotifyTarget notificationTarget)
		{
			AdbNotifyBucket adbNotifyBucket = new AdbNotifyBucket(this, notificationTarget);
			_notifiers.Add(adbNotifyBucket.Key, adbNotifyBucket);
			return adbNotifyBucket.Key;
		}

		public void UnNotifyRequest(string key)
		{
			if (_notifiers.ContainsKey(key))
			{
				_notifiers[key].Dispose();
				_notifiers.Remove(key);
			}
		}

		public bool AddNotify(string key, IAdbRecord record, object notifyTag)
		{
			if (!_notifiers.ContainsKey(key))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return _notifiers[key].AddNotify(record, notifyTag);
		}

		public int Compact()
		{
			List<Guid> list = FindOrphanedRecords();
			foreach (Guid item in list)
			{
				RecordMap.Remove(item, -1L);
			}
			return list.Count;
		}

		public void ResetReachable()
		{
			foreach (KeyValuePair<Guid, AdbRecordRef> item in RecordMap)
			{
				if (item.Value.Record != null)
				{
					item.Value.Record.Reachable = false;
				}
			}
		}

		public void ExemptFlowsheetObjects()
		{
			foreach (KeyValuePair<Guid, AdbRecordRef> item in RecordMap)
			{
				if (item.Value.Record != null)
				{
					AdbBaseRecord record = item.Value.Record;
					if (record.ExemptFromBlockStreamPurge)
					{
						record.Reachable = true;
					}
					else
					{
						record.Reachable = false;
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public List<Guid> FindOrphanedRecords()
		{
			List<Guid> list = new List<Guid>();
			ResetReachable();
			(TopRecord() as AdbBaseRecord).Traverse();
			foreach (KeyValuePair<Guid, AdbRecordRef> item in RecordMap)
			{
				if (item.Value.Record == null || item.Value.Record.Dead || !item.Value.Record.Reachable)
				{
					list.Add(item.Key);
				}
			}
			return list;
		}

		protected IEnumerator<IAdbRecord> GetRecEnum(Guid guid)
		{
			if (!_classTypes.TryGetValue(guid, out var classEntry))
			{
				yield break;
			}
			foreach (KeyValuePair<Guid, AdbRecordRef> item in classEntry.classInfo.classRecordMap)
			{
				yield return item.Value.Record;
			}
			foreach (Guid directChild in classEntry.classInfo.directChildren)
			{
				IEnumerator<IAdbRecord> childEnum = GetRecEnum(directChild);
				while (childEnum.MoveNext())
				{
					yield return childEnum.Current;
				}
			}
		}

		public IEnumerator<IAdbRecord> GetRecordEnumerator(string guidStr)
		{
			IEnumerator<IAdbRecord> classEnum = GetRecEnum(Guid.Parse(guidStr));
			while (classEnum.MoveNext())
			{
				yield return classEnum.Current;
			}
		}

		public void SetUnitConverter(UnitConverter ucvt)
		{
			_unitConverter = ucvt;
			if (!IsBlocked)
			{
				_backlog = new List<DelayedAction>();
			}
		}

		public double ConvertValueToSI(int row, double from, int fromCol)
		{
			double result = from;
			if (_unitConverter != null)
			{
				result = _unitConverter(row, from, fromCol, 1);
			}
			return result;
		}

		public void SaveToDB(string connectionstring, string caseFilePath)
		{
			DateTime utcNow = DateTime.UtcNow;
			using SqlConnection connection = new SqlConnection(connectionstring);
			DateTime utcNow2 = DateTime.UtcNow;
			AdbDataSet.SimCaseInfo simCaseInfo = AdbDataSet.LoadSimCaseInfo(connection, caseFilePath);
			bool isNewSimCase = simCaseInfo == null;
			if (simCaseInfo == null)
			{
				AdbBaseRecord adbCase = AdbDataSet.FindSimCaseRecord(this);
				simCaseInfo = AdbDataSet.CreateSimCaseInfo(connection, caseFilePath, adbCase, NextChangeNumber);
			}
			DateTime utcNow3 = DateTime.UtcNow;
			Dictionary<Guid, List<AdbBaseRecord>> adbRecordsForTypeMap = GetAdbRecordsForTypeMap();
			DateTime utcNow4 = DateTime.UtcNow;
			AdbDataSet.SaveAdbDataToDB(connection, simCaseInfo, this, _classTypes, adbRecordsForTypeMap, DeletedRecords, isNewSimCase);
			if (AdbDataSet.LogPerformance)
			{
				DateTime utcNow5 = DateTime.UtcNow;
				TimeSpan timeSpan = utcNow2.Subtract(utcNow);
				TimeSpan timeSpan2 = utcNow3.Subtract(utcNow2);
				TimeSpan timeSpan3 = utcNow4.Subtract(utcNow3);
				TimeSpan timeSpan4 = utcNow5.Subtract(utcNow4);
				TimeSpan timeSpan5 = utcNow5.Subtract(utcNow);
				StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
				stringBuilder.Append(caseFilePath + "\r\n");
				stringBuilder.Append("Total Time:              " + timeSpan5.Seconds + " s " + timeSpan5.Milliseconds + " ms \r\n");
				stringBuilder.Append("Update DB Tables:        " + timeSpan.Seconds + " sec " + timeSpan.Milliseconds + " ms\r\n");
				stringBuilder.Append("Get/Set SimCaseId:     " + timeSpan2.Seconds + " sec " + timeSpan2.Milliseconds + " ms\r\n");
				stringBuilder.Append("GetAdbRecordsForTypeMap: " + timeSpan3.Seconds + " s " + timeSpan3.Milliseconds + " ms \r\n");
				stringBuilder.Append("Save DataSet to DB:      " + timeSpan4.Seconds + " s " + timeSpan4.Milliseconds + " ms \r\n");
				stringBuilder.Append("Adb records Saved:       " + AdbDataSet.numRecordsSaved + "\r\n");
				stringBuilder.Append("Scalars Saved:           " + AdbDataSet.numScalarsSaved + "\r\n");
				stringBuilder.Append("Array Elements Saved:    " + AdbDataSet.numArrayElementsSaved + "\r\n");
				stringBuilder.Append("Total DB rows Saved:     " + (AdbDataSet.numRecordsSaved + AdbDataSet.numScalarsSaved + AdbDataSet.numArrayElementsSaved) + "\r\n");
				string path = "C:\\Users\\daking\\Desktop\\MDF Database\\SaveToDBTiming.txt";
				string text = ((!File.Exists(path)) ? "" : File.ReadAllText(path));
				File.WriteAllText(path, text + "\r\n" + stringBuilder.ToString());
			}
		}

		public Dictionary<Guid, List<AdbBaseRecord>> GetAdbRecordsForTypeMap()
		{
			Dictionary<Guid, List<AdbBaseRecord>> dictionary = new Dictionary<Guid, List<AdbBaseRecord>>();
			foreach (Guid key in _recordMap.Keys)
			{
				AdbBaseRecord record = _recordMap[key].Record;
				List<AdbBaseRecord> list = null;
				if (!dictionary.ContainsKey(record.ClassGuid))
				{
					list = new List<AdbBaseRecord>();
					dictionary[record.ClassGuid] = list;
				}
				else
				{
					list = dictionary[record.ClassGuid];
				}
				list.Add(record);
			}
			return dictionary;
		}

		public string GetFileName()
		{
			return _filename;
		}

		public bool LoadPsvAccessData(string docname)
		{
			bool result = false;
			string text = Path.ChangeExtension(docname, ".mdb");
			if (!File.Exists(text))
			{
				return false;
			}
			string fileName = GetFileName();
			dynamic val = null;
			try
			{
				val = Activator.CreateInstance(Type.GetTypeFromProgID("PSVLegacyDataRecallServer.Application.V15.0"));
				if (val != null)
				{
					PurgeCache();
					result = val.LoadSafetyCase(text, fileName);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				result = false;
			}
			finally
			{
				if (val != null)
				{
					Marshal.ReleaseComObject(val);
					val = null;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		private static bool ExtractHysysCOM(string val, string outfile)
		{
			_ = new object[1];
			try
			{
				Type typeFromProgID = Type.GetTypeFromProgID("AdbHysysExtractCOM.HysysExtractor");
				object target = Activator.CreateInstance(typeFromProgID);
				object[] args = new object[2] { val, outfile };
				int? num = typeFromProgID.InvokeMember("Extract", BindingFlags.InvokeMethod, null, target, args) as int?;
				return num.HasValue && num != 0;
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				return false;
			}
		}
	}
	public class AdbUnknownUtility
	{
		public static string ParseScalarVariable(XmlReader reader, AdbGuidCache guidCache, bool isRead)
		{
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder.Append("<Value guid=\"");
			string value = (isRead ? guidCache.GetGuidForIndex(int.Parse(reader["guid"])).ToString() : guidCache.GetIndexForGuid(new Guid(reader["guid"])).ToString());
			stringBuilder.Append(value);
			stringBuilder.Append("\"");
			bool num = reader["isguid"] != null;
			if (num)
			{
				stringBuilder.Append(" isguid=\"1\"");
			}
			stringBuilder.Append(">");
			reader.Read();
			string value2 = reader.Value;
			if (num)
			{
				string value3 = (isRead ? guidCache.GetGuidForIndex(int.Parse(value2)).ToString() : guidCache.GetIndexForGuid(new Guid(value2)).ToString());
				stringBuilder.Append(value3);
			}
			else
			{
				stringBuilder.Append(value2);
			}
			stringBuilder.Append("</Value>");
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		public static string ParseArrayVariable(XmlReader reader, AdbGuidCache guidCache, bool isRead)
		{
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			bool flag = reader["idguid"] != null;
			bool flag2 = reader["valguid"] != null;
			stringBuilder.Append("<Array guid=\"");
			string value = (isRead ? guidCache.GetGuidForIndex(int.Parse(reader["guid"])).ToString() : guidCache.GetIndexForGuid(new Guid(reader["guid"])).ToString());
			stringBuilder.Append(value);
			stringBuilder.Append("\"");
			if (flag)
			{
				stringBuilder.Append(" idguid=\"1\"");
			}
			if (flag2)
			{
				stringBuilder.Append(" valguid=\"1\"");
			}
			stringBuilder.Append(">");
			while (reader.Read() && reader.NodeType != XmlNodeType.EndElement)
			{
				if (reader.NodeType != XmlNodeType.Element || !reader.Name.Equals("Element"))
				{
					continue;
				}
				stringBuilder.Append("<Element>");
				reader.Read();
				while (reader.NodeType != XmlNodeType.EndElement)
				{
					if (reader.NodeType == XmlNodeType.Element && reader.Name.Equals("Key"))
					{
						if (!reader.IsEmptyElement)
						{
							reader.Read();
							if (reader.NodeType != XmlNodeType.EndElement)
							{
								stringBuilder.Append("<Key>");
								string text = reader.Value;
								if (flag)
								{
									text = (isRead ? guidCache.GetGuidForIndex(int.Parse(text)).ToString() : guidCache.GetIndexForGuid(new Guid(text)).ToString());
								}
								stringBuilder.Append(text);
								stringBuilder.Append("</Key>");
								reader.Read();
							}
						}
					}
					else if (reader.NodeType == XmlNodeType.Element && reader.Name.Equals("Val") && !reader.IsEmptyElement)
					{
						reader.Read();
						if (reader.NodeType != XmlNodeType.EndElement)
						{
							stringBuilder.Append("<Val>");
							string text2 = reader.Value;
							if (flag2)
							{
								text2 = (isRead ? guidCache.GetGuidForIndex(int.Parse(text2)).ToString() : guidCache.GetIndexForGuid(new Guid(text2)).ToString());
							}
							stringBuilder.Append(text2);
							stringBuilder.Append("</Val>");
							reader.Read();
						}
					}
					reader.Read();
				}
				stringBuilder.Append("</Element>");
			}
			stringBuilder.Append("</Array>");
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}
	}
	public class AdbXmlWriter
	{
		private static Guid PropertyHolderPropArray = new Guid("B604BBC7-C614-4D3B-A770-B8E207D5CD83");

		public void Generate(string[] dllNames, XDocument doc)
		{
			XElement xElement = new XElement("ADSDataModel");
			doc.Add(xElement);
			xElement.Add(new XElement("Date", DateTime.Now.ToString()));
			xElement.Add(new XAttribute("Prefix", "Ads"));
			XElement content = new XElement("Description", "Aspen Data Share data model");
			xElement.Add(content);
			XElement xElement2 = new XElement("Objects");
			xElement.Add(xElement2);
			List<Assembly> list = new List<Assembly>();
			list.Add(Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly());
			for (int i = 0; i < dllNames.Length; i++)
			{
				Assembly assembly = Assembly.LoadFrom(dllNames[i]);
				if (assembly != null)
				{
					list.Add(assembly);
				}
			}
			foreach (Assembly item in list)
			{
				Type[] types = item.GetTypes();
				foreach (Type type in types)
				{
					if (type.Equals(typeof(AdbBaseRecord)) || type.IsSubclassOf(typeof(AdbBaseRecord)))
					{
						GenerateXml(type, xElement2);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		private void AddDatatype(XElement varNode, Type tp)
		{
			if (tp.IsSubclassOf(typeof(AdbBaseRecord)))
			{
				varNode.Add(new XAttribute("datatype", "Reference"));
				object[] customAttributes = tp.GetCustomAttributes(typeof(GuidAttribute), inherit: false);
				if (customAttributes.Length == 1)
				{
					varNode.Add(new XAttribute("targetname", tp.Name));
					varNode.Add(new XAttribute("targetguid", ((GuidAttribute)customAttributes[0]).Value));
				}
			}
			else if (tp.IsGenericType && tp.GetGenericTypeDefinition() == typeof(Nullable<>))
			{
				Type[] genericArguments = tp.GetGenericArguments();
				varNode.Add(new XAttribute("datatype", genericArguments[0].Name + "?"));
			}
			else if (tp.IsGenericType)
			{
				varNode.Add(new XAttribute("datatype", "Array"));
				Type[] genericArguments2 = tp.GetGenericArguments();
				if (!genericArguments2[0].Name.Equals("AdbNoId"))
				{
					XElement xElement = new XElement("Id");
					AddDatatype(xElement, genericArguments2[0]);
					varNode.Add(xElement);
				}
				XElement xElement2 = new XElement("Value");
				AddDatatype(xElement2, genericArguments2[1]);
				varNode.Add(xElement2);
			}
			else
			{
				varNode.Add(new XAttribute("datatype", tp.Name));
			}
		}

		private void GenerateXml(Type T, XElement objectsNode)
		{
			XElement xElement = new XElement("Object");
			objectsNode.Add(xElement);
			xElement.Add(new XAttribute("name", T.Name));
			Type baseType = T.BaseType;
			string text = null;
			string text2 = null;
			if (baseType != null && (baseType.Equals(typeof(AdbBaseRecord)) || baseType.IsSubclassOf(typeof(AdbBaseRecord))))
			{
				object[] customAttributes = baseType.GetCustomAttributes(typeof(GuidAttribute), inherit: false);
				if (customAttributes.Length == 1)
				{
					text = baseType.Name;
					text2 = ((GuidAttribute)customAttributes[0]).Value;
				}
			}
			if (text != null)
			{
				xElement.Add(new XAttribute("parent", text));
			}
			object[] customAttributes2 = T.GetCustomAttributes(typeof(GuidAttribute), inherit: false);
			if (customAttributes2.Length == 1)
			{
				xElement.Add(new XAttribute("guid", ((GuidAttribute)customAttributes2[0]).Value));
			}
			if (text2 != null)
			{
				xElement.Add(new XAttribute("parentguid", text2));
			}
			XElement xElement2 = new XElement("Variables");
			xElement.Add(xElement2);
			FieldInfo[] fields = T.GetFields(BindingFlags.DeclaredOnly | BindingFlags.Instance | BindingFlags.Public);
			foreach (FieldInfo fieldInfo in fields)
			{
				object[] customAttributes3 = fieldInfo.GetCustomAttributes(typeof(AdbFieldIdent), inherit: false);
				if (customAttributes3.Length == 1)
				{
					AdbFieldIdent adbFieldIdent = (AdbFieldIdent)customAttributes3[0];
					if (!adbFieldIdent.Id.Equals(PropertyHolderPropArray))
					{
						XElement xElement3 = new XElement("Variable", new XAttribute("name", fieldInfo.Name), new XAttribute("guid", adbFieldIdent.Id.ToString()));
						AddDatatype(xElement3, fieldInfo.FieldType);
						xElement2.Add(xElement3);
					}
				}
			}
		}
	}
	public interface IAdbBaseObject
	{
		object Value(object[] id, int currentIndex);

		void SetValue(object[] id, int currentIndex, object newValue);

		void RestoreValue(object[] id, int currentIndex, object newValue);

		object ExecuteMethod(object[] id, int currentIndex, object[] arguments);

		void SetValue(XmlReader reader, AdbGuidCache guidCache);

		string OutputString(AdbGuidCache guidCache);

		void Traverse();

		bool DeleteAllOutput(int mask = 3);
	}
	public interface IAdbTopRecordInfo
	{
		AdbBaseRecord CreateTopRecord(AdbTop top);

		Guid TopRecordGuid();
	}
