		public static void Test(AdbTop adbTop, Dictionary<Guid, List<AdbBaseRecord>> adbRecordsForTypeMap, Dictionary<Guid, AdbTop.typeTable> classTypes)
		{
			foreach (Guid key in adbRecordsForTypeMap.Keys)
			{
				List<AdbBaseRecord> list = adbRecordsForTypeMap[key];
				string name = list[0].GetType().Name;
				AdbClassInfo classInfo = classTypes[key].classInfo;
				foreach (AdbBaseRecord item in list.OrderBy((AdbBaseRecord t) => t.MyGuid))
				{
					name.Equals("AdbAhHeatExchanger");
					foreach (Guid key2 in classInfo.fields.Keys)
					{
						FieldInfo f = classInfo.fields[key2].F;
						if (f.FieldType.IsGenericType && f.FieldType.GetGenericTypeDefinition() != typeof(Nullable<>))
						{
							continue;
						}
						if (f.FieldType.IsGenericType)
						{
							_ = f.FieldType.GetGenericArguments()[0].Name;
						}
						else if (f.FieldType.IsSubclassOf(typeof(AdbBaseRecord)))
						{
							_ = (AdbBaseRecord)f.GetValue(item);
						}
						object value = f.GetValue(item);
						if (value == null || f.FieldType.IsSubclassOf(typeof(AdbBaseRecord)))
						{
							continue;
						}
						if (value.GetType().Name.Equals("Int32"))
						{
							if ((int)value != 0)
							{
								item.SetScalarValue(key2.ToString(), (int)value + 1);
							}
						}
						else if (value.GetType().Name.Equals("Double"))
						{
							if ((double)value != 0.0)
							{
								item.SetScalarValue(key2.ToString(), (double)value + 1.0);
							}
						}
						else if (value.GetType().Name.Equals("String"))
						{
							if (!((string)value).Equals(""))
							{
								item.SetScalarValue(key2.ToString(), "Foobar");
							}
						}
						else if (value.GetType().Name.Equals("Boolean"))
						{
							item.SetScalarValue(key2.ToString(), !(bool)value);
						}
					}
				}
				foreach (AdbBaseRecord item2 in adbRecordsForTypeMap[key].OrderBy((AdbBaseRecord p) => p.MyGuid))
				{
					foreach (Guid item3 in classInfo.fields.Keys.OrderBy((Guid p) => p.ToString()))
					{
						AdbFieldAttribute adbFieldAttribute = classInfo.fields[item3];
						FieldInfo f2 = adbFieldAttribute.F;
						_ = f2.Name;
						if (!f2.FieldType.IsGenericType || !(f2.FieldType.GetGenericTypeDefinition() != typeof(Nullable<>)))
						{
							continue;
						}
						Type[] genericArguments = f2.FieldType.GetGenericArguments();
						bool flag = !genericArguments[0].Name.Equals("AdbNoId");
						f2.FieldType.GetMethod("RemoveAtLocation");
						MethodInfo method = f2.FieldType.GetMethod("Append", new Type[1] { typeof(object) });
						MethodInfo methodInfo = null;
						MethodInfo[] methods = f2.FieldType.GetMethods();
						for (int num = 0; num < methods.Count(); num++)
						{
							MethodInfo methodInfo2 = methods[num];
							if (methodInfo2.Name.Equals("Append") && methodInfo2.GetParameters().Length == 2)
							{
								methodInfo = methods[num];
								break;
							}
						}
						object value2 = f2.GetValue(item2);
						if (value2 == null)
						{
							continue;
						}
						Type type = value2.GetType();
						getArrayTableName(key, adbFieldAttribute.Attribute.Id);
						if ((int)type.GetProperty("Length").GetValue(value2) <= 0)
						{
							continue;
						}
						MethodInfo method2 = type.GetMethod("ValueAtLocation");
						MethodInfo methodInfo3 = (flag ? type.GetMethod("IDAtLocation") : null);
						object obj = method2.Invoke(value2, new object[1] { 0 });
						object obj2 = (flag ? methodInfo3.Invoke(value2, new object[1] { 0 }) : null);
						object obj3 = null;
						object obj4 = null;
						if (genericArguments[1].Name.Equals("String"))
						{
							obj3 = (string)obj + "MOD1";
							obj4 = (string)obj + "MOD2";
						}
						else if (genericArguments[1].Name.Equals("Int32"))
						{
							obj3 = (int)obj + 1;
							obj4 = (int)obj + 2;
						}
						else if (genericArguments[1].Name.Equals("Double"))
						{
							obj3 = (double)obj + 1.0;
							obj4 = (double)obj + 2.0;
						}
						else
						{
							obj3 = obj;
							obj4 = obj;
						}
						if (flag)
						{
							if (!genericArguments[0].IsSubclassOf(typeof(AdbBaseRecord)))
							{
								object obj5 = null;
								object obj6 = null;
								if (genericArguments[0].Name.Equals("String"))
								{
									obj5 = (string)obj2 + "MOD1";
									obj6 = (string)obj2 + "MOD2";
								}
								else if (genericArguments[0].Name.Equals("Int32"))
								{
									obj5 = (int)obj2 + 1;
									obj6 = (int)obj2 + 2;
								}
								else if (genericArguments[0].Name.Equals("Double"))
								{
									obj5 = (double)obj2 + 1.0;
									obj6 = (double)obj2 + 2.0;
								}
								methodInfo.Invoke(value2, new object[2] { obj5, obj3 });
								methodInfo.Invoke(value2, new object[2] { obj6, obj4 });
							}
						}
						else
						{
							method.Invoke(value2, new object[1] { obj3 });
							method.Invoke(value2, new object[1] { obj4 });
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

		public static void CreateDataBaseTables(string connectionString)
		{
			using SqlConnection sqlConnection = new SqlConnection(connectionString);
			sqlConnection.Open();
			new SqlCommand("CREATE TABLE Version (Version varchar(30) NOT NULL)", sqlConnection).ExecuteNonQuery();
			string assemblyFileVersion = GetAssemblyFileVersion();
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder.Append("INSERT INTO Version (Version) VALUES ('").Append(assemblyFileVersion).Append("')");
			new SqlCommand(stringBuilder.ToString(), sqlConnection).ExecuteNonQuery();
			new SqlCommand("CREATE TABLE SimCase (Id int NOT NULL, CaseFilePath NVARCHAR(MAX) NOT NULL, CaseGuid char(36) NOT NULL, CaseDBGuid char(36) NOT NULL, NextChangeNumber bigint NOT NULL, PRIMARY KEY (CaseGuid))", sqlConnection).ExecuteNonQuery();
			StringBuilder stringBuilder2 = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder2.Append("CREATE TABLE \"AdbRecords\" (Id int NOT NULL, Guid char(36) NOT NULL, ClassGuid char(36) NOT NULL, CaseId int NOT NULL, ");
			stringBuilder2.Append("LastChangeNumber bigint NULL, PRIMARY KEY (Id))");
			new SqlCommand(stringBuilder2.ToString(), sqlConnection).ExecuteNonQuery();
			StringBuilder stringBuilder3 = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder3.Append("CREATE TABLE \"ScalarFields\" (RecordId int NOT NULL, CaseId int NOT NULL, FieldGuid char(36) NOT NULL, ");
			stringBuilder3.Append("IntegerValue int NULL, DoubleValue float NULL, StringValue nvarchar(max) NULL");
			stringBuilder3.Append(",PRIMARY KEY (RecordId, FieldGuid))");
			new SqlCommand(stringBuilder3.ToString(), sqlConnection).ExecuteNonQuery();
			StringBuilder stringBuilder4 = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder4.Append("CREATE TABLE \"ArrayValues\" (RecordId int NOT NULL, CaseId int NOT NULL, FieldGuid char(36) NOT NULL, ");
			stringBuilder4.Append("Idx int NOT NULL, IntegerId int NULL, StringId nvarchar(max) NULL, ");
			stringBuilder4.Append("IntegerValue int NULL, DoubleValue float NULL, StringValue nvarchar(max) NULL");
			stringBuilder4.Append(",PRIMARY KEY (RecordId, FieldGuid, Idx))");
			new SqlCommand(stringBuilder4.ToString(), sqlConnection).ExecuteNonQuery();
			StringBuilder stringBuilder5 = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder5.Append("exec('CREATE PROCEDURE MergeRecordData AS ");
			stringBuilder5.Append("MERGE INTO AdbRecords as Target ");
			stringBuilder5.Append("USING #AdbRecords AS Source ");
			stringBuilder5.Append("ON Target.Id = Source.Id ");
			stringBuilder5.Append("WHEN MATCHED ");
			stringBuilder5.Append("THEN UPDATE SET Target.LastChangeNumber = Source.LastChangeNumber ");
			stringBuilder5.Append("WHEN NOT MATCHED BY TARGET THEN INSERT (Id, Guid, ClassGuid, CaseId) ");
			stringBuilder5.Append("VALUES (Source.Id, Source.Guid, Source.ClassGuid, Source.CaseId) ");
			stringBuilder5.Append("; ");
			stringBuilder5.Append("MERGE INTO ScalarFields as Target ");
			stringBuilder5.Append("USING #ScalarFields AS Source ");
			stringBuilder5.Append("ON Target.RecordId = Source.RecordId AND Target.FieldGuid = Source.FieldGuid ");
			stringBuilder5.Append("WHEN MATCHED ");
			stringBuilder5.Append("THEN ");
			stringBuilder5.Append("UPDATE SET Target.IntegerValue = Source.IntegerValue, Target.DoubleValue = Source.DoubleValue, Target.StringValue = Source.StringValue ");
			stringBuilder5.Append("WHEN NOT MATCHED BY TARGET THEN INSERT (RecordId, CaseId, FieldGuid, IntegerValue, DoubleValue, StringValue) ");
			stringBuilder5.Append("VALUES (Source.RecordId, Source.CaseId, Source.FieldGuid, Source.IntegerValue, Source.DoubleValue, Source.StringValue) ");
			stringBuilder5.Append("; ");
			stringBuilder5.Append("MERGE INTO ArrayValues as Target ");
			stringBuilder5.Append("USING #ArrayValues AS Source ");
			stringBuilder5.Append("ON Target.RecordId = Source.RecordId AND Target.FieldGuid = Source.FieldGuid AND Target.Idx = Source.Idx ");
			stringBuilder5.Append("WHEN MATCHED ");
			stringBuilder5.Append("THEN ");
			stringBuilder5.Append("UPDATE SET Target.IntegerId = Source.IntegerId, Target.StringId = Source.StringId, Target.IntegerValue = Source.IntegerValue, Target.DoubleValue = Source.DoubleValue, Target.StringValue = Source.StringValue ");
			stringBuilder5.Append("WHEN NOT MATCHED BY TARGET THEN INSERT (RecordId, CaseId, FieldGuid, Idx, IntegerId, StringId, IntegerValue, DoubleValue, StringValue) ");
			stringBuilder5.Append("VALUES (Source.RecordId, Source.CaseId, Source.FieldGuid, Source.Idx, Source.IntegerId, Source.StringId, Source.IntegerValue, Source.DoubleValue, Source.StringValue) ");
			stringBuilder5.Append("; ");
			stringBuilder5.Append("DELETE FROM AdbRecords WHERE Id IN(SELECT Id FROM #AdbRecordsToDelete) ");
			stringBuilder5.Append("DELETE FROM ScalarFields WHERE RecordId IN(SELECT Id FROM #AdbRecordsToDelete) ");
			stringBuilder5.Append("DELETE FROM ArrayValues WHERE RecordId IN(SELECT Id FROM #AdbRecordsToDelete) ");
			stringBuilder5.Append("')");
			SqlCommand sqlCommand = new SqlCommand(stringBuilder5.ToString(), sqlConnection);
			sqlCommand.CommandType = CommandType.Text;
			sqlCommand.ExecuteNonQuery();
			sqlConnection.Close();
		}

		public static void DeleteDataBaseTables(string connectionString)
		{
			string cmdText = "SELECT * FROM sysobjects WHERE xtype = 'U'";
			try
			{
				using SqlConnection sqlConnection = new SqlConnection(connectionString);
				sqlConnection.Open();
				SqlCommand sqlCommand = new SqlCommand(cmdText, sqlConnection);
				List<string> list = new List<string>();
				SqlDataReader sqlDataReader = sqlCommand.ExecuteReader();
				if (sqlDataReader.HasRows)
				{
					while (sqlDataReader.Read())
					{
						string item = sqlDataReader.GetString(0);
						list.Add(item);
					}
				}
				sqlDataReader.Close();
				foreach (string item2 in list)
				{
					new SqlCommand("DROP TABLE [" + item2 + "]", sqlConnection).ExecuteNonQuery();
				}
				StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
				stringBuilder.Append("if exists ( select * from sys.objects ");
				stringBuilder.Append("  where name='MergeRecordData' and objectproperty(object_id,'IsProcedure')=1 ) ");
				stringBuilder.Append("  DROP PROCEDURE MergeRecordData;");
				SqlCommand sqlCommand2 = new SqlCommand(stringBuilder.ToString(), sqlConnection);
				sqlCommand2.CommandType = CommandType.Text;
				sqlCommand2.ExecuteNonQuery();
			}
			catch (IOException)
			{
			}
		}

		public static string GetAssemblyFileVersion()
		{
			return ((AssemblyFileVersionAttribute)Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().GetCustomAttribute(typeof(AssemblyFileVersionAttribute))).Version;
		}

		public static int ParseVersion(string version)
		{
			int num = version.IndexOf('.');
			int num2 = version.IndexOf('.', num + 1);
			int num3 = version.IndexOf('.', num2 + 1);
			int num4 = ushort.Parse(version.Substring(0, num));
			int num5 = ushort.Parse(version.Substring(num + 1, num2 - num - 1));
			int num6 = ushort.Parse(version.Substring(num2 + 1, num3 - num2 - 1));
			int num7 = ushort.Parse(version.Substring(num3 + 1));
			return 10000000 * num4 + 1000000 * num5 * 100000 * num6 + num7;
		}

		public static void SaveVersion(string version, SqlConnection connection)
		{
			connection.Open();
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder.Append("UPDATE Version SET Version = '").Append(version).Append("'");
			new SqlCommand(stringBuilder.ToString(), connection).ExecuteNonQuery();
			connection.Close();
		}

		public static string LoadVersion(SqlConnection connection)
		{
			connection.Open();
			string result = (string)new SqlCommand("SELECT Version FROM Version", connection).ExecuteScalar();
			connection.Close();
			return result;
		}

		public static FieldInfo GetCaseFieldInfo(AdbBaseRecord topRecord)
		{
			FieldInfo result = null;
			foreach (AdbFieldAttribute field in topRecord.ClassInfo.fieldList)
			{
				FieldInfo f = field.F;
				if (f.Name.Equals("Case"))
				{
					result = f;
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static AdbBaseRecord FindSimCaseRecord(AdbTop adbTop)
		{
			AdbBaseRecord adbBaseRecord = (AdbBaseRecord)adbTop.TopRecord();
			AdbBaseRecord adbBaseRecord2 = (AdbBaseRecord)GetCaseFieldInfo(adbBaseRecord).GetValue(adbBaseRecord);
			if (adbBaseRecord2 == null)
			{
				adbBaseRecord2 = adbTop.CreateRecord(AdbCaseGuid, Guid.NewGuid());
			}
			PropertyInfo property = adbBaseRecord2.GetType().GetProperty("DBGuid");
			if ((string)property.GetValue(adbBaseRecord2) == null)
			{
				property.SetValue(adbBaseRecord2, Guid.NewGuid().ToString());
			}
			return adbBaseRecord2;
		}

		public static string GetCaseDBGuid(AdbBaseRecord adbCase)
		{
			PropertyInfo property = adbCase.GetType().GetProperty("DBGuid");
			string text = (string)property.GetValue(adbCase);
			if (text == null)
			{
				text = Guid.NewGuid().ToString();
				property.SetValue(adbCase, text);
			}
			return text;
		}

		public static void SetCaseDBGuid(AdbBaseRecord adbCase, string dbGuid)
		{
			adbCase.GetType().GetProperty("DBGuid").SetValue(adbCase, dbGuid);
		}

		public static int GetSimCaseId(string caseFilePath, SqlConnection connection)
		{
			connection.Open();
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder.Append("SELECT ID FROM SimCase WHERE CaseFilePath = '").Append(caseFilePath).Append("'");
			int result = (int)new SqlCommand(stringBuilder.ToString(), connection).ExecuteScalar();
			connection.Close();
			return result;
		}

		public static SimCaseInfo LoadSimCaseInfo(SqlConnection connection, string caseFilePath)
		{
			SimCaseInfo simCaseInfo = null;
			connection.Open();
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder.Append("SELECT Id, CaseGuid, CaseDBGuid, NextChangeNumber FROM SimCase WHERE CaseFilePath = '").Append(caseFilePath).Append("'");
			SqlDataReader sqlDataReader = new SqlCommand(stringBuilder.ToString(), connection).ExecuteReader();
			if (sqlDataReader.Read())
			{
				int @int = sqlDataReader.GetInt32(0);
				string g = sqlDataReader.GetString(1);
				string g2 = sqlDataReader.GetString(2);
				long int2 = sqlDataReader.GetInt64(3);
				simCaseInfo = new SimCaseInfo();
				simCaseInfo.caseId = @int;
				simCaseInfo.caseFilePath = caseFilePath;
				simCaseInfo.caseGuid = new Guid(g);
				simCaseInfo.caseDBGuid = new Guid(g2);
				simCaseInfo.nextChangeNumber = int2;
			}
			connection.Close();
			return simCaseInfo;
		}

		public static void SaveAdbDataToDB(SqlConnection connection, SimCaseInfo caseInfo, AdbTop adbTop, Dictionary<Guid, AdbTop.typeTable> classTypes, Dictionary<Guid, List<AdbBaseRecord>> adbRecordsForTypeMap, List<KeyValuePair<long, Guid>> deletedRecords, bool isNewSimCase)
		{
			numRecordsSaved = 0;
			numScalarsSaved = 0;
			numArrayElementsSaved = 0;
			HashSet<Guid> hashSet = new HashSet<Guid>();
			if (isNewSimCase || isDataFromDB)
			{
				recordGuidToId = new Dictionary<Guid, int>();
			}
			string destinationTableName = (isNewSimCase ? "AdbRecords" : "#AdbRecords");
			string destinationTableName2 = (isNewSimCase ? "ScalarFields" : "#ScalarFields");
			string destinationTableName3 = (isNewSimCase ? "ArrayValues" : "#ArrayValues");
			connection.Open();
			try
			{
				if (isNewSimCase)
				{
					recordGuidToLastChangeNumber = new Dictionary<Guid, long>();
					foreach (List<AdbBaseRecord> value in adbRecordsForTypeMap.Values)
					{
						foreach (AdbBaseRecord item in value)
						{
							recordGuidToLastChangeNumber[item.MyGuid] = item.LastChangeNumber;
						}
					}
				}
				else
				{
					foreach (KeyValuePair<long, Guid> deletedRecord in deletedRecords)
					{
						hashSet.Add(deletedRecord.Value);
					}
					Dictionary<Guid, List<AdbBaseRecord>> dictionary = new Dictionary<Guid, List<AdbBaseRecord>>();
					foreach (Guid key in adbRecordsForTypeMap.Keys)
					{
						foreach (AdbBaseRecord item2 in adbRecordsForTypeMap[key])
						{
							if ((!recordGuidToLastChangeNumber.ContainsKey(item2.MyGuid) || (recordGuidToLastChangeNumber.ContainsKey(item2.MyGuid) && item2.LastChangeNumber > recordGuidToLastChangeNumber[item2.MyGuid])) && !hashSet.Contains(item2.MyGuid))
							{
								if (!dictionary.ContainsKey(key))
								{
									dictionary[key] = new List<AdbBaseRecord>();
								}
								dictionary[key].Add(item2);
								recordGuidToLastChangeNumber[item2.MyGuid] = item2.LastChangeNumber;
							}
						}
					}
					adbRecordsForTypeMap = dictionary;
					CreateTemporaryTables(connection, hashSet.Count > 0);
				}
				if (adbRecordsForTypeMap.Count > 0)
				{
					int numExistingRecords = (int)new SqlCommand("SELECT count(*) FROM AdbRecords", connection).ExecuteScalar() - 1;
					AdbInstanceDataReader adbInstanceDataReader = new AdbInstanceDataReader(adbRecordsForTypeMap, caseInfo, recordGuidToId, numExistingRecords);
					using (SqlBulkCopy sqlBulkCopy = new SqlBulkCopy(connection))
					{
						numRecordsSaved = adbInstanceDataReader.count;
						sqlBulkCopy.BatchSize = adbInstanceDataReader.count;
						sqlBulkCopy.DestinationTableName = destinationTableName;
						sqlBulkCopy.WriteToServer(adbInstanceDataReader);
					}
					AdbFieldValueReader adbFieldValueReader = new AdbFieldValueReader(adbTop, adbRecordsForTypeMap, classTypes, caseInfo, recordGuidToId);
					using (SqlBulkCopy sqlBulkCopy2 = new SqlBulkCopy(connection))
					{
						numScalarsSaved = adbFieldValueReader.count;
						sqlBulkCopy2.BatchSize = adbFieldValueReader.count;
						sqlBulkCopy2.DestinationTableName = destinationTableName2;
						sqlBulkCopy2.WriteToServer(adbFieldValueReader);
					}
					AdbArrayValueReader adbArrayValueReader = new AdbArrayValueReader(adbTop, adbRecordsForTypeMap, classTypes, caseInfo, recordGuidToId);
					using (SqlBulkCopy sqlBulkCopy3 = new SqlBulkCopy(connection))
					{
						numArrayElementsSaved = adbArrayValueReader.count;
						sqlBulkCopy3.BatchSize = adbArrayValueReader.count;
						sqlBulkCopy3.DestinationTableName = destinationTableName3;
						sqlBulkCopy3.WriteToServer(adbArrayValueReader);
					}
					if (!isNewSimCase)
					{
						if (hashSet.Count > 0)
						{
							using SqlBulkCopy sqlBulkCopy4 = new SqlBulkCopy(connection);
							DeletedRecordDataReader deletedRecordDataReader = new DeletedRecordDataReader(caseInfo, recordGuidToId, hashSet);
							sqlBulkCopy4.BatchSize = deletedRecordDataReader.count;
							sqlBulkCopy4.DestinationTableName = "#AdbRecordsToDelete";
							sqlBulkCopy4.WriteToServer(deletedRecordDataReader);
						}
						SqlCommand sqlCommand = new SqlCommand("MergeRecordData");
						sqlCommand.Connection = connection;
						sqlCommand.CommandType = CommandType.StoredProcedure;
						sqlCommand.ExecuteNonQuery();
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			connection.Close();
			isDataFromDB = false;
		}

		public static void CreateTemporaryTables(SqlConnection connection, bool createDeletedRecordsTable)
		{
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder.Append("CREATE TABLE \"#AdbRecords\" (Id int NOT NULL, Guid char(36) NOT NULL, ClassGuid char(36) NOT NULL, CaseId int NOT NULL, ");
			stringBuilder.Append("LastChangeNumber bigint NULL, PRIMARY KEY (Id))");
			new SqlCommand(stringBuilder.ToString(), connection).ExecuteNonQuery();
			StringBuilder stringBuilder2 = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder2.Append("CREATE TABLE \"#ScalarFields\" (RecordId int NOT NULL, CaseId int NOT NULL, FieldGuid char(36) NOT NULL, ");
			stringBuilder2.Append("IntegerValue int NULL, DoubleValue float NULL, StringValue nvarchar(max) NULL");
			stringBuilder2.Append(",PRIMARY KEY (RecordId, FieldGuid))");
			new SqlCommand(stringBuilder2.ToString(), connection).ExecuteNonQuery();
			StringBuilder stringBuilder3 = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder3.Append("CREATE TABLE \"#ArrayValues\" (RecordId int NOT NULL, CaseId int NOT NULL, FieldGuid char(36) NOT NULL, ");
			stringBuilder3.Append("Idx int NOT NULL, IntegerId int NULL, StringId nvarchar(max) NULL, ");
			stringBuilder3.Append("IntegerValue int NULL, DoubleValue float NULL, StringValue nvarchar(max) NULL");
			stringBuilder3.Append(",PRIMARY KEY (RecordId, FieldGuid, Idx))");
			new SqlCommand(stringBuilder3.ToString(), connection).ExecuteNonQuery();
			if (createDeletedRecordsTable)
			{
				StringBuilder stringBuilder4 = new StringBuilder();
				stringBuilder4.Append("CREATE TABLE \"#AdbRecordsToDelete\" (Id int NOT NULL");
				stringBuilder4.Append(")");
				new SqlCommand(stringBuilder4.ToString(), connection).ExecuteNonQuery();
			}
		}

		public static void LoadAdbDataFromDB(string connectionString, int caseId, AdbTop adbTop, Dictionary<Guid, AdbTop.typeTable> classTypes)
		{
			numRecordsLoaded = 0;
			numScalarsLoaded = 0;
			numArrayElementsLoaded = 0;
			AdbGuidCache adbGuidCache = new AdbGuidCache();
			adbTop.Fixups = new AdbReferenceFixups(adbTop);
			adbGuidCache.Fixups = adbTop.Fixups;
			Dictionary<int, string> dictionary = new Dictionary<int, string>();
			Dictionary<string, AdbBaseRecord> dictionary2 = new Dictionary<string, AdbBaseRecord>();
			string cmdText = "SELECT * FROM AdbRecords WHERE CaseId=" + caseId;
			using (SqlConnection sqlConnection = new SqlConnection(connectionString))
			{
				sqlConnection.Open();
				SqlDataReader sqlDataReader = new SqlCommand(cmdText, sqlConnection).ExecuteReader();
				if (sqlDataReader.HasRows)
				{
					while (sqlDataReader.Read())
					{
						int @int = sqlDataReader.GetInt32(0);
						string text = sqlDataReader.GetString(1);
						string text2 = sqlDataReader.GetString(2);
						AdbBaseRecord topRecord = (dictionary2[text] = adbTop.CreateRecord(new Guid(text2), new Guid(text)));
						dictionary[@int] = text;
						if (text2.Equals(AdbTopRecordGuid.ToString()))
						{
							adbTop._topRecord = topRecord;
						}
						numRecordsLoaded++;
					}
				}
				sqlDataReader.Close();
				sqlDataReader = new SqlCommand("SELECT * FROM ScalarFields WHERE CaseId=" + caseId, sqlConnection).ExecuteReader();
				if (sqlDataReader.HasRows)
				{
					while (sqlDataReader.Read())
					{
						numScalarsLoaded++;
						int int2 = sqlDataReader.GetInt32(0);
						string g = sqlDataReader.GetString(2);
						string key = dictionary[int2];
						AdbBaseRecord record = dictionary2[key];
						AdbFieldAttribute adbFieldAttribute = record.ClassInfo.fields[new Guid(g)];
						FieldInfo f = adbFieldAttribute.F;
						if (f.FieldType.IsGenericType)
						{
							Type[] genericArguments = f.FieldType.GetGenericArguments();
							if (genericArguments[0].Name.Equals("Boolean"))
							{
								bool flag = sqlDataReader.GetInt32(3) == 1;
								f.SetValue(record, flag);
							}
							else if (genericArguments[0].Name.Equals("Int32"))
							{
								f.SetValue(record, sqlDataReader.GetInt32(3));
							}
							else if (genericArguments[0].Name.Equals("Double"))
							{
								f.SetValue(record, sqlDataReader.GetDouble(4));
							}
						}
						else if (f.FieldType.Name.Equals("Boolean"))
						{
							bool flag2 = sqlDataReader.GetInt32(3) == 1;
							f.SetValue(record, flag2);
						}
						else if (f.FieldType.Name.Equals("Int32"))
						{
							f.SetValue(record, sqlDataReader.GetInt32(3));
						}
						else if (f.FieldType.IsSubclassOf(typeof(AdbBaseRecord)) && !sqlDataReader.IsDBNull(3))
						{
							int int3 = sqlDataReader.GetInt32(3);
							if (dictionary.ContainsKey(int3))
							{
								Guid reference = new Guid(dictionary[int3]);
								adbGuidCache.Fixups.AddReferenceFixup(reference, delegate(object o)
								{
									f.SetValue(record, o);
								}, null);
							}
						}
						else if (f.FieldType.Name.Equals("Double"))
						{
							f.SetValue(record, sqlDataReader.GetDouble(4));
						}
						else if (!sqlDataReader.IsDBNull(5))
						{
							f.SetValue(record, sqlDataReader.GetString(5));
						}
					}
				}
				sqlDataReader.Close();
				Dictionary<object, int> dictionary3 = new Dictionary<object, int>();
				sqlDataReader = new SqlCommand("SELECT * FROM ArrayValues WHERE CaseId=" + caseId, sqlConnection).ExecuteReader();
				if (sqlDataReader.HasRows)
				{
					while (sqlDataReader.Read())
					{
						numArrayElementsLoaded++;
						int int4 = sqlDataReader.GetInt32(0);
						string g2 = sqlDataReader.GetString(2);
						int idx = sqlDataReader.GetInt32(3);
						string key2 = dictionary[int4];
						AdbBaseRecord adbBaseRecord2 = dictionary2[key2];
						FieldInfo f2 = adbBaseRecord2.ClassInfo.fields[new Guid(g2)].F;
						Type fieldType = f2.FieldType;
						Type[] genericArguments2 = f2.FieldType.GetGenericArguments();
						bool flag3 = !genericArguments2[0].Name.Equals("AdbNoId");
						MethodInfo setValueAtLocationMethod = fieldType.GetMethod("SetValueAtLocation");
						MethodInfo setIdMethod = (flag3 ? fieldType.GetMethod("SetID") : null);
						MethodInfo appendMethod1 = fieldType.GetMethod("Append", new Type[1] { typeof(object) });
						MethodInfo appendMethod2 = null;
						MethodInfo[] methods = fieldType.GetMethods();
						for (int num = 0; num < methods.Count(); num++)
						{
							MethodInfo methodInfo = methods[num];
							if (methodInfo.Name.Equals("Append") && methodInfo.GetParameters().Length == 2)
							{
								appendMethod2 = methods[num];
								break;
							}
						}
						object array = f2.GetValue(adbBaseRecord2);
						if (array == null)
						{
							ConstructorInfo constructor = fieldType.GetConstructor(new Type[2]
							{
								typeof(AdbTop),
								adbBaseRecord2.GetType()
							});
							array = constructor.Invoke(new object[2] { adbTop, adbBaseRecord2 });
							f2.SetValue(adbBaseRecord2, array);
						}
						PropertyInfo property = array.GetType().GetProperty("Length");
						int num2 = (int)property.GetValue(array);
						object val = null;
						if (!sqlDataReader.IsDBNull(6))
						{
							val = sqlDataReader.GetInt32(6);
						}
						else if (!sqlDataReader.IsDBNull(7))
						{
							val = sqlDataReader.GetDouble(7);
						}
						else if (!sqlDataReader.IsDBNull(8))
						{
							val = sqlDataReader.GetString(8);
						}
						if (flag3)
						{
							object id = null;
							if (!sqlDataReader.IsDBNull(4))
							{
								id = sqlDataReader.GetInt32(4);
							}
							else if (!sqlDataReader.IsDBNull(5))
							{
								id = sqlDataReader.GetString(5);
							}
							if (num2 <= idx)
							{
								if (genericArguments2[0].IsSubclassOf(typeof(AdbBaseRecord)))
								{
									if (genericArguments2[1].IsSubclassOf(typeof(AdbBaseRecord)))
									{
										Guid reference2 = new Guid(dictionary[(int)id]);
										Guid reference3 = new Guid(dictionary[(int)val]);
										adbGuidCache.Fixups.AddReferenceFixup(reference3, delegate(object o)
										{
											appendMethod1.Invoke(array, new object[1] { o });
										}, null);
										adbGuidCache.Fixups.AddReferenceFixup(reference2, delegate(object o)
										{
											setIdMethod.Invoke(array, new object[2] { idx, o });
										}, null);
									}
									else
									{
										Guid reference4 = new Guid(dictionary[(int)id]);
										adbGuidCache.Fixups.AddReferenceFixup(reference4, delegate(object o)
										{
											appendMethod2.Invoke(array, new object[2] { o, val });
										}, null);
									}
								}
								else if (genericArguments2[1].IsSubclassOf(typeof(AdbBaseRecord)) && val != null)
								{
									Guid reference5 = new Guid(dictionary[(int)val]);
									adbGuidCache.Fixups.AddReferenceFixup(reference5, delegate(object o)
									{
										appendMethod2.Invoke(array, new object[2] { id, o });
									}, null);
								}
								else
								{
									appendMethod2.Invoke(array, new object[2] { id, val });
								}
								continue;
							}
							if (genericArguments2[0].IsSubclassOf(typeof(AdbBaseRecord)) && id != null)
							{
								Guid reference6 = new Guid(dictionary[(int)id]);
								adbGuidCache.Fixups.AddReferenceFixup(reference6, delegate(object o)
								{
									setIdMethod.Invoke(array, new object[2] { idx, o });
								}, null);
							}
							else
							{
								setIdMethod.Invoke(array, new object[2] { idx, id });
							}
							if (genericArguments2[1].IsSubclassOf(typeof(AdbBaseRecord)) && val != null)
							{
								Guid reference7 = new Guid(dictionary[(int)val]);
								adbGuidCache.Fixups.AddReferenceFixup(reference7, delegate(object o)
								{
									setValueAtLocationMethod.Invoke(array, new object[2] { idx, o });
								}, null);
							}
							else
							{
								setValueAtLocationMethod.Invoke(array, new object[2] { idx, val });
							}
							int val2 = (dictionary3.ContainsKey(array) ? ((int)property.GetValue(array)) : (-1));
							dictionary3[array] = Math.Max(val2, idx);
						}
						else if (genericArguments2[1].IsSubclassOf(typeof(AdbBaseRecord)) && val != null)
						{
							Guid reference8 = new Guid(dictionary[(int)val]);
							if (num2 <= idx)
							{
								adbGuidCache.Fixups.AddReferenceFixup(reference8, delegate(object o)
								{
									appendMethod1.Invoke(array, new object[1] { o });
								}, null);
								continue;
							}
							adbGuidCache.Fixups.AddReferenceFixup(reference8, delegate(object o)
							{
								setValueAtLocationMethod.Invoke(array, new object[2] { idx, o });
							}, null);
							int val3 = (dictionary3.ContainsKey(array) ? ((int)property.GetValue(array)) : (-1));
							dictionary3[array] = Math.Max(val3, idx);
						}
						else if (num2 <= idx)
						{
							appendMethod1.Invoke(array, new object[1] { val });
						}
						else
						{
							setValueAtLocationMethod.Invoke(array, new object[2] { idx, val });
							int val4 = (dictionary3.ContainsKey(array) ? ((int)property.GetValue(array)) : (-1));
							dictionary3[array] = Math.Max(val4, idx);
						}
					}
				}
				sqlDataReader.Close();
			}
			adbTop.Fixups.DoFixups(removeUntouched: true);
			isDataFromDB = true;
		}

		public static void SetArrayFieldLengthForRecord(Dictionary<string, Dictionary<string, int>> arrayFieldLengthMap, string arrayTableName, string guid, int len)
		{
			Dictionary<string, int> dictionary = null;
			dictionary = (arrayFieldLengthMap.ContainsKey(arrayTableName) ? arrayFieldLengthMap[arrayTableName] : (arrayFieldLengthMap[arrayTableName] = new Dictionary<string, int>()));
			dictionary[guid] = len;
		}

		public static int GetArrayFieldLengthForRecord(Dictionary<string, Dictionary<string, int>> arrayFieldLengthMap, string arrayTableName, string guid)
		{
			int result = -1;
			if (arrayFieldLengthMap.ContainsKey(arrayTableName))
			{
				Dictionary<string, int> dictionary = arrayFieldLengthMap[arrayTableName];
				if (dictionary.ContainsKey(guid))
				{
					result = dictionary[guid];
				}
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static SimCaseInfo CreateSimCaseInfo(SqlConnection connection, string caseFilePath, AdbBaseRecord adbCase, long nextChangeNumber)
		{
			connection.Open();
			int caseId = (int)new SqlCommand("SELECT count(*) FROM SimCase", connection).ExecuteScalar() + 1;
			_ = (string)adbCase.GetType().GetProperty("DBGuid").GetValue(adbCase);
			SimCaseInfo simCaseInfo = new SimCaseInfo();
			simCaseInfo.caseFilePath = caseFilePath;
			simCaseInfo.caseId = caseId;
			simCaseInfo.caseGuid = adbCase.MyGuid;
			simCaseInfo.caseDBGuid = Guid.NewGuid();
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder.Append("INSERT INTO SimCase (Id, CaseFilePath, CaseGuid, CaseDBGuid, NextChangeNumber) ");
			stringBuilder.Append("VALUES (").Append(simCaseInfo.caseId.ToString()).Append(",'")
				.Append(simCaseInfo.caseFilePath)
				.Append("','")
				.Append(simCaseInfo.caseGuid)
				.Append("','");
			stringBuilder.Append(simCaseInfo.caseDBGuid).Append("',").Append(simCaseInfo.nextChangeNumber)
				.Append(")");
			new SqlCommand(stringBuilder.ToString(), connection).ExecuteNonQuery();
			connection.Close();
			return simCaseInfo;
		}

		private static string getArrayTableName(Guid classGuid, Guid fieldGuid)
		{
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			stringBuilder.Append(classGuid.ToString()).Append("@").Append(fieldGuid.ToString());
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		private static string getSqlDataType(string csType)
		{
			string text = null;
			if (csType.Equals("Boolean"))
			{
				return "int";
			}
			if (csType.Equals("Double"))
			{
				return "float";
			}
			if (csType.Equals("Int32"))
			{
				return "int";
			}
			if (csType.Equals("String"))
			{
				return "nvarchar(max)";
			}
			return "char(36)";
		}

		private static Type GetDataColumnDataType(string adbType)
		{
			Type type = null;
			if (adbType.Equals("Double"))
			{
				return typeof(double);
			}
			if (adbType.Equals("Boolean"))
			{
				return typeof(int);
			}
			if (adbType.Equals("Int32"))
			{
				return typeof(int);
			}
			return null;
		}

		public static void PrintAdbClasses(string caseFilePath, string directory, Dictionary<Guid, AdbTop.typeTable> classTypes, Dictionary<Guid, List<AdbBaseRecord>> adbRecordsForTypeMap)
		{
			if (!Directory.Exists(directory))
			{
				Directory.CreateDirectory(directory);
			}
			if (caseFilePath.Contains("."))
			{
				caseFilePath = caseFilePath.Substring(0, caseFilePath.IndexOf("."));
			}
			string text = directory + "\\" + caseFilePath + "\\";
			if (!Directory.Exists(text))
			{
				Directory.CreateDirectory(text);
			}
			foreach (Guid key in adbRecordsForTypeMap.Keys)
			{
				List<AdbBaseRecord> list = adbRecordsForTypeMap[key];
				string name = list[0].GetType().Name;
				StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
				int num = 0;
				AdbClassInfo classInfo = classTypes[key].classInfo;
				foreach (AdbBaseRecord item in list.OrderBy((AdbBaseRecord t) => t.MyGuid))
				{
					StringBuilder stringBuilder2 = stringBuilder;
					int num2 = ++num;
					stringBuilder2.Append("\r\n" + name + num2 + "\r\n");
					stringBuilder.Append("Guid " + item.MyGuid.ToString() + "\r\n");
					stringBuilder.Append("LastChangeNumber " + item.LastChangeNumber + "\r\n");
					foreach (AdbFieldAttribute item2 in classInfo.fields.Values.OrderBy((AdbFieldAttribute p) => p.F.Name))
					{
						FieldInfo f = item2.F;
						if (!f.FieldType.IsGenericType || !(f.FieldType.GetGenericTypeDefinition() != typeof(Nullable<>)))
						{
							stringBuilder.Append(f.Name + " ");
							if (f.FieldType.IsGenericType)
							{
								string name2 = f.FieldType.GetGenericArguments()[0].Name;
								stringBuilder.Append(name2);
							}
							else if (f.FieldType.IsSubclassOf(typeof(AdbBaseRecord)))
							{
								_ = (AdbBaseRecord)f.GetValue(item);
								stringBuilder.Append("char(36)");
							}
							else
							{
								string name3 = f.FieldType.Name;
								stringBuilder.Append(name3);
							}
							object value = f.GetValue(item);
							if (value == null)
							{
								stringBuilder.Append(" null");
							}
							else if (f.FieldType.IsSubclassOf(typeof(AdbBaseRecord)))
							{
								stringBuilder.Append(" " + ((AdbBaseRecord)value).MyGuid.ToString());
							}
							else if (f.FieldType.Equals(typeof(AdbIOReal)))
							{
								AdbIOReal adbIOReal = (AdbIOReal)value;
								stringBuilder.Append("\r\n  Calculated: " + (double)adbIOReal.Value(new object[1] { "Calculated" }, 0) + "\r\n");
								stringBuilder.Append("  Input: " + (double)adbIOReal.Value(new object[1] { "Input" }, 0));
							}
							else
							{
								stringBuilder.Append(" " + value.ToString());
							}
							stringBuilder.Append("\r\n");
						}
					}
				}
				if (stringBuilder.Length > 0)
				{
					File.WriteAllText(text + name + ".txt", stringBuilder.ToString());
				}
				stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
				num = 1;
				foreach (AdbBaseRecord item3 in adbRecordsForTypeMap[key].OrderBy((AdbBaseRecord p) => p.MyGuid))
				{
					stringBuilder.Append("\r\n" + name + num + "\r\n");
					foreach (Guid item4 in classInfo.fields.Keys.OrderBy((Guid p) => p.ToString()))
					{
						AdbFieldAttribute adbFieldAttribute = classInfo.fields[item4];
						FieldInfo f2 = adbFieldAttribute.F;
						string name4 = f2.Name;
						if (!f2.FieldType.IsGenericType || !(f2.FieldType.GetGenericTypeDefinition() != typeof(Nullable<>)))
						{
							continue;
						}
						Type[] genericArguments = f2.FieldType.GetGenericArguments();
						bool flag = !genericArguments[0].Name.Equals("AdbNoId");
						object value2 = f2.GetValue(item3);
						if (value2 == null)
						{
							continue;
						}
						Type type = value2.GetType();
						getArrayTableName(key, adbFieldAttribute.Attribute.Id);
						int num3 = (int)type.GetProperty("Length").GetValue(value2);
						if (num3 <= 0)
						{
							continue;
						}
						stringBuilder.Append(name4 + "\r\n");
						if (stringBuilder.Length <= 0)
						{
							continue;
						}
						MethodInfo method = type.GetMethod("ValueAtLocation");
						MethodInfo methodInfo = (flag ? type.GetMethod("IDAtLocation") : null);
						for (int num4 = 0; num4 < num3; num4++)
						{
							object obj = method.Invoke(value2, new object[1] { num4 });
							if (genericArguments[1].IsSubclassOf(typeof(AdbBaseRecord)))
							{
								obj = ((AdbBaseRecord)obj).MyGuid;
							}
							if (flag)
							{
								object obj2 = methodInfo.Invoke(value2, new object[1] { num4 });
								if (genericArguments[0].IsSubclassOf(typeof(AdbBaseRecord)))
								{
									obj2 = ((AdbBaseRecord)obj2).MyGuid;
								}
								stringBuilder.Append(num4 + "  Id: " + obj2?.ToString() + " Value: " + obj?.ToString() + "\r\n");
							}
							else
							{
								stringBuilder.Append(num4 + "  Value: " + obj?.ToString() + "\r\n");
							}
						}
					}
					num++;
				}
				if (stringBuilder.Length > 0)
				{
					File.WriteAllText(text + name + "-Arrays.txt", stringBuilder.ToString());
				}
			}
		}

		public static void PrintDBables(string connectionString, string caseId, string caseName, AdbTop adbTop, Dictionary<Guid, List<AdbBaseRecord>> adbRecordsForTypeMap)
		{
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			Dictionary<string, string> recordGuidToAdbClass = new Dictionary<string, string>();
			Dictionary<string, int> dictionary = new Dictionary<string, int>();
			Dictionary<string, string> dictionary2 = new Dictionary<string, string>();
			string cmdText = "SELECT * FROM AdbRecords WHERE CaseId=" + caseId;
			using SqlConnection sqlConnection = new SqlConnection(connectionString);
			sqlConnection.Open();
			SqlDataReader sqlDataReader = new SqlCommand(cmdText, sqlConnection).ExecuteReader();
			if (sqlDataReader.HasRows)
			{
				while (sqlDataReader.Read())
				{
					int @int = sqlDataReader.GetInt32(0);
					string text = sqlDataReader.GetString(1);
					string guid = sqlDataReader.GetString(2);
					Type typeForGuid = adbTop.GetTypeForGuid(guid);
					list.Add(text);
					dictionary[text] = @int;
					recordGuidToAdbClass[text] = typeForGuid.Name;
				}
			}
			sqlDataReader.Close();
			list.Sort((string o1, string o2) => recordGuidToAdbClass[o1].CompareTo(recordGuidToAdbClass[o2]));
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			foreach (string item in list)
			{
				Dictionary<Guid, AdbFieldAttribute>.Enumerator enumerator2 = ((AdbBaseRecord)adbTop.FindRecord(item)).ClassInfo.fields.GetEnumerator();
				while (enumerator2.MoveNext())
				{
					KeyValuePair<Guid, AdbFieldAttribute> current2 = enumerator2.Current;
					dictionary2[current2.Key.ToString()] = current2.Value.F.Name;
				}
				string text2 = recordGuidToAdbClass[item];
				int num = dictionary[item];
				stringBuilder.Append(text2 + " " + num + "\r\n");
				sqlDataReader = new SqlCommand("SELECT * FROM ScalarFields WHERE CaseId='" + caseId + "' AND RecordId = '" + num + "'", sqlConnection).ExecuteReader();
				if (sqlDataReader.HasRows)
				{
					while (sqlDataReader.Read())
					{
						string key = sqlDataReader.GetString(2);
						string text3 = dictionary2[key];
						for (int num2 = 3; num2 < 6; num2++)
						{
							string text4 = null;
							if (!sqlDataReader.IsDBNull(num2))
							{
								text4 = num2 switch
								{
									3 => sqlDataReader.GetInt32(num2).ToString(), 
									4 => sqlDataReader.GetDouble(num2).ToString(), 
									_ => sqlDataReader.GetString(num2), 
								};
								stringBuilder.Append("  " + text3 + ": " + text4 + "\r\n");
								break;
							}
						}
					}
				}
				sqlDataReader.Close();
			}
			File.WriteAllText("C:\\Users\\daking\\Desktop\\MDF Database\\" + caseName + "-Scalars.txt", stringBuilder.ToString());
			stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			foreach (string item2 in list)
			{
				Dictionary<Guid, AdbFieldAttribute>.Enumerator enumerator3 = ((AdbBaseRecord)adbTop.FindRecord(item2)).ClassInfo.fields.GetEnumerator();
				while (enumerator3.MoveNext())
				{
					KeyValuePair<Guid, AdbFieldAttribute> current4 = enumerator3.Current;
					dictionary2[current4.Key.ToString()] = current4.Value.F.Name;
				}
				int num3 = dictionary[item2];
				string text5 = recordGuidToAdbClass[item2];
				stringBuilder.Append(text5 + " " + num3 + "\r\n");
				sqlDataReader = new SqlCommand("SELECT * FROM ArrayValues WHERE CaseId='" + caseId + "' AND RecordId = " + num3, sqlConnection).ExecuteReader();
				if (sqlDataReader.HasRows)
				{
					while (sqlDataReader.Read())
					{
						string key2 = sqlDataReader.GetString(2);
						string text6 = dictionary2[key2];
						int int2 = sqlDataReader.GetInt32(3);
						string text7 = null;
						string text8 = null;
						for (int num4 = 4; num4 < 9; num4++)
						{
							if (!sqlDataReader.IsDBNull(num4))
							{
								switch (num4)
								{
								case 4:
									text7 = sqlDataReader.GetInt32(num4).ToString();
									break;
								case 5:
									text7 = sqlDataReader.GetString(num4);
									break;
								case 6:
									text8 = sqlDataReader.GetInt32(num4).ToString();
									break;
								case 7:
									text8 = sqlDataReader.GetDouble(num4).ToString();
									break;
								case 8:
									text8 = sqlDataReader.GetString(num4);
									break;
								}
							}
						}
						if (text7 != null)
						{
							stringBuilder.Append("  " + text6 + "[" + int2 + "]: " + text7 + " --> " + text8 + "\r\n");
						}
						else
						{
							stringBuilder.Append("  " + text6 + "[" + int2 + "]: " + text8 + "\r\n");
						}
					}
				}
				sqlDataReader.Close();
			}
			File.WriteAllText("C:\\Users\\daking\\Desktop\\MDF Database\\" + caseName + "-Arrays.txt", stringBuilder.ToString());
		}

		public static List<string> ListSimCases(string connectionString)
		{
			List<string> list = new List<string>();
			using (SqlConnection sqlConnection = new SqlConnection(connectionString))
			{
				StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
				stringBuilder.Append("SELECT * FROM ");
				stringBuilder.Append("SimCase");
				sqlConnection.Open();
				SqlDataReader sqlDataReader = new SqlCommand(stringBuilder.ToString(), sqlConnection).ExecuteReader();
				while (sqlDataReader.Read())
				{
					string item = sqlDataReader.GetString(1);
					list.Add(item);
				}
			}
			foreach (string item2 in list)
			{
				Console.WriteLine(item2);
			}
			return list;
		}

		public static void DeleteCaseFromDB(SqlConnection connection, string caseFilePath)
		{
			SimCaseInfo simCaseInfo = LoadSimCaseInfo(connection, caseFilePath);
			if (simCaseInfo == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			string text = simCaseInfo.caseGuid.ToString();
			string cmdText = "SELECT * FROM sysobjects WHERE xtype = 'U'";
			try
			{
				connection.Open();
				SqlCommand sqlCommand = new SqlCommand(cmdText, connection);
				List<string> list = new List<string>();
				SqlDataReader sqlDataReader = sqlCommand.ExecuteReader();
				if (sqlDataReader.HasRows)
				{
					while (sqlDataReader.Read())
					{
						string item = sqlDataReader.GetString(0);
						list.Add(item);
					}
				}
				sqlDataReader.Close();
				foreach (string item2 in list)
				{
					if (!item2.Equals("Version"))
					{
						new SqlCommand("DELETE FROM \"" + item2 + "\" WHERE CaseGuid='" + text + "'", connection).ExecuteNonQuery();
					}
				}
				new SqlCommand("DELETE FROM SimCase WHERE CaseGuid ='" + text + "'", connection).ExecuteNonQuery();
				connection.Close();
			}
			catch (IOException)
			{
			}
		}
	}
}
